=== FILE: D:\DO AN 2 CHUYEN NGANH\cau-hinh\ket-noi-db.js ===
const sql = require('mssql');
require('dotenv').config();

const config = {
  server: process.env.DB_SERVER || 'localhost',
  database: process.env.DB_DATABASE || process.env.DB_NAME || 'LuaVangDB',
  user: process.env.DB_USER,
  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD,
  options: {
    encrypt: process.env.DB_ENCRYPT !== 'false',
    trustServerCertificate: process.env.DB_TRUST_SERVER_CERTIFICATE !== 'false',
    enableArithAbort: true,
    useUTC: false
  }
};

let poolPromise = null;

async function ketNoiDb() {
  if (!poolPromise) {
    const pool = new sql.ConnectionPool(config);
    poolPromise = pool.connect().catch((error) => {
      poolPromise = null;
      throw error;
    });
  }

  return poolPromise;
}

async function dongDb() {
  if (poolPromise) {
    const pool = await poolPromise;
    await pool.close();
    poolPromise = null;
  }
}

module.exports = { sql, ketNoiDb, dongDb };

const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

// Xá»­ lÃ½ ÄÄƒng kÃ½ tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n
const dangKy = async (req, res) => {
    const { fullname, phone, address, password } = req.body;

    if (!fullname || !phone || !password) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘iá»n Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ cÃ¡c thÃ´ng tin báº¯t buá»™c!' });
    }

    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();

        // Kiá»ƒm tra trÃ¹ng bÃªn báº£ng Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn vÃ  NÃ´ng dÃ¢n
        const [checkAdmin, checkPhone] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request()
                .input('phone', sql.VarChar, phone)
                .query(`
                    IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.QuanTriVien', 'U') IS NOT NULL
                        SELECT MaAdmin FROM QuanTriVien WHERE TenDangNhap = @phone
                    ELSE
                        SELECT 1 AS Empty WHERE 1 = 0
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('phone', sql.VarChar, phone)
                .query('SELECT MaNongDan FROM NongDan WHERE SoDienThoai = @phone')
        ]);

        if (checkAdmin.recordset.length > 0 || checkPhone.recordset.length > 0) {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tÃ i khoáº£n trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³!' });
        }

        await pool.request()
            .input('fullname', sql.NVarChar, fullname)
            .input('phone', sql.VarChar, phone)
            .input('address', sql.NVarChar, address || '')
            .input('password', sql.NVarChar, password)
            .query(`
                INSERT INTO NongDan (HoTen, SoDienThoai, DiaChi, MatKhau)
                VALUES (@fullname, @phone, @address, @password)
            `);

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½ tÃ i khoáº£n thÃ nh cÃ´ng vÃ  Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u vÃ o CSDL!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i khi lÆ°u CSDL:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i há»‡ thá»‘ng server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// Xá»­ lÃ½ ÄÄƒng nháº­p (TÃ¡ch riÃªng báº£ng QuanTriVien vÃ  NongDan)
const dangNhap = async (req, res) => {
    const { username, password } = req.body;

    if (!username || !password) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n vÃ  máº­t kháº©u!' });
    }

    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();

        // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p bÃªn báº£ng QuanTriVien trÆ°á»›c
        const adminRes = await pool.request()
            .input('username', sql.VarChar, username)
            .input('password', sql.NVarChar, password)
            .query(`
                IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.QuanTriVien', 'U') IS NOT NULL
                    SELECT MaAdmin, TenDangNhap, HoTen, VaiTro
                    FROM QuanTriVien
                    WHERE TenDangNhap = @username AND MatKhau = @password
                ELSE
                    SELECT 1 AS Empty WHERE 1 = 0
            `);

        if (adminRes.recordset.length > 0) {
            const admin = adminRes.recordset[0];
            return res.json({
                success: true,
                message: 'ÄÄƒng nháº­p Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn thÃ nh cÃ´ng!',
                user: {
                    id: admin.MaAdmin,
                    name: admin.HoTen,
                    phone: admin.TenDangNhap,
                    address: 'Há»‡ thá»‘ng LÃºa VÃ ng',
                    vaiTro: admin.VaiTro,
                    role: 'admin'
                }
            });
        }

        // 2. Náº¿u khÃ´ng pháº£i Admin, kiá»ƒm tra bÃªn báº£ng NongDan
        const farmerRes = await pool.request()
            .input('phone', sql.VarChar, username)
            .input('password', sql.NVarChar, password)
            .query(`
                SELECT MaNongDan, HoTen, SoDienThoai, ISNULL(DiaChi, N'') AS DiaChi
                FROM NongDan
                WHERE SoDienThoai = @phone AND MatKhau = @password
            `);

        if (farmerRes.recordset.length === 0) {
            return res.status(401).json({ success: false, message: 'Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c!' });
        }

        const user = farmerRes.recordset[0];
        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: 'ÄÄƒng nháº­p thÃ nh cÃ´ng!',
            user: {
                id: user.MaNongDan,
                name: user.HoTen,
                phone: user.SoDienThoai,
                address: user.DiaChi,
                diaChi: user.DiaChi,
                role: 'nong-dan'
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i khi Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i há»‡ thá»‘ng server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

module.exports = { dangKy, dangNhap };

const { ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

// 1. LÆ°u thÃ´ng tin nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tham gia sá»± kiá»‡n
const dangKySuKien = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { tieuDeSuKien, hoTen, soDienThoai } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('tieuDeSuKien', tieuDeSuKien)
            .input('hoTen', hoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n')
            .input('soDienThoai', soDienThoai || 'ChÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t')
            .query(`INSERT INTO DangKySuKien (TieuDeSuKien, HoTen, SoDienThoai) 
                    VALUES (@tieuDeSuKien, @hoTen, @soDienThoai)`);
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½ tham gia thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 2. Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘áº¡i biá»ƒu Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ cho Admin xem
const layDanhSachDangKy = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        let result = await pool.request().query('SELECT * FROM DangKySuKien ORDER BY MaDK DESC');
        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 3. XÃ³a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n ra khá»i danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ sá»± kiá»‡n theo MaDK
const xoaDangKySuKien = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('id', id)
            .query('DELETE FROM DangKySuKien WHERE MaDK = @id');
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'XÃ³a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n khá»i sá»± kiá»‡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

=== FILE: D:\DO AN 2 CHUYEN NGANH\dieu-khien\gioHangController.js ===
const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');
const { taoLinkVietQR } = require('./sepayController');
const sepayConfig = require('../cau-hinh/sepay');

const THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY = 600; // 10 phÃºt = 600 giÃ¢y

// ================= HÃ€M Há»– TRá»¢ KIá»‚M TRA Lá»ŠCH THUÃŠ MÃY =================
function kiemTraTrungKhungGio(gioA, gioB) {
    if (!gioA || !gioB) return false;
    if (gioA === gioB) return true;
    if (gioA.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y') || gioB.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y')) return true;
    return false;
}

function anSoDienThoai(sdt) {
    const s = String(sdt || '').trim();
    if (s.length < 7) return s;
    return s.slice(0, 4) + '***' + s.slice(-3);
}

function rutGonTen(hoTen) {
    const parts = String(hoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n').trim().split(/\s+/);
    if (parts.length <= 1) return parts[0];
    const tenCuoi = parts.pop();
    return parts.join(' ') + ' ' + tenCuoi[0] + '.';
}

async function damBaoCotThanhToan(pool) {
    await pool.request().query(`
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
    `);
}

function taoMaThanhToan() {
    const prefix = (sepayConfig.CONTENT_PREFIX || 'SEVQR').trim();
    return `${prefix} LV${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`;
}

// ================= Tá»° Äá»˜NG TRáº¢ ÄÆ N QUÃ Háº N 10 PHÃšT Vá»€ GIá»Ž HÃ€NG =================
async function donDepDonQuaHan10Phut(poolParam = null) {
    try {
        const pool = poolParam || await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);

        // 1. Tráº£ cÃ¡c Ä‘Æ¡n Sáº£n pháº©m 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' quÃ¡ 10 phÃºt vá» láº¡i 'Trong giá»'
        const spQuaHan = await pool.request()
            .input('hanGiay', sql.Int, THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY)
            .query(`
                SELECT MaGH, SoDienThoai, MaSanPham, SoLuong
                FROM GioHangSanPham
                WHERE TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n'
                  AND NgayThem IS NOT NULL
                  AND DATEDIFF(SECOND, NgayThem, GETDATE()) >= @hanGiay
            `);

        for (const item of spQuaHan.recordset) {
            const existRes = await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, item.SoDienThoai)
                .input('maSP', sql.Int, item.MaSanPham)
                .input('id', sql.Int, item.MaGH)
                .query(`
                    SELECT MaGH FROM GioHangSanPham
                    WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND MaSanPham = @maSP
                      AND TrangThai = N'Trong giá»'
                      AND MaGH <> @id
                `);

            if (existRes.recordset.length > 0) {
                await pool.request()
                    .input('targetId', sql.Int, existRes.recordset[0].MaGH)
                    .input('sl', sql.Int, item.SoLuong)
                    .query('UPDATE GioHangSanPham SET SoLuong = SoLuong + @sl WHERE MaGH = @targetId');

                await pool.request()
                    .input('id', sql.Int, item.MaGH)
                    .query('DELETE FROM GioHangSanPham WHERE MaGH = @id');
            } else {
                await pool.request()
                    .input('id', sql.Int, item.MaGH)
                    .query(`
                        UPDATE GioHangSanPham
                        SET TrangThai = N'Trong giá»',
                            MaThanhToan = NULL,
                            TienCoc = NULL,
                            NgayThem = GETDATE()
                        WHERE MaGH = @id
                    `);
            }
        }

        // 2. Tráº£ cÃ¡c lá»‹ch ThuÃª mÃ¡y 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' quÃ¡ 10 phÃºt vá» 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n' -> Má»Ÿ láº¡i khung giá» trá»‘ng
        const mayQuaHan = await pool.request()
            .input('hanGiay', sql.Int, THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY)
            .query(`
                UPDATE GioHangThueMay
                SET TrangThai = N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n',
                    MaThanhToan = NULL,
                    TienCoc = NULL,
                    NgayTao = GETDATE()
                OUTPUT INSERTED.MaDatMay
                WHERE TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n'
                  AND NgayTao IS NOT NULL
                  AND DATEDIFF(SECOND, NgayTao, GETDATE()) >= @hanGiay
            `);

        if (spQuaHan.recordset.length > 0 || mayQuaHan.recordset.length > 0) {
            console.log(`â±ï¸ [QuÃ¡ háº¡n 10 phÃºt] ÄÃ£ tráº£ ${spQuaHan.recordset.length} Ä‘Æ¡n SP & ${mayQuaHan.recordset.length} lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y vá» giá» hÃ ng.`);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tráº£ Ä‘Æ¡n quÃ¡ háº¡n 10 phÃºt:', err.message);
    }
}

async function kiemTraMayDaBiDat(pool, maMay, ngayThue, khungGio, boQuaMaDatMay = null) {
    if (!ngayThue || !khungGio) return null;

    // Dá»n dáº¹p ngay cÃ¡c Ä‘Æ¡n quÃ¡ háº¡n 10 phÃºt trÆ°á»›c khi kiá»ƒm tra trÃ¹ng ca
    await donDepDonQuaHan10Phut(pool);

    const req = pool.request()
        .input('maMay', sql.Int, parseInt(maMay))
        .input('ngayThue', sql.NVarChar, String(ngayThue).trim());

    let query = `
        SELECT MaDatMay, KhungGio, TrangThai
        FROM GioHangThueMay
        WHERE MaMay = @maMay
          AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), NgayThue, 23) = @ngayThue
          AND TrangThai IN (
              N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n',
              N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)',
              N'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch',
              N'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n',
              N'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh'
          )
    `;

    if (boQuaMaDatMay) {
        req.input('boQuaId', sql.Int, parseInt(boQuaMaDatMay));
        query += ` AND MaDatMay <> @boQuaId`;
    }

    const result = await req.query(query);
    return result.recordset.find(row => kiemTraTrungKhungGio(row.KhungGio, khungGio)) || null;
}

// 1. Láº¥y toÃ n bá»™ 3 má»¥c (Sáº£n pháº©m, ThuÃª mÃ¡y, Sá»± kiá»‡n) cá»§a 1 tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n theo SÄT
const layGioHang = async (req, res) => {
    const { sdt } = req.params;
    if (!sdt) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i tÃ i khoáº£n!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await donDepDonQuaHan10Phut(pool);

        const [resSanPham, resThueMay, resSuKien] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .input('hanGiay', sql.Int, THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY)
                .query(`
                    SELECT gh.MaGH, gh.SoLuong, gh.TrangThai,
                           gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                           CONVERT(varchar(19), gh.NgayThem, 126) AS NgayThem,
                           CASE
                               WHEN gh.TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' AND gh.NgayThem IS NOT NULL
                               THEN IIF(@hanGiay - DATEDIFF(SECOND, gh.NgayThem, GETDATE()) > 0,
                                        @hanGiay - DATEDIFF(SECOND, gh.NgayThem, GETDATE()), 0)
                               ELSE 0
                           END AS GiayConLai,
                           sp.MaSanPham, sp.TenSanPham, sp.DanhMuc, sp.GiaTien, sp.DonViTinh, sp.HinhAnh
                    FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                    INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                    WHERE gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt
                    ORDER BY gh.MaGH DESC
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .input('hanGiay', sql.Int, THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY)
                .query(`
                    SELECT tm_gh.MaDatMay, tm_gh.SoCong,
                           CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), tm_gh.NgayThue, 23) AS NgayThue,
                           tm_gh.KhungGio, tm_gh.GhiChu, tm_gh.TrangThai,
                           tm_gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(tm_gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                           CONVERT(varchar(19), tm_gh.NgayTao, 126) AS NgayTao,
                           CASE
                               WHEN tm_gh.TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' AND tm_gh.NgayTao IS NOT NULL
                               THEN IIF(@hanGiay - DATEDIFF(SECOND, tm_gh.NgayTao, GETDATE()) > 0,
                                        @hanGiay - DATEDIFF(SECOND, tm_gh.NgayTao, GETDATE()), 0)
                               ELSE 0
                           END AS GiayConLai,
                           tm.MaMay, tm.TenMay, tm.LoaiMay, tm.GiaThue, tm.DonViTinh, tm.HinhAnh
                    FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                    INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                    WHERE tm_gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt
                    ORDER BY tm_gh.MaDatMay DESC
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query(`
                    SELECT dk.MaDK, dk.TieuDeSuKien, dk.HoTen, dk.SoDienThoai,
                           CONVERT(varchar(19), dk.NgayDangKy, 126) AS NgayDangKy,
                           sk.ThoiGian, sk.DiaDiem, sk.MoTa
                    FROM DangKySuKien dk
                    LEFT JOIN SuKien sk ON LTRIM(RTRIM(dk.TieuDeSuKien)) = LTRIM(RTRIM(sk.TieuDe))
                    WHERE dk.SoDienThoai = @sdt
                    ORDER BY dk.MaDK DESC
                `)
        ]);

        const tongCocSP = {};
        const tongConLaiSP = {};
        resSanPham.recordset.forEach(item => {
            if (item.MaThanhToan && item.TrangThai !== 'ÄÃ£ há»§y') {
                const thanhTien = Number(item.GiaTien || 0) * Number(item.SoLuong || 0);
                const tienCoc = Number(item.TienCoc || 0);
                const conLai = Math.max(0, thanhTien - tienCoc);

                tongCocSP[item.MaThanhToan] = (tongCocSP[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) + tienCoc;
                tongConLaiSP[item.MaThanhToan] = (tongConLaiSP[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) + conLai;
            }
        });

        const dsSanPham = resSanPham.recordset.map(item => {
            const tongCocDon = item.MaThanhToan ? (tongCocSP[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) : 0;
            const tongConLaiDon = item.MaThanhToan ? (tongConLaiSP[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) : 0;
            const maThanhToanCOD = item.MaThanhToan ? `${item.MaThanhToan} COD` : '';

            return {
                ...item,
                TongCocDon: tongCocDon,
                TongConLaiDon: tongConLaiDon,
                MaThanhToanCOD: maThanhToanCOD,
                QrUrl: (item.MaThanhToan && tongCocDon > 0) ? taoLinkVietQR(tongCocDon, item.MaThanhToan) : '',
                QrUrlCOD: (maThanhToanCOD && tongConLaiDon > 0) ? taoLinkVietQR(tongConLaiDon, maThanhToanCOD) : ''
            };
        });

        const tongCocMay = {};
        const tongConLaiMay = {};
        resThueMay.recordset.forEach(item => {
            if (item.MaThanhToan && item.TrangThai !== 'ÄÃ£ há»§y') {
                const thanhTien = Number(item.GiaThue || 0) * Number(item.SoCong || 0);
                const tienCoc = Number(item.TienCoc || 0);
                const conLai = Math.max(0, thanhTien - tienCoc);

                tongCocMay[item.MaThanhToan] = (tongCocMay[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) + tienCoc;
                tongConLaiMay[item.MaThanhToan] = (tongConLaiMay[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) + conLai;
            }
        });

        const dsThueMay = resThueMay.recordset.map(item => {
            const tongCocDon = item.MaThanhToan ? (tongCocMay[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) : 0;
            const tongConLaiDon = item.MaThanhToan ? (tongConLaiMay[item.MaThanhToan] || 0) : 0;
            const maThanhToanCOD = item.MaThanhToan ? `${item.MaThanhToan} COD` : '';

            return {
                ...item,
                TongCocDon: tongCocDon,
                TongConLaiDon: tongConLaiDon,
                MaThanhToanCOD: maThanhToanCOD,
                QrUrl: (item.MaThanhToan && tongCocDon > 0) ? taoLinkVietQR(tongCocDon, item.MaThanhToan) : '',
                QrUrlCOD: (maThanhToanCOD && tongConLaiDon > 0) ? taoLinkVietQR(tongConLaiDon, maThanhToanCOD) : ''
            };
        });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            sanPham: dsSanPham,
            thueMay: dsThueMay,
            suKien: resSuKien.recordset,
            bankInfo: {
                bankName: 'VietinBank',
                accountNo: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO,
                accountName: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y giá» hÃ ng:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 2. ThÃªm sáº£n pháº©m vÃ o giá» hÃ ng HOáº¶C táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30% ngay qua SePay
const themSanPhamVaoGio = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai, maSanPham, soLuong, trangThai } = req.body;
    if (!soDienThoai || !maSanPham) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u thÃ´ng tin tÃ i khoáº£n hoáº·c sáº£n pháº©m!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);
        const slThem = Math.max(1, parseInt(soLuong) || 1);

        if (trangThai === 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' || trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng') {
            const spRes = await pool.request()
                .input('maSP', sql.Int, maSanPham)
                .query('SELECT TenSanPham, GiaTien FROM SanPham WHERE MaSanPham = @maSP');

            if (spRes.recordset.length === 0) {
                return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y sáº£n pháº©m!' });
            }

            const giaTien = Number(spRes.recordset[0].GiaTien || 0);
            const tongTien = giaTien * slThem;
            const tienCoc = Math.round(tongTien * 0.3);
            const maTT = taoMaThanhToan();

            await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
                .input('maSP', sql.Int, maSanPham)
                .input('sl', sql.Int, slThem)
                .input('mtt', sql.VarChar, maTT)
                .input('tienCoc', sql.Decimal(18, 0), tienCoc)
                .query(`
                    INSERT INTO GioHangSanPham (SoDienThoai, MaSanPham, SoLuong, TrangThai, MaThanhToan, TienCoc, NgayThem)
                    VALUES (@sdt, @maSP, @sl, N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', @mtt, @tienCoc, GETDATE())
                `);

            return res.json({
                success: true,
                message: 'ÄÃ£ táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30%! Vui lÃ²ng thanh toÃ¡n trong 10 phÃºt.',
                maThanhToan: maTT,
                tongTien,
                tienCoc,
                giayConLai: THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY,
                qrUrl: taoLinkVietQR(tienCoc, maTT),
                bankInfo: {
                    bankName: 'VietinBank',
                    accountNo: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO,
                    accountName: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME
                }
            });
        }

        const check = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .input('maSP', sql.Int, maSanPham)
            .query(`
                SELECT MaGH FROM GioHangSanPham 
                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt AND MaSanPham = @maSP AND TrangThai = N'Trong giá»'
            `);

        if (check.recordset.length > 0) {
            await pool.request()
                .input('maGH', sql.Int, check.recordset[0].MaGH)
                .input('sl', sql.Int, slThem)
                .query('UPDATE GioHangSanPham SET SoLuong = SoLuong + @sl WHERE MaGH = @maGH');
        } else {
            await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
                .input('maSP', sql.Int, maSanPham)
                .input('sl', sql.Int, slThem)
                .query(`
                    INSERT INTO GioHangSanPham (SoDienThoai, MaSanPham, SoLuong, TrangThai, NgayThem)
                    VALUES (@sdt, @maSP, @sl, N'Trong giá»', GETDATE())
                `);
        }

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ thÃªm sáº£n pháº©m vÃ o giá» hÃ ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i thÃªm sáº£n pháº©m vÃ o giá»:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 3. Cáº­p nháº­t sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng sáº£n pháº©m trong giá»
const capNhatSanPhamGio = async (req, res) => {
    const { maGH, soLuong } = req.body;
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        if (parseInt(soLuong) <= 0) {
            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                .query('DELETE FROM GioHangSanPham WHERE MaGH = @id');
        } else {
            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                .input('sl', sql.Int, parseInt(soLuong))
                .query('UPDATE GioHangSanPham SET SoLuong = @sl WHERE MaGH = @id');
        }
        res.json({ success: true });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 4. XÃ³a sáº£n pháº©m khá»i giá» / Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng
const xoaSanPhamGio = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, req.params.id)
            .query('DELETE FROM GioHangSanPham WHERE MaGH = @id');
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ xÃ³a má»¥c sáº£n pháº©m!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 5. NÃ´ng dÃ¢n xÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘áº·t mua cÃ¡c sáº£n pháº©m Ä‘ang trong giá» -> Táº¡o QR Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30% SePay
const datMuaSanPham = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai } = req.body;
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);

        const gioRes = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .query(`
                SELECT gh.MaGH, gh.SoLuong, sp.GiaTien
                FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                WHERE gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt AND gh.TrangThai = N'Trong giá»'
            `);

        const items = gioRes.recordset;
        if (items.length === 0) {
            return res.json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o trong giá» Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘áº·t mua!' });
        }

        const tongTien = items.reduce((sum, x) => sum + Number(x.GiaTien) * Number(x.SoLuong), 0);
        const tongTienCoc = Math.round(tongTien * 0.3);
        const maTT = taoMaThanhToan();

        let daPhanBo = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            const item = items[i];
            const thanhTienItem = Number(item.GiaTien) * Number(item.SoLuong);
            const cocItem = (i === items.length - 1)
                ? (tongTienCoc - daPhanBo)
                : Math.round(thanhTienItem * 0.3);
            daPhanBo += cocItem;

            await pool.request()
                .input('maGH', sql.Int, item.MaGH)
                .input('mtt', sql.VarChar, maTT)
                .input('coc', sql.Decimal(18, 0), cocItem)
                .query(`
                    UPDATE GioHangSanPham
                    SET TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n',
                        MaThanhToan = @mtt,
                        TienCoc = @coc,
                        NgayThem = GETDATE()
                    WHERE MaGH = @maGH
                `);
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: 'ÄÃ£ táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30%! Vui lÃ²ng thanh toÃ¡n trong 10 phÃºt.',
            maThanhToan: maTT,
            tongTien,
            tienCoc: tongTienCoc,
            giayConLai: THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY,
            qrUrl: taoLinkVietQR(tongTienCoc, maTT),
            bankInfo: {
                bankName: 'VietinBank',
                accountNo: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO,
                accountName: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 6. API Láº¥y lá»‹ch Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘áº·t minh báº¡ch cá»§a tá»«ng mÃ¡y (tá»± má»Ÿ láº¡i ca quÃ¡ háº¡n 10 phÃºt trÆ°á»›c khi tráº£ káº¿t quáº£)
const layLichDaDatMay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const maMay = parseInt(req.params.maMay);
        if (!maMay) {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u mÃ£ mÃ¡y!' });
        }

        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await donDepDonQuaHan10Phut(pool);

        const result = await pool.request()
            .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
            .query(`
                SELECT tm_gh.MaDatMay,
                       CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), tm_gh.NgayThue, 23) AS NgayThue,
                       tm_gh.KhungGio, tm_gh.SoCong, tm_gh.GhiChu, tm_gh.TrangThai,
                       tm_gh.SoDienThoai, nd.HoTen, tm.DonViTinh
                FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON tm_gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                LEFT JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                WHERE tm_gh.MaMay = @maMay
                  AND tm_gh.TrangThai <> N'ÄÃ£ há»§y'
                ORDER BY tm_gh.NgayThue ASC, tm_gh.MaDatMay ASC
            `);

        const lichDaDat = [];
        const lichTrongGio = [];

        result.recordset.forEach(r => {
            if (r.TrangThai === 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n') {
                lichTrongGio.push({
                    NgayThue: r.NgayThue,
                    KhungGio: r.KhungGio
                });
            } else {
                lichDaDat.push({
                    MaDatMay: r.MaDatMay,
                    NgayThue: r.NgayThue,
                    KhungGio: r.KhungGio,
                    SoCong: r.SoCong,
                    DonViTinh: r.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng',
                    KhuVuc: r.GhiChu || 'Khu vá»±c lÃ¢n cáº­n',
                    NguoiThue: `${rutGonTen(r.HoTen)} (${anSoDienThoai(r.SoDienThoai)})`,
                    TrangThai: r.TrangThai
                });
            }
        });

        res.json({ success: true, lichDaDat, lichTrongGio });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y lá»‹ch Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘áº·t mÃ¡y:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 7. ThÃªm dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y vÃ o giá» HOáº¶C Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch thuÃª ngay kÃ¨m QR cá»c 30% SePay (giá»¯ lá»‹ch tá»‘i Ä‘a 10 phÃºt)
const themThueMayVaoGio = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai, maMay, soCong, ngayThue, khungGio, ghiChu, trangThai } = req.body;
    if (!soDienThoai || !maMay) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u thÃ´ng tin tÃ i khoáº£n hoáº·c thiáº¿t bá»‹!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);
        const cong = Math.max(1, parseInt(soCong) || 1);
        const ngayStr = String(ngayThue || '').trim();
        const gioStr = String(khungGio || '').trim();

        // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng láº¥y Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n náº¿u ghi chÃº trá»‘ng
        let ghiChuFinal = String(ghiChu || '').trim();
        if (!ghiChuFinal) {
            const ndRes = await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
                .query(`
                    IF COL_LENGTH('NongDan', 'DiaChi') IS NOT NULL
                        SELECT DiaChi FROM NongDan WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                    ELSE
                        SELECT N'' AS DiaChi
                `);
            if (ndRes.recordset.length > 0 && ndRes.recordset[0].DiaChi) {
                ghiChuFinal = String(ndRes.recordset[0].DiaChi).trim();
            }
        }

        if (trangThai === 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n' || trangThai === 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch') {
            if (!ngayStr || !gioStr) {
                return res.json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng chá»n Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ NgÃ y thuÃª vÃ  Khung giá»!' });
            }
            const donTrung = await kiemTraMayDaBiDat(pool, maMay, ngayStr, gioStr);
            if (donTrung) {
                return res.json({
                    success: false,
                    message: `Ráº¥t tiáº¿c, khung giá» "${gioStr}" ngÃ y ${ngayStr} vá»«a cÃ³ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n khÃ¡c Ä‘áº·t trÆ°á»›c. Vui lÃ²ng chá»n khung giá» trá»‘ng khÃ¡c!`
                });
            }

            const mayRes = await pool.request()
                .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
                .query('SELECT TenMay, GiaThue FROM ThueMay WHERE MaMay = @maMay');

            if (mayRes.recordset.length === 0) {
                return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thiáº¿t bá»‹ mÃ¡y!' });
            }

            const giaThue = Number(mayRes.recordset[0].GiaThue || 0);
            const tongTien = giaThue * cong;
            const tienCoc = Math.round(tongTien * 0.3);
            const maTT = taoMaThanhToan();

            await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
                .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
                .input('cong', sql.Int, cong)
                .input('ngayThue', sql.NVarChar, ngayStr)
                .input('khungGio', sql.NVarChar, gioStr)
                .input('ghiChu', sql.NVarChar, ghiChuFinal)
                .input('mtt', sql.VarChar, maTT)
                .input('tienCoc', sql.Decimal(18, 0), tienCoc)
                .query(`
                    INSERT INTO GioHangThueMay (SoDienThoai, MaMay, SoCong, NgayThue, KhungGio, GhiChu, TrangThai, MaThanhToan, TienCoc, NgayTao)
                    VALUES (@sdt, @maMay, @cong, @ngayThue, @khungGio, @ghiChu, N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', @mtt, @tienCoc, GETDATE())
                `);

            return res.json({
                success: true,
                message: 'ÄÃ£ giá»¯ lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y trong 10 phÃºt! Vui lÃ²ng quÃ©t mÃ£ QR cá»c 30%.',
                maThanhToan: maTT,
                tongTien,
                tienCoc,
                giayConLai: THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY,
                qrUrl: taoLinkVietQR(tienCoc, maTT),
                bankInfo: {
                    bankName: 'VietinBank',
                    accountNo: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO,
                    accountName: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME
                }
            });
        }

        const check = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
            .input('ngayThue', sql.NVarChar, ngayStr)
            .input('khungGio', sql.NVarChar, gioStr)
            .query(`
                SELECT MaDatMay FROM GioHangThueMay 
                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                  AND MaMay = @maMay
                  AND ISNULL(CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), NgayThue, 23), '') = @ngayThue
                  AND ISNULL(KhungGio, '') = @khungGio
                  AND TrangThai = N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n'
            `);

        if (check.recordset.length > 0) {
            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, check.recordset[0].MaDatMay)
                .input('cong', sql.Int, cong)
                .input('ghiChu', sql.NVarChar, ghiChuFinal)
                .query(`
                    UPDATE GioHangThueMay 
                    SET SoCong = SoCong + @cong,
                        GhiChu = CASE WHEN @ghiChu <> '' THEN @ghiChu ELSE GhiChu END
                    WHERE MaDatMay = @id
                `);
        } else {
            await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
                .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
                .input('cong', sql.Int, cong)
                .input('ngayThue', sql.NVarChar, ngayStr)
                .input('khungGio', sql.NVarChar, gioStr)
                .input('ghiChu', sql.NVarChar, ghiChuFinal)
                .query(`
                    INSERT INTO GioHangThueMay (SoDienThoai, MaMay, SoCong, NgayThue, KhungGio, GhiChu, TrangThai, NgayTao)
                    VALUES (@sdt, @maMay, @cong, @ngayThue, @khungGio, @ghiChu, N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n', GETDATE())
                `);
        }

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ thÃªm dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y vÃ o giá»!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i thÃªm thuÃª mÃ¡y:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 8. Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin thuÃª mÃ¡y
const capNhatThueMayGio = async (req, res) => {
    const { maDatMay, soCong, ngayThue, khungGio, ghiChu } = req.body;
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        if (parseInt(soCong) <= 0) {
            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)
                .query('DELETE FROM GioHangThueMay WHERE MaDatMay = @id');
        } else {
            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)
                .input('cong', sql.Int, parseInt(soCong))
                .input('ngayThue', sql.NVarChar, ngayThue || '')
                .input('khungGio', sql.NVarChar, khungGio || '')
                .input('ghiChu', sql.NVarChar, ghiChu || '')
                .query(`
                    UPDATE GioHangThueMay 
                    SET SoCong = @cong, NgayThue = @ngayThue, KhungGio = @khungGio, GhiChu = @ghiChu 
                    WHERE MaDatMay = @id
                `);
        }
        res.json({ success: true });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 9. XÃ³a / Há»§y má»¥c thuÃª mÃ¡y
const xoaThueMayGio = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, req.params.id)
            .query('DELETE FROM GioHangThueMay WHERE MaDatMay = @id');
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ xÃ³a dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 10. NÃ´ng dÃ¢n xÃ¡c nháº­n lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y tá»« giá» -> KhÃ³a khung giá» & táº¡o QR cá»c 30% SePay (giá»¯ 10 phÃºt)
const xacNhanThueMay = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai } = req.body;
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);

        const dsCho = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .query(`
                SELECT tm_gh.MaDatMay, tm_gh.MaMay, tm_gh.SoCong,
                       CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), tm_gh.NgayThue, 23) AS NgayThue,
                       tm_gh.KhungGio, tm.TenMay, tm.GiaThue
                FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                WHERE tm_gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt AND tm_gh.TrangThai = N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n'
            `);

        const items = dsCho.recordset;
        if (items.length === 0) {
            return res.json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ o trong giá» Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ¡c nháº­n!' });
        }

        const danhSachHopLe = [];
        for (const item of items) {
            if (!item.NgayThue || !item.KhungGio) {
                return res.json({
                    success: false,
                    message: `Vui lÃ²ng chá»n Ä‘á»§ NgÃ y thuÃª vÃ  Khung giá» cho "${item.TenMay}" trÆ°á»›c khi xÃ¡c nháº­n!`
                });
            }

            const trungTrongGio = danhSachHopLe.find(
                x => x.MaMay === item.MaMay && x.NgayThue === item.NgayThue && kiemTraTrungKhungGio(x.KhungGio, item.KhungGio)
            );
            if (trungTrongGio) {
                return res.json({
                    success: false,
                    message: `Báº¡n Ä‘ang chá»n 2 khung giá» trÃ¹ng nhau cho "${item.TenMay}" vÃ o ngÃ y ${item.NgayThue}. Vui lÃ²ng chá»‰nh láº¡i!`
                });
            }

            const donTrung = await kiemTraMayDaBiDat(pool, item.MaMay, item.NgayThue, item.KhungGio, item.MaDatMay);
            if (donTrung) {
                return res.json({
                    success: false,
                    message: `MÃ¡y "${item.TenMay}" khung giá» "${item.KhungGio}" ngÃ y ${item.NgayThue} Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i Ä‘áº·t trÆ°á»›c. Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘á»•i khung giá» khÃ¡c!`
                });
            }

            danhSachHopLe.push(item);
        }

        const tongTien = items.reduce((sum, x) => sum + Number(x.GiaThue) * Number(x.SoCong), 0);
        const tongTienCoc = Math.round(tongTien * 0.3);
        const maTT = taoMaThanhToan();

        let daPhanBo = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            const item = items[i];
            const thanhTienItem = Number(item.GiaThue) * Number(item.SoCong);
            const cocItem = (i === items.length - 1)
                ? (tongTienCoc - daPhanBo)
                : Math.round(thanhTienItem * 0.3);
            daPhanBo += cocItem;

            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, item.MaDatMay)
                .input('mtt', sql.VarChar, maTT)
                .input('coc', sql.Decimal(18, 0), cocItem)
                .query(`
                    UPDATE GioHangThueMay 
                    SET TrangThai = N'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n',
                        MaThanhToan = @mtt,
                        TienCoc = @coc,
                        NgayTao = GETDATE()
                    WHERE MaDatMay = @id
                `);
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: 'ÄÃ£ giá»¯ khung giá» trong 10 phÃºt! Vui lÃ²ng quÃ©t mÃ£ QR Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30%.',
            maThanhToan: maTT,
            tongTien,
            tienCoc: tongTienCoc,
            giayConLai: THOI_GIAN_GIU_DON_GIAY,
            qrUrl: taoLinkVietQR(tongTienCoc, maTT),
            bankInfo: {
                bankName: 'VietinBank',
                accountNo: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO,
                accountName: sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

// 11. Admin láº¥y toÃ n bá»™ Ä‘Æ¡n mua sáº£n pháº©m & lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y
const layTatCaDonHangAdmin = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await donDepDonQuaHan10Phut(pool);

        const [resDonSP, resDonMay] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT gh.MaGH, gh.SoDienThoai, gh.SoLuong, gh.TrangThai,
                       gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                       CONVERT(varchar(19), gh.NgayThem, 126) AS NgayThem,
                       sp.TenSanPham, sp.DanhMuc, sp.GiaTien, sp.DonViTinh,
                       nd.HoTen, nd.DiaChi
                FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                ORDER BY gh.MaGH DESC
            `),
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT tm_gh.MaDatMay, tm_gh.SoDienThoai, tm_gh.SoCong,
                       CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), tm_gh.NgayThue, 23) AS NgayThue,
                       tm_gh.KhungGio, tm_gh.GhiChu, tm_gh.TrangThai,
                       tm_gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(tm_gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                       CONVERT(varchar(19), tm_gh.NgayTao, 126) AS NgayTao,
                       tm.TenMay, tm.LoaiMay, tm.GiaThue, tm.DonViTinh,
                       nd.HoTen, nd.DiaChi
                FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON tm_gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                ORDER BY tm_gh.MaDatMay DESC
            `)
        ]);

        res.json({
            success: true,
            donSanPham: resDonSP.recordset,
            donThueMay: resDonMay.recordset
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng Admin:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 12. Cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n sáº£n pháº©m
const capNhatTrangThaiDonSP = async (req, res) => {
    const { maGH, trangThai } = req.body;
    if (!maGH || !trangThai) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u mÃ£ Ä‘Æ¡n hoáº·c tráº¡ng thÃ¡i!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);

        if (trangThai === 'Trong giá»') {
            const itemRes = await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                .query(`
                    SELECT MaGH, SoDienThoai, MaSanPham, SoLuong, TrangThai
                    FROM GioHangSanPham
                    WHERE MaGH = @id
                `);

            if (itemRes.recordset.length === 0) {
                return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng!' });
            }

            const item = itemRes.recordset[0];
            const choPhepTraGio = ['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)'];
            if (!choPhepTraGio.includes(item.TrangThai)) {
                return res.json({
                    success: false,
                    message: `KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ tráº£ vá» giá» vÃ¬ Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘ang á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "${item.TrangThai}"!`
                });
            }

            const existRes = await pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, item.SoDienThoai)
                .input('maSP', sql.Int, item.MaSanPham)
                .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                .query(`
                    SELECT MaGH FROM GioHangSanPham
                    WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND MaSanPham = @maSP
                      AND TrangThai = N'Trong giá»'
                      AND MaGH <> @id
                `);

            if (existRes.recordset.length > 0) {
                await pool.request()
                    .input('targetId', sql.Int, existRes.recordset[0].MaGH)
                    .input('sl', sql.Int, item.SoLuong)
                    .query('UPDATE GioHangSanPham SET SoLuong = SoLuong + @sl WHERE MaGH = @targetId');

                await pool.request()
                    .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                    .query('DELETE FROM GioHangSanPham WHERE MaGH = @id');
            } else {
                await pool.request()
                    .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
                    .query(`
                        UPDATE GioHangSanPham
                        SET TrangThai = N'Trong giá»',
                            MaThanhToan = NULL,
                            TienCoc = NULL,
                            NgayThem = GETDATE()
                        WHERE MaGH = @id
                    `);
            }

            return res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  tráº£ sáº£n pháº©m láº¡i giá» hÃ ng!' });
        }

        const infoRes = await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
            .query(`
                SELECT gh.MaGH, gh.SoDienThoai, gh.SoLuong, gh.TrangThai AS TrangThaiCu,
                       ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                       sp.TenSanPham, sp.GiaTien,
                       nd.HoTen
                FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                WHERE gh.MaGH = @id
            `);

        const donInfo = infoRes.recordset[0];

        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, maGH)
            .input('trangThai', sql.NVarChar, trangThai)
            .query('UPDATE GioHangSanPham SET TrangThai = @trangThai WHERE MaGH = @id');

        if (trangThai === 'Äang giao hÃ ng' && donInfo && donInfo.TrangThaiCu !== 'Äang giao hÃ ng') {
            try {
                const thanhTien = Number(donInfo.GiaTien || 0) * Number(donInfo.SoLuong || 0);
                const tienCoc = Number(donInfo.TienCoc || 0);
                const conLai = Math.max(0, thanhTien - tienCoc);

                const noiDungTin = conLai > 0
                    ? `ðŸ“¦ [ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o giao hÃ ng]: ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng #SP${donInfo.MaGH} (${donInfo.TenSanPham}) Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c giao Ä‘áº¿n báº¡n. Vui lÃ²ng chuáº©n bá»‹ ${conLai.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ tiá»n máº·t hoáº·c má»Ÿ Giá» hÃ ng Ä‘á»ƒ quÃ©t QR thanh toÃ¡n ná»‘t 70% nhÃ©!`
                    : `ðŸ“¦ [ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o giao hÃ ng]: ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng #SP${donInfo.MaGH} (${donInfo.TenSanPham}) Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c giao Ä‘áº¿n báº¡n. BÃ  con chÃº Ã½ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i nháº­n hÃ ng nhÃ©!`;

                await pool.request()
                    .input('hoTen', sql.NVarChar, donInfo.HoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n')
                    .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, donInfo.SoDienThoai)
                    .input('noiDung', sql.NVarChar, noiDungTin)
                    .query(`
                        INSERT INTO TinNhanTuVan (NguoiGui, HoTen, SoDienThoai, NoiDung, NgayGui)
                        VALUES ('admin', @hoTen, @sdt, @noiDung, GETDATE())
                    `);
            } catch (chatErr) {
                console.error('Lá»—i gá»­i tin nháº¯n tá»± Ä‘á»™ng khi giao hÃ ng:', chatErr.message);
            }
        }

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n sáº£n pháº©m!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: err.message });
    }
};

const capNhatTrangThaiDonMay = async (req, res) => {
    const { maDatMay, trangThai } = req.body;
    if (!maDatMay || !trangThai) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u mÃ£ phiáº¿u thuÃª hoáº·c tráº¡ng thÃ¡i!' });
    }

        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotThanhToan(pool);

            const phieuRes = await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)
                .query('SELECT MaDatMay, TrangThai FROM GioHangThueMay WHERE MaDatMay = @id');

                return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y phiáº¿u thuÃª mÃ¡y!' });

                    success: false,

                });
            }

            await pool.request()
                .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)
                .query(`
                    UPDATE GioHangThueMay
                    SET TrangThai = N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n',
                        MaThanhToan = NULL,
                        TienCoc = NULL,
                        NgayTao = GETDATE()
                    WHERE MaDatMay = @id
                `);

            return res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ há»§y lá»‹ch vÃ  Ä‘Æ°a dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y trá»Ÿ láº¡i giá» hÃ ng!' });
        }

        const infoRes = await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)

        const phieu = infoRes.recordset[0];
        if (!phieu) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y phiáº¿u thuÃª mÃ¡y!' });
        }

        if (['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n', 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh'].includes(trangThai)) {

            if (donTrung) {

                    success: false,

                });
            }
        }

        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, maDatMay)
            .input('trangThai', sql.NVarChar, trangThai)
            .query('UPDATE GioHangThueMay SET TrangThai = @trangThai WHERE MaDatMay = @id');

        if (trangThai === 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n' && phieu.TrangThaiCu !== 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n') {

                    ? `ðŸšœ [ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o Ä‘iá»u mÃ¡y]: Phiáº¿u thuÃª #TM${phieu.MaDatMay} (${phieu.TenMay}) Ä‘Ã£ xuáº¥t phÃ¡t phá»¥c vá»¥ Ä‘á»“ng ruá»™ng ngÃ y ${phieu.NgayThue} (${phieu.KhungGio}).                     : `ðŸšœ [ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o Ä‘iá»u mÃ¡y]: Phiáº¿u thuÃª #TM${phieu.MaDatMay} (${phieu.TenMay}) Ä‘Ã£ xuáº¥t phÃ¡t phá»¥c vá»¥ Ä‘á»“ng ruá»™ng ngÃ y ${phieu.NgayThue} (${phieu.KhungGio}). BÃ  con chÃº Ã½ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i phá»‘i há»£p nhÃ©!`;

const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

async function damBaoCotNongDan(pool) {
    await pool.request().query(`
        IF COL_LENGTH('NongDan', 'DiaChi') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE NongDan ADD DiaChi NVARCHAR(255) NULL;
    `);
}

// ================= PHáº¦N DÃ€NH CHO NÃ”NG DÃ‚N (Há»’ SÆ  & Äá»”I Máº¬T KHáº¨U) =================

// 1. Láº¥y thÃ´ng tin há»“ sÆ¡ + thá»‘ng kÃª hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n
const layHoSoNongDan = async (req, res) => {
    const { sdt } = req.params;
    if (!sdt) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotNongDan(pool);

        const userRes = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
            .query(`
                SELECT HoTen, SoDienThoai, ISNULL(DiaChi, N'') AS DiaChi
                FROM NongDan
                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
            `);

        if (userRes.recordset.length === 0) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n!' });
        }

        const [spRes, mayRes, skRes] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query(`
                    SELECT
                        COUNT(*) AS SoDon,
                        ISNULL(SUM(
                            CASE
                                WHEN gh.TrangThai = N'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh' THEN CAST(sp.GiaTien AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * gh.SoLuong
                                WHEN gh.TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang giao hÃ ng') THEN ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0)
                                ELSE 0
                            END
                        ), 0) AS DaChiTieu
                    FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                    INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                    WHERE gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND gh.TrangThai NOT IN (N'Trong giá»', N'ÄÃ£ há»§y')
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query(`
                    SELECT
                        COUNT(*) AS SoLich,
                        ISNULL(SUM(
                            CASE
                                WHEN tm_gh.TrangThai = N'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh' THEN CAST(tm.GiaThue AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * tm_gh.SoCong
                                WHEN tm_gh.TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', N'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n') THEN ISNULL(tm_gh.TienCoc, 0)
                                ELSE 0
                            END
                        ), 0) AS DaChiTieu
                    FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                    INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                    WHERE tm_gh.SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND tm_gh.TrangThai NOT IN (N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n', N'ÄÃ£ há»§y')
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query('SELECT COUNT(*) AS SoSuKien FROM DangKySuKien WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt')
        ]);

        const soDonSP = Number(spRes.recordset[0]?.SoDon || 0);
        const soLichMay = Number(mayRes.recordset[0]?.SoLich || 0);
        const soSuKien = Number(skRes.recordset[0]?.SoSuKien || 0);
        const tongChiTieu = Number(spRes.recordset[0]?.DaChiTieu || 0) + Number(mayRes.recordset[0]?.DaChiTieu || 0);

        res.json({
            success: true,
            data: userRes.recordset[0],
            thongKe: {
                soDonSP,
                soLichMay,
                soSuKien,
                tongChiTieu
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y há»“ sÆ¡ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 2. NÃ´ng dÃ¢n tá»± cáº­p nháº­t Há» tÃªn & Äá»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh
const capNhatHoSoNongDan = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai, hoTen, diaChi } = req.body;
    if (!soDienThoai || !hoTen || !hoTen.trim()) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ Há» tÃªn!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotNongDan(pool);

        const hoTenClean = hoTen.trim();
        const diaChiClean = String(diaChi || '').trim();

        const result = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .input('hoTen', sql.NVarChar, hoTenClean)
            .input('diaChi', sql.NVarChar, diaChiClean)
            .query(`
                UPDATE NongDan
                SET HoTen = @hoTen,
                    DiaChi = @diaChi
                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
            `);

        if (result.rowsAffected[0] === 0) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘á»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t!' });
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: 'ÄÃ£ lÆ°u thÃ´ng tin há»“ sÆ¡ & Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh!',
            data: {
                soDienThoai,
                hoTen: hoTenClean,
                diaChi: diaChiClean
            }
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i cáº­p nháº­t há»“ sÆ¡:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// 3. NÃ´ng dÃ¢n tá»± Ä‘á»•i máº­t kháº©u
const doiMatKhauNongDan = async (req, res) => {
    const { soDienThoai, matKhauCu, matKhauMoi } = req.body;
    if (!soDienThoai || !matKhauCu || !matKhauMoi) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ máº­t kháº©u cÅ© vÃ  máº­t kháº©u má»›i!' });
    }
    if (String(matKhauMoi).length < 6) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Máº­t kháº©u má»›i pháº£i cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 6 kÃ½ tá»±!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const checkRes = await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .query('SELECT MatKhau FROM NongDan WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');

        if (checkRes.recordset.length === 0) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n!' });
        }

        const matKhauHienTai = String(checkRes.recordset[0].MatKhau || '');
        if (matKhauHienTai !== String(matKhauCu)) {
            return res.json({ success: false, message: 'Máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c!' });
        }

        await pool.request()
            .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai)
            .input('mkMoi', sql.NVarChar, String(matKhauMoi))
            .query('UPDATE NongDan SET MatKhau = @mkMoi WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Äá»•i máº­t kháº©u thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i Ä‘á»•i máº­t kháº©u:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// ================= PHáº¦N DÃ€NH CHO ADMIN QUáº¢N LÃ NÃ”NG DÃ‚N =================

// 4. Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch toÃ n bá»™ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n kÃ¨m tá»•ng chi tiÃªu, sá»‘ Ä‘Æ¡n, Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng
const layDanhSachNongDanAdmin = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCotNongDan(pool);

        const result = await pool.request().query(`
            SELECT
                nd.HoTen,
                nd.SoDienThoai,
                ISNULL(nd.DiaChi, N'') AS DiaChi,
                ISNULL(sp_agg.SoDonSP, 0) AS SoDonSP,
                ISNULL(sp_agg.ChiTieuSP, 0) AS ChiTieuSP,
                ISNULL(sp_agg.GiaTriSP, 0) AS GiaTriSP,
                ISNULL(may_agg.SoLichMay, 0) AS SoLichMay,
                ISNULL(may_agg.ChiTieuMay, 0) AS ChiTieuMay,
                ISNULL(may_agg.GiaTriMay, 0) AS GiaTriMay,
                ISNULL(sk_agg.SoSuKien, 0) AS SoSuKien,
                (ISNULL(sp_agg.ChiTieuSP, 0) + ISNULL(may_agg.ChiTieuMay, 0)) AS TongChiTieu,
                (ISNULL(sp_agg.GiaTriSP, 0) + ISNULL(may_agg.GiaTriMay, 0)) AS TongGiaTriDon
            FROM NongDan nd
            LEFT JOIN (
                SELECT
                    gh.SoDienThoai,
                    COUNT(*) AS SoDonSP,
                    SUM(CAST(sp.GiaTien AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * gh.SoLuong) AS GiaTriSP,
                    SUM(
                        CASE
                            WHEN gh.TrangThai = N'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh' THEN CAST(sp.GiaTien AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * gh.SoLuong
                            WHEN gh.TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang giao hÃ ng') THEN ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0)
                            ELSE 0
                        END
                    ) AS ChiTieuSP
                FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                WHERE gh.TrangThai NOT IN (N'Trong giá»', N'ÄÃ£ há»§y')
                GROUP BY gh.SoDienThoai
            ) sp_agg ON nd.SoDienThoai = sp_agg.SoDienThoai
            LEFT JOIN (
                SELECT
                    tm_gh.SoDienThoai,
                    COUNT(*) AS SoLichMay,
                    SUM(CAST(tm.GiaThue AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * tm_gh.SoCong) AS GiaTriMay,
                    SUM(
                        CASE
                            WHEN tm_gh.TrangThai = N'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh' THEN CAST(tm.GiaThue AS DECIMAL(18,0)) * tm_gh.SoCong
                            WHEN tm_gh.TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', N'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n') THEN ISNULL(tm_gh.TienCoc, 0)
                            ELSE 0
                        END
                    ) AS ChiTieuMay
                FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                WHERE tm_gh.TrangThai NOT IN (N'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n', N'ÄÃ£ há»§y')
                GROUP BY tm_gh.SoDienThoai
            ) may_agg ON nd.SoDienThoai = may_agg.SoDienThoai
            LEFT JOIN (
                SELECT SoDienThoai, COUNT(*) AS SoSuKien
                FROM DangKySuKien
                GROUP BY SoDienThoai
            ) sk_agg ON nd.SoDienThoai = sk_agg.SoDienThoai
            ORDER BY TongChiTieu DESC, nd.HoTen ASC
        `);

        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Admin:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt

        }

        res.json({ success: true, message: `ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t láº¡i máº­t kháº©u má»›i cho SÄT ${soDienThoai}!` });
    } catch (err) {

    }
};

// 7. Admin xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n
const adminXoaNongDan = async (req, res) => {
    const { sdt } = req.params;
    if (!sdt) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i!' });
    }

    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const [activeSP, activeMay] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query(`
                    SELECT COUNT(*) AS Dem FROM GioHangSanPham
                    WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang giao hÃ ng')
                `),
            pool.request()
                .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt)
                .query(`
                    SELECT COUNT(*) AS Dem FROM GioHangThueMay
                    WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt
                      AND TrangThai IN (N'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', N'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', N'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n')
                `)
        ]);

        if ((activeSP.recordset[0]?.Dem || 0) > 0 || (activeMay.recordset[0]?.Dem || 0) > 0) {
            return res.json({
                success: false,
                message: 'TÃ i khoáº£n nÃ y Ä‘ang cÃ³ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng Ä‘Ã£ cá»c hoáº·c lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y Ä‘ang triá»ƒn khai, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a!'
            });
        }

        await pool.request().input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt).query('DELETE FROM GioHangSanPham WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');
        await pool.request().input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt).query('DELETE FROM GioHangThueMay WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');
        await pool.request().input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt).query('DELETE FROM DangKySuKien WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');
        await pool.request().input('sdt', sql.VarChar, sdt).query('DELETE FROM NongDan WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt');

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n khá»i há»‡ thá»‘ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

// HÃ m há»— trá»£ láº¥y MaDanhMuc tá»« báº£ng DanhMucSanPham (tá»± thÃªm náº¿u chÆ°a cÃ³)
async function layHoacTaoMaDanhMuc(pool, tenDanhMuc) {
    const tenClean = String(tenDanhMuc || '').trim();
    if (!tenClean) return null;

    await pool.request().query(`
        IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.DanhMucSanPham', 'U') IS NULL
        BEGIN
            CREATE TABLE dbo.DanhMucSanPham (
                MaDanhMuc INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
                TenDanhMuc NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                MoTa NVARCHAR(255) NULL
            );
        END
        IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.SanPham', 'MaDanhMuc') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE dbo.SanPham ADD MaDanhMuc INT NULL;
    `);

    const checkRes = await pool.request()
        .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenClean)
        .query('SELECT MaDanhMuc FROM dbo.DanhMucSanPham WHERE TenDanhMuc = @ten');

    if (checkRes.recordset.length > 0) {
        return checkRes.recordset[0].MaDanhMuc;
    }

    const insertRes = await pool.request()
        .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenClean)
        .query(`
            INSERT INTO dbo.DanhMucSanPham (TenDanhMuc)
            OUTPUT INSERTED.MaDanhMuc
            VALUES (@ten)
        `);

    return insertRes.recordset[0].MaDanhMuc;
}

// Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch sáº£n pháº©m tá»« CSDL (JOIN vá»›i báº£ng DanhMucSanPham)
const layDanhSachSanPham = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        let result = await pool.request().query(`
            SELECT sp.*, ISNULL(dm.TenDanhMuc, sp.DanhMuc) AS TenDanhMucChuan
            FROM SanPham sp
            LEFT JOIN DanhMucSanPham dm ON sp.MaDanhMuc = dm.MaDanhMuc
            ORDER BY sp.MaSanPham DESC
        `);
        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i khi láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch sáº£n pháº©m:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// ThÃªm sáº£n pháº©m má»›i vÃ o CSDL (tá»± Ä‘á»™ng gáº¯n khÃ³a ngoáº¡i MaDanhMuc)
const themSanPham = async (req, res) => {
    const { tenSanPham, danhMuc, giaTien, hinhAnh, moTa } = req.body;

    if (!tenSanPham || !giaTien || !danhMuc) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘iá»n Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ tÃªn sáº£n pháº©m, danh má»¥c vÃ  giÃ¡ tiá»n!' });
    }

    let donViTinh = 'Chai';
    const dmLower = String(danhMuc).toLowerCase();
    if (dmLower.includes('phÃ¢n') || dmLower.includes('giá»‘ng')) {
        donViTinh = 'Bao';
    }

    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const maDanhMuc = await layHoacTaoMaDanhMuc(pool, danhMuc);

        await pool.request()
            .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenSanPham)
            .input('danhmuc', sql.NVarChar, danhMuc)
            .input('madanhmuc', sql.Int, maDanhMuc)
            .input('giatien', sql.Decimal(18, 0), giaTien)
            .input('donvitinh', sql.NVarChar, donViTinh)
            .input('hinhanh', sql.NVarChar, hinhAnh || '')
            .input('mota', sql.NVarChar, moTa || '')
            .query(`
                INSERT INTO SanPham (TenSanPham, DanhMuc, MaDanhMuc, GiaTien, DonViTinh, HinhAnh, MoTa, TrangThai)
                VALUES (@ten, @danhmuc, @madanhmuc, @giatien, @donvitinh, @hinhanh, @mota, N'Äang bÃ¡n')
            `);

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ThÃªm sáº£n pháº©m thÃ nh cÃ´ng vÃ o CSDL!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i khi thÃªm sáº£n pháº©m:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

// Cáº­p nháº­t sáº£n pháº©m (Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ cáº£ DanhMuc vÃ  khÃ³a ngoáº¡i MaDanhMuc)
const suaSanPham = async (req, res) => {
    const { maSanPham, tenSanPham, danhMuc, giaTien, hinhAnh, moTa } = req.body;

    if (!maSanPham || !tenSanPham || !giaTien || !danhMuc) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Thiáº¿u thÃ´ng tin cáº­p nháº­t sáº£n pháº©m!' });
    }

    let donViTinh = 'Chai';
    const dmLower = String(danhMuc).toLowerCase();
    if (dmLower.includes('phÃ¢n') || dmLower.includes('giá»‘ng')) {
        donViTinh = 'Bao';
    }

    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const maDanhMuc = await layHoacTaoMaDanhMuc(pool, danhMuc);

        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, maSanPham)
            .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenSanPham)
            .input('danhmuc', sql.NVarChar, danhMuc)
            .input('madanhmuc', sql.Int, maDanhMuc)
            .input('giatien', sql.Decimal(18, 0), giaTien)
            .input('donvitinh', sql.NVarChar, donViTinh)
            .input('hinhanh', sql.NVarChar, hinhAnh || '')
            .input('mota', sql.NVarChar, moTa || '')
            .query(`
                UPDATE SanPham 
                SET TenSanPham = @ten, DanhMuc = @danhmuc, MaDanhMuc = @madanhmuc, GiaTien = @giatien, 
                    DonViTinh = @donvitinh, HinhAnh = @hinhanh, MoTa = @mota
                WHERE MaSanPham = @id
            `);

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t sáº£n pháº©m thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i khi sá»­a sáº£n pháº©m:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

};

=== FILE: D:\DO AN 2 CHUYEN NGANH\dieu-khien\sepayController.js ===
const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');
const sepayConfig = require('../cau-hinh/sepay');

// HÃ m táº¡o áº£nh mÃ£ QR VietQR Ä‘á»™ng (tá»± Ä‘á»™ng gáº¯n tiá»n tá»‘ SEVQR chuáº©n VietinBank + SePay)
function taoLinkVietQR(soTien, maThanhToan) {
    const bankId = encodeURIComponent(sepayConfig.BANK_ID);
    const accNo = encodeURIComponent(sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NO);
    const accName = encodeURIComponent(sepayConfig.ACCOUNT_NAME);

    const prefix = (sepayConfig.CONTENT_PREFIX || 'SEVQR').trim();
    const rawInfo = String(maThanhToan || '').trim();
    const fullInfo = rawInfo.toUpperCase().startsWith(prefix.toUpperCase())
        ? rawInfo
        : `${prefix} ${rawInfo}`;

    const addInfo = encodeURIComponent(fullInfo);
    return `https://img.vietqr.io/image/${bankId}-${accNo}-compact2.png?amount=${soTien}&addInfo=${addInfo}&accountName=${accName}`;
}

// Äáº£m báº£o báº£ng LichSuGiaoDich vÃ  cÃ¡c cá»™t thanh toÃ¡n tá»“n táº¡i
async function damBaoCauTrucThanhToan(pool) {
    await pool.request().query(`
        IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.LichSuGiaoDich', 'U') IS NULL
        BEGIN
            CREATE TABLE dbo.LichSuGiaoDich (
                MaGD INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
                MaGiaoDichSePay VARCHAR(100) NULL,
                MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL,
                SoTienNhan DECIMAL(18, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                NoiDungCK NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
                NganHang VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'VietinBank',
                ThoiGianGD DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE(),
                TrangThaiXuLy NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'ÄÃ£ ghi nháº­n'
            );
        END

        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
        IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
    `);
}

// Ghi 1 dÃ²ng giao dá»‹ch vÃ o báº£ng LichSuGiaoDich trong SQL Server
async function ghiLichSuGiaoDich(pool, { maGiaoDichSePay, maThanhToan, soTienNhan, noiDungCK, trangThaiXuLy }) {
    try {
        await pool.request()
            .input('maGDSePay', sql.VarChar, String(maGiaoDichSePay || ''))
            .input('maTT', sql.VarChar, String(maThanhToan || ''))
            .input('soTien', sql.Decimal(18, 0), Number(soTienNhan || 0))
            .input('noiDung', sql.NVarChar, String(noiDungCK || ''))
            .input('trangThai', sql.NVarChar, String(trangThaiXuLy || 'ÄÃ£ ghi nháº­n'))
            .query(`
                INSERT INTO dbo.LichSuGiaoDich (MaGiaoDichSePay, MaThanhToan, SoTienNhan, NoiDungCK, NganHang, ThoiGianGD, TrangThaiXuLy)
                VALUES (@maGDSePay, @maTT, @soTien, @noiDung, 'VietinBank', GETDATE(), @trangThai)
            `);
    } catch (logErr) {
        console.error('Lá»—i ghi báº£ng LichSuGiaoDich:', logErr.message);
    }
}

// API nháº­n biáº¿n Ä‘á»™ng sá»‘ dÆ° trá»±c tiáº¿p tá»« SePay (POST /api/payment/webhook)
const nhanWebhookSePay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const body = req.body || {};
        const thoiGian = new Date().toLocaleString('vi-VN');

        console.log('====================================================');
        console.log(`ðŸ“© [${thoiGian}] NHáº¬N WEBHOOK Tá»ª SEPAY:`);
        console.log(JSON.stringify(body, null, 2));
        console.log('====================================================');

        const pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await damBaoCauTrucThanhToan(pool);

        const maGDSePay = String(body.id || body.referenceCode || body.transaction_id || '');
        const loaiGD = String(body.transferType || '').toLowerCase();
        const soTienVao = parseFloat(body.transferAmount || body.amount_in || 0);
        const noiDungGoc = String(body.content || body.transaction_content || body.code || '');
        const noiDungSach = noiDungGoc.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '');

        // Chá»‰ xá»­ lÃ½ giao dá»‹ch tiá»n vÃ o tÃ i khoáº£n
        if (loaiGD && loaiGD !== 'in') {
            await ghiLichSuGiaoDich(pool, {
                maGiaoDichSePay: maGDSePay,
                maThanhToan: '',
                soTienNhan: soTienVao,
                noiDungCK: noiDungGoc,
                trangThaiXuLy: 'Bá» qua (Giao dá»‹ch tiá»n ra)'
            });
            return res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'Bá» qua giao dá»‹ch tiá»n ra' });
        }

        // TÃ¬m mÃ£ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng dáº¡ng LVxxxxxx trong ná»™i dung chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n
        const match = noiDungSach.match(/LV\d{6}/);
        if (!match || soTienVao <= 0) {
            await ghiLichSuGiaoDich(pool, {
                maGiaoDichSePay: maGDSePay,
                maThanhToan: '',
                soTienNhan: soTienVao,

// 1. Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch sá»± kiá»‡n
const layDanhSachSuKien = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        let result = await pool.request().query('SELECT * FROM SuKien ORDER BY MaSuKien DESC');
        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 2. ThÃªm sá»± kiá»‡n má»›i
const themSuKien = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { tieuDe, thoiGian, diaDiem, moTa } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('tieuDe', tieuDe)
            .input('thoiGian', thoiGian)
            .input('diaDiem', diaDiem)
            .input('moTa', moTa || '')
            .query(`INSERT INTO SuKien (TieuDe, ThoiGian, DiaDiem, MoTa) 
                    VALUES (@tieuDe, @thoiGian, @diaDiem, @moTa)`);
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Táº¡o sá»± kiá»‡n má»›i thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

const suaSuKien = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { maSuKien, tieuDe, thoiGian, diaDiem, moTa } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('maSuKien', maSuKien)
            .input('tieuDe', tieuDe)
            .input('thoiGian', thoiGian)
            .input('diaDiem', diaDiem)
            .input('moTa', moTa || '')
            .query(`UPDATE SuKien SET TieuDe=@tieuDe, ThoiGian=@thoiGian, 
                    DiaDiem=@diaDiem, MoTa=@moTa 
                    WHERE MaSuKien=@maSuKien`);
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t sá»± kiá»‡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};
