const { ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

// API láº¥y toÃ n bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u phá»¥c vá»¥ thá»‘ng kÃª theo NgÃ y / ThÃ¡ng / NÄƒm cho Trang chá»§ Admin
const layThongKeTongHop = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const pool = await ketNoiDb();

        // Äáº£m báº£o cÃ¡c cá»™t thanh toÃ¡n tá»“n táº¡i Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh lá»—i truy váº¥n trÃªn DB cÅ©
        await pool.request().query(`
            IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
                ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
            IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangSanPham', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
                ALTER TABLE GioHangSanPham ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
            IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'MaThanhToan') IS NULL
                ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD MaThanhToan VARCHAR(50) NULL;
            IF COL_LENGTH('GioHangThueMay', 'TienCoc') IS NULL
                ALTER TABLE GioHangThueMay ADD TienCoc DECIMAL(18, 0) NULL;
        `);

        const [resSP, resMay, resSuKien, resDangKySK, resNongDan] = await Promise.all([
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT gh.MaGH, gh.SoDienThoai, gh.SoLuong, gh.TrangThai,
                       gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                       CONVERT(varchar(19), gh.NgayThem, 126) AS NgayThem,
                       sp.MaSanPham, sp.TenSanPham, sp.DanhMuc, sp.GiaTien, sp.DonViTinh,
                       nd.HoTen
                FROM GioHangSanPham gh
                INNER JOIN SanPham sp ON gh.MaSanPham = sp.MaSanPham
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                ORDER BY gh.MaGH DESC
            `),
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT tm_gh.MaDatMay, tm_gh.SoDienThoai, tm_gh.SoCong,
                       CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), tm_gh.NgayThue, 23) AS NgayThue,
                       tm_gh.KhungGio, tm_gh.GhiChu, tm_gh.TrangThai,
                       tm_gh.MaThanhToan, ISNULL(tm_gh.TienCoc, 0) AS TienCoc,
                       CONVERT(varchar(19), tm_gh.NgayTao, 126) AS NgayTao,
                       tm.MaMay, tm.TenMay, tm.LoaiMay, tm.GiaThue, tm.DonViTinh,
                       nd.HoTen
                FROM GioHangThueMay tm_gh
                INNER JOIN ThueMay tm ON tm_gh.MaMay = tm.MaMay
                LEFT JOIN NongDan nd ON tm_gh.SoDienThoai = nd.SoDienThoai
                ORDER BY tm_gh.MaDatMay DESC
            `),
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT * FROM SuKien
            `),
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT dk.MaDK, dk.TieuDeSuKien, dk.HoTen, dk.SoDienThoai,
                       CONVERT(varchar(19), dk.NgayDangKy, 126) AS NgayDangKy
                FROM DangKySuKien dk
                ORDER BY dk.MaDK DESC
            `),
            pool.request().query(`
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS TongNongDan FROM NongDan
            `)
        ]);

        res.json({
            success: true,
            donSanPham: resSP.recordset || [],
            donThueMay: resMay.recordset || [],
            danhSachSuKien: resSuKien.recordset || [],
            dangKySuKien: resDangKySK.recordset || [],
            tongNongDan: resNongDan.recordset[0]?.TongNongDan || 0
        });
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Lá»—i láº¥y thá»‘ng kÃª tá»•ng há»£p Admin:', err);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Lá»—i server: ' + err.message });
    }
};

    layThongKeTongHop
};
=== FILE: D:\DO AN 2 CHUYEN NGANH\dieu-khien\thueMayController.js ===
const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');

// HÃ m há»— trá»£ láº¥y MaLoaiMay tá»« báº£ng LoaiMay (tá»± thÃªm náº¿u chÆ°a cÃ³)
async function layHoacTaoMaLoaiMay(pool, tenLoaiMay) {
    const tenClean = String(tenLoaiMay || '').trim();
    if (!tenClean) return null;

    await pool.request().query(`
        IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.LoaiMay', 'U') IS NULL
        BEGIN
            CREATE TABLE dbo.LoaiMay (
                MaLoaiMay INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
                TenLoaiMay NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                MoTa NVARCHAR(255) NULL
            );
        END
        IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.ThueMay', 'MaLoaiMay') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE dbo.ThueMay ADD MaLoaiMay INT NULL;
    `);

    const checkRes = await pool.request()
        .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenClean)
        .query('SELECT MaLoaiMay FROM dbo.LoaiMay WHERE TenLoaiMay = @ten');

    if (checkRes.recordset.length > 0) {
        return checkRes.recordset[0].MaLoaiMay;
    }

    const insertRes = await pool.request()
        .input('ten', sql.NVarChar, tenClean)
        .query(`
            INSERT INTO dbo.LoaiMay (TenLoaiMay)
            OUTPUT INSERTED.MaLoaiMay
            VALUES (@ten)
        `);

    return insertRes.recordset[0].MaLoaiMay;
}

// 1. Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuÃª (JOIN vá»›i báº£ng LoaiMay)
const layDanhSachThueMay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        let result = await pool.request().query(`
            SELECT tm.*, ISNULL(lm.TenLoaiMay, tm.LoaiMay) AS TenLoaiMayChuan
            FROM ThueMay tm
            LEFT JOIN LoaiMay lm ON tm.MaLoaiMay = lm.MaLoaiMay
            ORDER BY tm.MaMay DESC
        `);
        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 2. ThÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuÃª má»›i (tá»± Ä‘á»™ng gáº¯n khÃ³a ngoáº¡i MaLoaiMay)
const themThueMay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { tenMay, loaiMay, giaThue, donViTinh, hinhAnh, moTa, trangThai } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const maLoaiMay = await layHoacTaoMaLoaiMay(pool, loaiMay);

        await pool.request()
            .input('tenMay', sql.NVarChar, tenMay)
            .input('loaiMay', sql.NVarChar, loaiMay)
            .input('maLoaiMay', sql.Int, maLoaiMay)
            .input('giaThue', sql.Decimal(18, 0), giaThue)
            .input('donViTinh', sql.NVarChar, donViTinh || 'cÃ´ng')
            .input('hinhAnh', sql.NVarChar, hinhAnh || 'default.jpg')
            .input('moTa', sql.NVarChar, moTa || '')
            .input('trangThai', sql.NVarChar, trangThai || 'Sáºµn sÃ ng')
            .query(`
                INSERT INTO ThueMay (TenMay, LoaiMay, MaLoaiMay, GiaThue, DonViTinh, HinhAnh, MoTa, TrangThai) 
                VALUES (@tenMay, @loaiMay, @maLoaiMay, @giaThue, @donViTinh, @hinhAnh, @moTa, @trangThai)
            `);
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ThÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuÃª thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 3. Chá»‰nh sá»­a thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuÃª (Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ cáº£ LoaiMay vÃ  khÃ³a ngoáº¡i MaLoaiMay)
const suaThueMay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { maMay, tenMay, loaiMay, giaThue, donViTinh, hinhAnh, moTa, trangThai } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        const maLoaiMay = await layHoacTaoMaLoaiMay(pool, loaiMay);

        await pool.request()
            .input('maMay', sql.Int, maMay)
            .input('tenMay', sql.NVarChar, tenMay)
            .input('loaiMay', sql.NVarChar, loaiMay)
            .input('maLoaiMay', sql.Int, maLoaiMay)
            .input('giaThue', sql.Decimal(18, 0), giaThue)
            .input('donViTinh', sql.NVarChar, donViTinh)
            .input('hinhAnh', sql.NVarChar, hinhAnh)
            .input('moTa', sql.NVarChar, moTa)
            .input('trangThai', sql.NVarChar, trangThai)
            .query(`
                UPDATE ThueMay 
                SET TenMay = @tenMay, LoaiMay = @loaiMay, MaLoaiMay = @maLoaiMay, GiaThue = @giaThue, 
                    DonViTinh = @donViTinh, HinhAnh = @hinhAnh, MoTa = @moTa, TrangThai = @trangThai 
                WHERE MaMay = @maMay
            `);
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t thiáº¿t bá»‹ thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

// 4. XÃ³a thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuÃª
const xoaThueMay = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        await pool.request()
            .input('id', sql.Int, id)
            .query('DELETE FROM ThueMay WHERE MaMay = @id');
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'ÄÃ£ xÃ³a thiáº¿t bá»‹ thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

    layDanhSachThueMay,
    themThueMay,
    suaThueMay,
    xoaThueMay

const { sql, ketNoiDb } = require('../cau-hinh/ket-noi-db');
const { Groq } = require('groq-sdk');
require('dotenv').config();

const groq = new Groq({
    apiKey: process.env.GROQ_API_KEY
});

// Gemini lÃ  tÃ¹y chá»n: chá»‰ báº­t khi cÃ³ GEMINI_API_KEY trong .env
let gemini = null;
if (process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) {
    const { GoogleGenAI } = require('@google/genai');
    gemini = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey: process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY });
}

const DANH_SACH_MODEL_GROQ = ["openai/gpt-oss-120b", "openai/gpt-oss-20b"];

// ---- Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i admin online (lÆ°u trong bá»™ nhá»› server) ----
let adminLastSeen = 0;
const ADMIN_ONLINE_MS = 30 * 1000;

const adminDangOnline = () => Date.now() - adminLastSeen < ADMIN_ONLINE_MS;

const adminPing = (req, res) => {
    adminLastSeen = Date.now();
    res.json({ success: true });
};

const trangThaiAdmin = (req, res) => {
    res.json({ success: true, online: adminDangOnline() });
};

const adminOffline = (req, res) => {
    adminLastSeen = 0;
    res.json({ success: true });
};

// Bá» pháº§n chá»¯ thÃªm vÃ o khi lÆ°u Ä‘á»ƒ AI khÃ´ng Ä‘á»c láº¡i pháº§n Ä‘Ã³
function lamSachTinAI(text) {
    return String(text || '')
        .replace(/^ðŸ¤– \[Trá»£ lÃ½ AI LÃºa VÃ ng\]: /, '')
        .replace(/\n\nâ³ \(Há»‡ thá»‘ng[\s\S]*$/, '');
}

// Truy váº¥n toÃ n bá»™ Sáº£n pháº©m, ThuÃª mÃ¡y, Sá»± kiá»‡n tá»« SQL Server Ä‘á»ƒ náº¡p cho AI
async function taoPromptHeThong(pool) {
    const [resSanPham, resThueMay, resSuKien] = await Promise.all([
        pool.request().query(`
            SELECT TenSanPham, DanhMuc, GiaTien, DonViTinh, MoTa, TrangThai 
            FROM SanPham
        `),
        pool.request().query(`
            SELECT TenMay, LoaiMay, GiaThue, DonViTinh, MoTa, TrangThai 
            FROM ThueMay
        `),
        pool.request().query(`
            SELECT TieuDe, ThoiGian, DiaDiem, MoTa 
            FROM SuKien
        `)
    ]);

    const dsSanPham = resSanPham.recordset.map(sp => {
        const gia = Number(sp.GiaTien).toLocaleString('vi-VN');
        const moTaNgan = (sp.MoTa || '').slice(0, 120);
        return `- [${sp.DanhMuc}] ${sp.TenSanPham}: ${gia} Ä‘/${sp.DonViTinh} (${sp.TrangThai || 'Äang bÃ¡n'}) - ${moTaNgan}`;
    }).join('\n') || 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m.';

    const dsThueMay = resThueMay.recordset.map(m => {
        const gia = Number(m.GiaThue).toLocaleString('vi-VN');
        const moTaNgan = (m.MoTa || '').slice(0, 110);
        return `- [${m.LoaiMay}] ${m.TenMay}: ${gia} Ä‘/${m.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng'} (${m.TrangThai || 'Sáºµn sÃ ng'}) - ${moTaNgan}`;
    }).join('\n') || 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y.';

    const dsSuKien = resSuKien.recordset.map(sk => {
        return `- ${sk.TieuDe} | Thá»i gian: ${sk.ThoiGian} | Äá»‹a Ä‘iá»ƒm: ${sk.DiaDiem} (${sk.MoTa || ''})`;
    }).join('\n') || 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ o.';

    return `Báº¡n lÃ  Trá»£ lÃ½ AI nÃ´ng nghiá»‡p chuyÃªn nghiá»‡p cá»§a há»‡ thá»‘ng 'LÃºa VÃ ng' táº¡i miá»n TÃ¢y.
Phong cÃ¡ch tráº£ lá»i: Ngáº¯n gá»n (3-5 cÃ¢u), thÃ¢n thiá»‡n (xÆ°ng 'Dáº¡ bÃ  con' / 'em'), rÃµ rÃ ng, bÃ¡o Ä‘Ãºng giÃ¡ tiá»n VNÄ.

DÆ¯á»šI ÄÃ‚Y LÃ€ Dá»® LIá»†U THá»°C Táº¾ Cá»¦A Há»† THá»NG LÃšA VÃ€NG (CHá»ˆ ÄÆ¯á»¢C TÆ¯ Váº¤N Dá»°A TRÃŠN Dá»® LIá»†U NÃ€Y):

1. THÃ”NG TIN LIÃŠN Há»† CHÃNH THá»¨C:
- Hotline tÆ° váº¥n ká»¹ thuáº­t: 1900 6868
- Zalo há»— trá»£: 0988 123 456
- Email: hotro@luavang.vn
- Äáº·t mua sáº£n pháº©m táº¡i má»¥c "Sáº£n pháº©m", Ä‘áº·t thuÃª mÃ¡y táº¡i má»¥c "ThuÃª mÃ¡y", Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ há»™i tháº£o táº¡i má»¥c "Sá»± kiá»‡n" trÃªn website.

2. DANH SÃCH Sáº¢N PHáº¨M ÄANG BÃN (Giá»‘ng lÃºa, PhÃ¢n bÃ³n, Thuá»‘c BVTV):
${dsSanPham}

3. Dá»ŠCH Vá»¤ CHO THUÃŠ MÃY NÃ”NG NGHIá»†P:
${dsThueMay}

${dsSuKien}

QUY Táº®C Báº®T BUá»˜C:
- ÄÆ°á»£c phÃ©p sá»­ dá»¥ng kiáº¿n thá»©c chuyÃªn gia nÃ´ng nghiá»‡p Ä‘á»ƒ giáº£i Ä‘Ã¡p má»i tháº¯c máº¯c cá»§a bÃ  con vá» ká»¹ thuáº­t trá»“ng lÃºa, chÄƒm sÃ³c cÃ¢y trá»“ng, thá»i tiáº¿t, sÃ¢u bá»‡nh, thá»• nhÆ°á»¡ng vÃ  Ä‘á»i sá»‘ng nhÃ  nÃ´ng.
- Khi tÆ° váº¥n thuá»‘c BVTV, phÃ¢n bÃ³n, giá»‘ng lÃºa hoáº·c thuÃª mÃ¡y: Æ°u tiÃªn giá»›i thiá»‡u ÄÃšNG tÃªn vÃ  GIÃ BÃN cÃ¡c máº·t hÃ ng Ä‘ang cÃ³ á»Ÿ má»¥c 2 vÃ  má»¥c 3.
- Náº¿u bÃ  con há»i cÃ¡c chá»§ Ä‘á» khÃ¡c ngoÃ i nÃ´ng nghiá»‡p hoáº·c trÃ² chuyá»‡n thÃ´ng thÆ°á»ng, hÃ£y tráº£ lá»i tá»± nhiÃªn, thÃ´ng minh vÃ  thÃ¢n thiá»‡n.
- Chá»‰ khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»± bá»‹a ra tÃªn sáº£n pháº©m, giÃ¡ tiá»n, sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i hoáº·c Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ kho bÃ£i giáº£ cá»§a cá»­a hÃ ng LÃºa VÃ ng. }

async function hoiAI(lichSu, systemPrompt) {
    // 1) Groq
    for (const model of DANH_SACH_MODEL_GROQ) {
        try {
            const completion = await groq.chat.completions.create({
                model,
                messages: [{ role: "system", content: systemPrompt }, ...lichSu]
            });
            const text = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content;
            if (text) return text;
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(`Groq model ${model} lá»—i:`, err.status, err.message);
        }
    }

    // 2) Gemini (náº¿u cÃ³ key)
    if (gemini) {
        try {
            const cauHoiCuoi = lichSu[lichSu.length - 1].content;
            const r = await gemini.models.generateContent({
                model: "gemini-2.5-flash",
                contents: cauHoiCuoi,
                config: { systemInstruction: systemPrompt }
            });
            if (r.text) return r.text;
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Gemini lá»—i:', err.status || '', err.message);
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Táº¥t cáº£ AI Ä‘á»u lá»—i');
}

const layTinNhan = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();
        let result = await pool.request().query(`
            SELECT Id, NguoiGui, HoTen, SoDienThoai, NoiDung,
                   CONVERT(varchar(19), NgayGui, 126) AS NgayGui
            FROM TinNhanTuVan
            ORDER BY Id ASC
        `);
        res.json({ success: true, data: result.recordset });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

const guiTinNhan = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { nguoiGui, hoTen, soDienThoai, noiDung } = req.body;
        let pool = await ketNoiDb();

        await pool.request()
            .input('nguoiGui', sql.VarChar, nguoiGui)
            .input('hoTen', sql.NVarChar, hoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n')
            .input('soDienThoai', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai || 'khach')
            .input('noiDung', sql.NVarChar, noiDung)
            .query(`INSERT INTO TinNhanTuVan (NguoiGui, HoTen, SoDienThoai, NoiDung, NgayGui) 
                    VALUES (@nguoiGui, @hoTen, @soDienThoai, @noiDung, GETDATE())`);

        // Chá»‰ gá»i AI khi nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nháº¯n VÃ€ admin khÃ´ng Ä‘ang má»Ÿ trang QL TÆ° váº¥n
        if (nguoiGui === 'nong-dan' && !adminDangOnline()) {
            let noiDungPhanHoi = '';
            try {
                // Láº¥y Ä‘á»“ng thá»i dá»¯ liá»‡u há»‡ thá»‘ng má»›i nháº¥t vÃ  6 tin nháº¯n gáº§n nháº¥t
                const [systemPrompt, ketQua] = await Promise.all([
                    taoPromptHeThong(pool),
                    pool.request()
                        .input('sdt', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai || 'khach')
                        .query(`SELECT TOP 6 NguoiGui, NoiDung FROM TinNhanTuVan 
                                WHERE SoDienThoai = @sdt ORDER BY NgayGui DESC, Id DESC`)
                ]);

                const lichSu = ketQua.recordset.reverse().map(m => ({
                    role: m.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan' ? 'user' : 'assistant',
                    content: m.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan' ? m.NoiDung : lamSachTinAI(m.NoiDung)
                }));

                const cauTraLoiAI = (await hoiAI(lichSu, systemPrompt)) || "Xin lá»—i bÃ  con, há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘ang báº­n.";
                noiDungPhanHoi = `ðŸ¤– [Trá»£ lÃ½ AI LÃºa VÃ ng]: ${cauTraLoiAI}\n\nâ³ (Há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘Ã£ ghi nháº­n cÃ¢u há»i, Admin sáº½ trá»±c tiáº¿p há»— trá»£ thÃªm cho bÃ  con trong thá»i gian ngáº¯n nháº¥t áº¡!)`;
            } catch (aiErr) {
                console.error('Lá»—i AI:', aiErr);
                noiDungPhanHoi = `ðŸ¤– [Trá»£ lÃ½ AI LÃºa VÃ ng]: Dáº¡ bÃ  con Æ¡i, hiá»‡n táº¡i há»‡ thá»‘ng AI Ä‘ang báº­n má»™t chÃºt, Admin sáº½ trá»±c tiáº¿p giáº£i Ä‘Ã¡p tháº¯c máº¯c cho bÃ  con ngay nhÃ©!`;
            }

            await pool.request()
                .input('nguoiGui', sql.VarChar, 'admin')
                .input('hoTen', sql.NVarChar, hoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n')
                .input('soDienThoai', sql.VarChar, soDienThoai || 'khach')
                .input('noiDung', sql.NVarChar, noiDungPhanHoi)
                .query(`INSERT INTO TinNhanTuVan (NguoiGui, HoTen, SoDienThoai, NoiDung, NgayGui) 
                        VALUES (@nguoiGui, @hoTen, @soDienThoai, @noiDung, GETDATE())`);
        }

        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Gá»­i tin nháº¯n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!' });
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: err.message });
    }
};

    <script>
        let dsDonSP = [];
        let dsDonMay = [];

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function () {
            const rawUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p báº±ng tÃ i khoáº£n Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            if (!sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', rawUser);
            }

            const user = JSON.parse(rawUser);
            if (user.role !== 'admin') {
                await hienThongBao('Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n truy cáº­p trang Quáº£n trá»‹!');
                window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
                return;
            }

            fetch('/admin-header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }
                });

            taiDuLieuAdmin();
        });

        function chuyenTabAdmin(tab) {
            const secSP = document.getElementById('section-don-sp');
            const secMay = document.getElementById('section-don-may');
            const btnSP = document.getElementById('btn-tab-sp');
            const btnMay = document.getElementById('btn-tab-may');

            if (tab === 'san-pham') {
                secSP.classList.remove('hidden');
                secMay.classList.add('hidden');

                btnSP.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
                btnSP.classList.remove('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
                btnSP.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'bg-white/20 text-white text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full';

                btnMay.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
                btnMay.classList.add('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
                btnMay.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full';
            } else {
                secMay.classList.remove('hidden');
                secSP.classList.add('hidden');

                btnMay.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
                btnMay.classList.remove('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
                btnMay.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'bg-white/20 text-white text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full';

                btnSP.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
                btnSP.classList.add('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
                btnSP.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full';
            }
        }

        async function taiDuLieuAdmin() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/don-hang');
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    dsDonSP = result.donSanPham || [];
                    dsDonMay = result.donThueMay || [];
                    capNhatThongKe();
                    renderTatCa();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng:', err);
            }
        }

        function capNhatThongKe() {
            const spDaDat = dsDonSP.filter(x => x.TrangThai !== 'Trong giá»');
            const mayDaDat = dsDonMay.filter(x => x.TrangThai !== 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n');

            const choXuLySP = dsDonSP.filter(x => ['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng', 'Äang giao hÃ ng'].includes(x.TrangThai)).length;
            const choXuLyMay = dsDonMay.filter(x => ['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n'].includes(x.TrangThai)).length;

            let doanhThu = 0;
            dsDonSP.forEach(x => {
                if (x.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') {
                    doanhThu += Number(x.GiaTien) * Number(x.SoLuong);
                } else if (['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang giao hÃ ng'].includes(x.TrangThai)) {
                    doanhThu += Number(x.TienCoc || 0);
                }
            });
            dsDonMay.forEach(x => {
                if (x.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') {
                    doanhThu += Number(x.GiaThue) * Number(x.SoCong);
                } else if (['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch'].includes(x.TrangThai)) {
                    doanhThu += Number(x.TienCoc || 0);
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('stat-so-don-sp').innerText = spDaDat.length;
            document.getElementById('stat-so-don-may').innerText = mayDaDat.length;
            document.getElementById('stat-cho-xu-ly').innerText = choXuLySP + choXuLyMay;
            document.getElementById('stat-doanh-thu').innerText = doanhThu.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
        }

        function locDanhSachChung(ds, loai) {
            const tuKhoa = (document.getElementById('input-tim-kiem').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const boLoc = document.getElementById('select-loc-trang-thai').value;

            return ds.filter(item => {
                if (boLoc === 'DA_DAT') {
                    if (loai === 'SP' && item.TrangThai === 'Trong giá»') return false;
                    if (loai === 'MAY' && item.TrangThai === 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n') return false;
                } else if (boLoc === 'CHO_THANH_TOAN') {
                    if (loai === 'SP' && !['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng', 'Äang giao hÃ ng'].includes(item.TrangThai)) return false;
                    if (loai === 'MAY' && !['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n'].includes(item.TrangThai)) return false;
                } else if (boLoc === 'DA_THANH_TOAN') {
                    if (item.TrangThai !== 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') return false;
                } else if (boLoc === 'DA_HUY') {
                    if (item.TrangThai !== 'ÄÃ£ há»§y') return false;
                }

                if (tuKhoa) {
                    const tenND = (item.HoTen || '').toLowerCase();
                    const sdt = (item.SoDienThoai || '').toLowerCase();
                    const tenHang = (item.TenSanPham || item.TenMay || '').toLowerCase();
                    const maTT = (item.MaThanhToan || '').toLowerCase();
                    if (!tenND.includes(tuKhoa) && !sdt.includes(tuKhoa) && !tenHang.includes(tuKhoa) && !maTT.includes(tuKhoa)) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return true;
            });
        }

        function mauSelectTrangThai(trangThai) {
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300';
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)') return 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border-teal-300';
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ há»§y') return 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 border-red-300';
            if (trangThai === 'Äang giao hÃ ng' || trangThai === 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n') return 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 border-sky-300';
            return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300';
        }

        function renderTatCa() {
            const listSP = locDanhSachChung(dsDonSP, 'SP');
            const listMay = locDanhSachChung(dsDonMay, 'MAY');

            document.getElementById('badge-tab-sp').innerText = listSP.length;
            document.getElementById('badge-tab-may').innerText = listMay.length;

            renderBangSanPham(listSP);
            renderBangThueMay(listMay);
        }

        function renderBangSanPham(ds) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-don-sp');
            if (ds.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">KhÃ´ng cÃ³ Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘áº·t mua sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o phÃ¹ há»£p.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            const cacTrangThaiSP = [
                'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n',
                'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)',
                'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng',
                'Äang giao hÃ ng',
                'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh',
                'ÄÃ£ há»§y',
                'Trong giá»'
            ];

            tbody.innerHTML = ds.map(sp => {
                const thanhTien = Number(sp.GiaTien) * Number(sp.SoLuong);
                const tienCoc = Number(sp.TienCoc || 0);
                const conThu = Math.max(0, thanhTien - tienCoc);
                const thoiGian = sp.NgayThem ? new Date(sp.NgayThem).toLocaleString('vi-VN') : '--';
                const selectCls = mauSelectTrangThai(sp.TrangThai);

                const optionsHtml = cacTrangThaiSP.map(tt =>
                    `<option value="${tt}" ${sp.TrangThai === tt ? 'selected' : ''}>${tt}</option>`
                ).join('');

                const cocBadgeHtml = tienCoc > 0
                    ? `<span class="block text-[11px] text-teal-700 font-semibold mt-0.5">Cá»c 30%: ${tienCoc.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</span>
                       <span class="block text-[11px] text-gray-500">CÃ²n thu: ${conThu.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</span>`
                    : '';

                return `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/80 transition">
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 font-bold text-luaGreen">
                            #SP${sp.MaGH}
                            ${sp.MaThanhToan ? `<span class="block text-[11px] text-amber-700 font-extrabold">${sp.MaThanhToan}</span>` : ''}
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <p class="font-bold text-gray-800">${sp.HoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n'}</p>
                            <p class="text-xs text-luaGreen font-semibold">${sp.SoDienThoai}</p>
                            <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">${sp.DiaChi || 'ChÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰'}</p>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <span class="text-[11px] font-semibold bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${sp.DanhMuc}</span>
                            <p class="font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1">${sp.TenSanPham}</p>
                            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">${Number(sp.GiaTien).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ / ${sp.DonViTinh}</p>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center font-bold">${sp.SoLuong}</td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                            <span class="font-black text-luaYellow">${thanhTien.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</span>
                            ${cocBadgeHtml}
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-xs text-gray-500">${thoiGian}</td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <select onchange="doiTrangThaiSP(${sp.MaGH}, this.value)" class="text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer focus:outline-none ${selectCls}">
                                ${optionsHtml}
                            </select>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                            <button onclick="xoaDonSP(${sp.MaGH})" class="text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg transition" title="XÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i>
                            </button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function renderBangThueMay(ds) {

                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-xs space-y-1">
                            <p class="text-gray-700"><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-days text-luaGreen mr-1"></i> NgÃ y thuÃª: <strong>${m.NgayThue || 'ChÆ°a chá»n ngÃ y'}</strong></p>
                            <p class="text-luaGreen font-semibold"><i class="fa-regular fa-clock text-luaGreen mr-1"></i> Ca thuÃª: <strong>${m.KhungGio || 'ChÆ°a chá»n ca'}</strong></p>
                            <p class="text-gray-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot text-amber-600 mr-1"></i> ${m.GhiChu || 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ ghi chÃº'}</p>

        async function doiTrangThaiSP(maGH, trangThaiMoi) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/don-hang/san-pham', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ maGH, trangThai: trangThaiMoi })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    taiDuLieuAdmin();
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    taiDuLieuAdmin();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i server!');
            }
        }

        async function doiTrangThaiMay(maDatMay, trangThaiMoi) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/don-hang/thue-may', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ maDatMay, trangThai: trangThaiMoi })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    taiDuLieuAdmin();
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    taiDuLieuAdmin();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i server!');
            }
        }

        async function xoaDonSP(maGH) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('XÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n sáº£n pháº©m nÃ y khá»i há»‡ thá»‘ng?')) return;
            await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/san-pham/${maGH}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            taiDuLieuAdmin();
        }

        async function xoaDonMay(maDatMay) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('XÃ³a phiáº¿u thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ y khá»i há»‡ thá»‘ng?')) return;
            await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/thue-may/${maDatMay}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            taiDuLieuAdmin();
        }
    </script>

<script>
    window.capNhatAdminHeader = function() {
        const loggedUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
        if (loggedUser && !sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
            sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', loggedUser);
        }

        const nameDisplay = document.getElementById('admin-name-display');

        if (loggedUser && nameDisplay) {
            try {
                const user = JSON.parse(loggedUser);
                nameDisplay.innerText = user.name || 'Admin';
            } catch(e) {}
        }

        const currentPath = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop();
        const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-admin-link');

        navLinks.forEach(link => {
            const target = link.getAttribute('data-target');
            if (target === currentPath || (currentPath === '' && target === 'admin-trang-chu.html')) {
                link.classList.remove('hover:text-luaGreen', 'text-gray-600');
                link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
            } else {
                link.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                link.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
            }
        });
    };

    window.dangXuatAdmin = function() {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('luaVangUser');
        localStorage.removeItem('luaVangUser');
        window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
    };

    window.capNhatAdminHeader();
</script>

    <script>
        let danhSachNongDan = [];

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function () {
            const rawUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p báº±ng tÃ i khoáº£n Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            const user = JSON.parse(rawUser);
            if (user.role !== 'admin') {
                await hienThongBao('Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n truy cáº­p trang Quáº£n trá»‹!');
                window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
                return;
            }

            fetch('/admin-header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }
                });

            taiDanhSachNongDan();
        });

        async function taiDanhSachNongDan() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/nong-dan');
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    danhSachNongDan = result.data || [];
                    capNhatKPI();
                    locDanhSachNongDan();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i danh sÃ¡ch nÃ´ng dÃ¢n:', err);
            }
        }

        function capNhatKPI() {
            const tongND = danhSachNongDan.length;
            const coDiaChi = danhSachNongDan.filter(x => (x.DiaChi || '').trim() !== '').length;
            const khachMua = danhSachNongDan.filter(x => Number(x.SoDonSP || 0) > 0 || Number(x.SoLichMay || 0) > 0).length;
            const tongChiTieu = danhSachNongDan.reduce((sum, x) => sum + Number(x.TongChiTieu || 0), 0);

            document.getElementById('kpi-tong-nd').innerText = tongND;
            document.getElementById('kpi-co-dia-chi').innerText = `${coDiaChi} / ${tongND}`;
            document.getElementById('kpi-khach-mua').innerText = khachMua;
            document.getElementById('kpi-tong-chi-tieu').innerText = tongChiTieu.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
        }

        function locDanhSachNongDan() {
            const tuKhoa = document.getElementById('input-tim-nong-dan').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            const kieuLoc = document.getElementById('select-loc-nong-dan').value;

            const ketQua = danhSachNongDan.filter(nd => {
                const khopTuKhoa = !tuKhoa ||
                    (nd.HoTen || '').toLowerCase().includes(tuKhoa) ||
                    (nd.SoDienThoai || '').toLowerCase().includes(tuKhoa) ||
                    (nd.DiaChi || '').toLowerCase().includes(tuKhoa);

                if (!khopTuKhoa) return false;

                if (kieuLoc === 'co-chi-tieu') return Number(nd.TongChiTieu || 0) > 0;
                if (kieuLoc === 'chua-chi-tieu') return Number(nd.TongChiTieu || 0) === 0;
                if (kieuLoc === 'thieu-dia-chi') return !(nd.DiaChi || '').trim();
                return true;
            });

            renderBangNongDan(ketQua);
        }

        function renderBangNongDan(ds) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-nong-dan');
            if (ds.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center py-10 text-gray-400">KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nÃ o phÃ¹ há»£p.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = ds.map(nd => {
                const diaChiHtml = (nd.DiaChi || '').trim()
                    ? `<span class="text-gray-700 font-medium"><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot text-emerald-600 mr-1"></i>${nd.DiaChi}</span>`
                    : `<span class="text-gray-400 italic">ChÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng</span>`;

                const tenSafe = String(nd.HoTen || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
                const diaChiSafe = String(nd.DiaChi || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/\n/g, ' ');

                return `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/80 transition">
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">${nd.HoTen || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n'}</p>
                            <p class="text-luaGreen font-semibold mt-0.5"><i class="fa-solid fa-phone text-[10px] mr-1"></i>${nd.SoDienThoai}</p>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 max-w-xs">${diaChiHtml}</td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
                            <span class="bg-emerald-50 text-luaGreen font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${nd.SoDonSP} Ä‘Æ¡n</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
                            <span class="bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${nd.SoLichMay} lá»‹ch</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
                            <span class="bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${nd.SoSuKien} SK</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                            <p class="font-black text-luaYellow text-sm">${Number(nd.TongChiTieu || 0).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</p>
                            <span class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">Tá»•ng Ä‘Æ¡n: ${Number(nd.TongGiaTriDon || 0).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3.5 px-4">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">

        function moModalSuaND(sdt, ten, diaChi) {
            document.getElementById('edit-nd-sdt').value = sdt;
            document.getElementById('edit-nd-ten').value = ten;
            document.getElementById('edit-nd-diachi').value = diaChi;

        async function luuSuaNongDan(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const soDienThoai = document.getElementById('edit-nd-sdt').value;
            const hoTen = document.getElementById('edit-nd-ten').value.trim();
            const diaChi = document.getElementById('edit-nd-diachi').value.trim();

            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/nong-dan/cap-nhat', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ soDienThoai, hoTen, diaChi })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                dongModalSuaND();
                taiDanhSachNongDan();
            } else {
                alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
            }
        }

        function moModalResetMK(sdt, ten) {

        function dongModalResetMK() {

        }

        async function xacNhanResetMK(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const soDienThoai = document.getElementById('reset-nd-sdt').value;
            const matKhauMoi = document.getElementById('reset-nd-mk').value.trim();

            const res = await fetch('/api/admin/nong-dan/dat-lai-mat-khau', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ soDienThoai, matKhauMoi })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                alert(`âœ… ${result.message}\nMáº­t kháº©u má»›i lÃ : ${matKhauMoi}`);
                dongModalResetMK();
            } else {
                alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
            }
        }

        async function xoaTaiKhoanND(sdt, ten) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao(`Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n "${ten}" (${sdt})?`)) return;
            const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/nong-dan/${encodeURIComponent(sdt)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                alert('âœ… ' + result.message);
                taiDanhSachNongDan();
            } else {
                alert('âš ï¸ ' + result.message);
            }
        }

        let danhSachSPGoc = [];

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', taiDanhSachSanPham);

        async function taiDanhSachSanPham() {
            try {
                let response = await fetch('/api/san-pham');
                let result = await response.json();
                
                if (result.success) {
                    danhSachSPGoc = result.data;
                    const tbody = document.getElementById('bang-san-pham');
                    tbody.innerHTML = '';

                    if (result.data.length === 0) {
                        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-6 text-center text-gray-400">ChÆ°a cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u.</td></tr>`;
                        return;
                    }

                    result.data.forEach(sp => {
                        let iconClass = 'fa-bottle-droplet text-luaGreen bg-emerald-50';
                        if (sp.DanhMuc === 'PhÃ¢n') iconClass = 'fa-sack-dollar text-amber-700 bg-amber-50';
                        if (sp.DanhMuc === 'Giá»‘ng') iconClass = 'fa-seedling text-luaGreen bg-emerald-50';

                        let giaFormatted = Number(sp.GiaTien).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘ / ' + (sp.DonViTinh ? sp.DonViTinh.toLowerCase() : 'chai');

                        let row = `
                            <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/50 transition">
                                <td class="p-4 font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center space-x-3">
                                    <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg ${iconClass} flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                                        <i class="fa-solid ${iconClass.split(' ')[0]}"></i>
                                    </div>
                                    <span>${sp.TenSanPham}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td class="p-4 text-gray-600">${sp.DanhMuc}</td>
                                <td class="p-4 font-bold text-luaYellow">${giaFormatted}</td>
                                <td class="p-4"><span class="bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${sp.TrangThai || 'Äang bÃ¡n'}</span></td>
                                <td class="p-4 text-center space-x-2">
                                    <button onclick="moModalSua(${sp.MaSanPham})" class="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 p-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i></button>
                                    <button onclick="xoaSanPham(${sp.MaSanPham})" class="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 p-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        `;
                        tbody.innerHTML += row;
                    });
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i khi táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u sáº£n pháº©m:', err);
            }
        }

        // Má»Ÿ Modal ThÃªm má»›i
        function moModalThem() {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'ThÃªm sáº£n pháº©m má»›i';
            document.getElementById('modal-icon').className = 'fa-solid fa-circle-plus text-xl';
            document.getElementById('form-them-san-pham').reset();
            document.getElementById('maSanPham').value = '';
            capNhatNhanGia();
            document.getElementById('modal-them-san-pham').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        // Má»Ÿ Modal Sá»­a sáº£n pháº©m (Äiá»n sáºµn thÃ´ng tin cÅ©)
        function moModalSua(id) {
            const sp = danhSachSPGoc.find(item => item.MaSanPham === id);
            if (!sp) return;

            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Chá»‰nh sá»­a sáº£n pháº©m';
            document.getElementById('modal-icon').className = 'fa-solid fa-pen-to-square text-xl';
            
            document.getElementById('maSanPham').value = sp.MaSanPham;
            document.getElementById('tenSanPham').value = sp.TenSanPham;
            document.getElementById('danhMuc').value = sp.DanhMuc;
            document.getElementById('giaTien').value = sp.GiaTien;
            document.getElementById('hinhAnh').value = sp.HinhAnh || '';
            document.getElementById('moTa').value = sp.MoTa || '';

            capNhatNhanGia();
            document.getElementById('modal-them-san-pham').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function dongModalThem() {
            document.getElementById('modal-them-san-pham').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('form-them-san-pham').reset();
        }

        function capNhatNhanGia() {
            const danhMuc = document.getElementById('danhMuc').value;
            const labelGia = document.getElementById('label-gia-tien');
            if (danhMuc === 'Thuá»‘c') {
                labelGia.innerText = 'GiÃ¡ tiá»n (VNÄ / chai)';
            } else if (danhMuc === 'PhÃ¢n') {
                labelGia.innerText = 'GiÃ¡ tiá»n (VNÄ / bao)';
            } else if (danhMuc === 'Giá»‘ng') {
                labelGia.innerText = 'GiÃ¡ tiá»n (VNÄ / bao)';
            }
        }

        // Xá»­ lÃ½ submit chung cho cáº£ ThÃªm vÃ  Sá»­a
        async function guiFormSanPham(e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            const maSanPham = document.getElementById('maSanPham').value;
            const data = {
                tenSanPham: document.getElementById('tenSanPham').value,
                danhMuc: document.getElementById('danhMuc').value,
                giaTien: document.getElementById('giaTien').value,
                hinhAnh: document.getElementById('hinhAnh').value,
                moTa: document.getElementById('moTa').value
            };

            let url = '/api/them-san-pham';
            let method = 'POST';

            if (maSanPham) {
                url = '/api/sua-san-pham';
                method = 'PUT';
                data.maSanPham = maSanPham;
            }

            try {
                let response = await fetch(url, {
                    method: method,
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify(data)
                });

                let result = await response.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert(result.message);
                    dongModalThem();
                    taiDanhSachSanPham();
                } else {
                    alert('Lá»—i: ' + result.message);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                alert('KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n server!');
            }
        }

        // XÃ³a sáº£n pháº©m
        async function xoaSanPham(id) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a sáº£n pháº©m nÃ y khÃ´ng?')) return;

            try {
                let response = await fetch(`/api/xoa-san-pham/${id}`, {
                    method: 'DELETE'
                });

                let result = await response.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert(result.message);
                    taiDanhSachSanPham();
                } else {
                    alert('Lá»—i: ' + result.message);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                alert('KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n server!');
            }
        }
    </script>

        async function taiTatCaDuLieu() {
            try {
                const [resSuKien, resDangKy] = await Promise.all([
                    fetch('/api/su-kien'),
                    fetch('/api/danh-sach-dang-ky')
                ]);

                const dataSuKien = await resSuKien.json();
                const dataDangKy = await resDangKy.json();

                if (dataSuKien.success) danhSachSuKienGoc = dataSuKien.data;
                if (dataDangKy.success) danhSachNongDanGoc = dataDangKy.data;

                renderGiaoDienSuKien();
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u:", err);
            }
        }

        function renderGiaoDienSuKien() {
            const container = document.getElementById('danh-sach-su-kien-container');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            if (danhSachSuKienGoc.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `

                        ChÆ°a cÃ³ sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ o Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o. HÃ£y báº¥m "Táº¡o sá»± kiá»‡n má»›i" á»Ÿ gÃ³c trÃªn.
                    </div>

                return;

            danhSachSuKienGoc.forEach(sk => {
                const nongDanCuaSuKien = danhSachNongDanGoc.filter(
                    nd => nd.TieuDeSuKien && nd.TieuDeSuKien.trim() === sk.TieuDe.trim()
                );

                if (nongDanCuaSuKien.length === 0) {
                    bangNongDanHtml = `
                        <div class="py-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-400 italic bg-gray-50/50 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-200">
                            ChÆ°a cÃ³ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nÃ o Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tham gia sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ y.
                        </div>
                    `;
                } else {
                    let rowsHtml = nongDanCuaSuKien.map((nd, idx) => {
                        let ngayDK = nd.NgayDangKy ? new Date(nd.NgayDangKy).toLocaleString('vi-VN') : '';
                        return `

                                        <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        `;

                    bangNongDanHtml = `

                            <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
                                <thead>

                                        <th class="py-2.5 px-4">Thá»i gian Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½</th>

                container.innerHTML += `

                                <button onclick="xoaSuKien(${sk.MaSuKien})" class="bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 p-2.5 rounded-xl transition" title="XÃ³a sá»± kiá»‡n">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                                    Danh sÃ¡ch nÃ´ng dÃ¢n tham gia (${nongDanCuaSuKien.length})
                                </h3>

        // HÃ m xÃ³a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ sá»± kiá»‡n theo MaDK
        async function xoaDangKySuKien(maDK) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao("Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nÃ y ra khá»i sá»± kiá»‡n khÃ´ng?")) {
                return;
            }

            try {
                let res = await fetch(`/api/dang-ky-su-kien/${maDK}`, {
                    method: 'DELETE'
                });
                let result = await res.json();
                
                if (result.success) {
                    alert("ÄÃ£ xÃ³a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!");
                    taiTatCaDuLieu(); // Load láº¡i toÃ n bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘á»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t báº£ng vÃ  STT
                } else {
                    alert("Lá»—i khi xÃ³a: " + result.message);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i:", err);
                alert("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§.");
            }
        }

        function toggleDanhSach(maSuKien) {
            const listEl = document.getElementById(`list-nongdan-${maSuKien}`);
            const textEl = document.getElementById(`text-toggle-${maSuKien}`);
            const iconEl = document.getElementById(`icon-toggle-${maSuKien}`);

            if (listEl.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                listEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                textEl.innerText = 'Thu gá»n';
                iconEl.classList.replace('fa-chevron-down', 'fa-chevron-up');
            } else {
                listEl.classList.add('hidden');
                textEl.innerText = 'Xem danh sÃ¡ch';
                iconEl.classList.replace('fa-chevron-up', 'fa-chevron-down');
            }
        }

        function xuatCSVRieng(tieuDeSuKien) {
            const dsLoc = danhSachNongDanGoc.filter(
                nd => nd.TieuDeSuKien && nd.TieuDeSuKien.trim() === tieuDeSuKien.trim()
            );

            if (dsLoc.length === 0) {
                alert(`Sá»± kiá»‡n "${tieuDeSuKien}" chÆ°a cÃ³ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nÃ o Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ Ä‘á»ƒ xuáº¥t!`);
                return;
            }

            let csvContent = "\uFEFFSTT,Há» TÃªn NÃ´ng DÃ¢n,Sá»‘ Äiá»‡n Thoáº¡i,Sá»± Kiá»‡n Tham Gia,Thá»i Gian ÄÄƒng KÃ½\n";
            dsLoc.forEach((item, index) => {
                let ngayDK = item.NgayDangKy ? new Date(item.NgayDangKy).toLocaleString('vi-VN').replace(',', '') : '';
                let row = [
                    index + 1,
                    `"${item.HoTen}"`,
                    `"${item.SoDienThoai}"`,
                    `"${item.TieuDeSuKien}"`,
                    `"${ngayDK}"`
                ].join(",");
                csvContent += row + "\n";
            });

            const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
            const link = document.createElement("a");
            link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const safeName = tieuDeSuKien.substring(0, 30).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\u00C0-\u1EF9]/g, '_');
            link.download = `DanhSach_NongDan_${safeName}.csv`;
            link.click();
        }

        function moModalThem() {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = "Táº¡o Sá»± Kiá»‡n Má»›i";
            document.getElementById('formSuKien').reset();
            document.getElementById('maSuKien').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.add('flex');
        }

        function moModalSua(id) {
            let item = danhSachSuKienGoc.find(s => s.MaSuKien === id);
            if(!item) return;
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = "Cáº­p Nháº­t Sá»± Kiá»‡n";
            document.getElementById('maSuKien').value = item.MaSuKien;
            document.getElementById('tieuDe').value = item.TieuDe;
            document.getElementById('thoiGian').value = item.ThoiGian;
            document.getElementById('diaDiem').value = item.DiaDiem;
            document.getElementById('moTa').value = item.MoTa || '';
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.add('flex');
        }

        function dongModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.remove('flex');
            document.getElementById('modalSuKien').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        async function luuSuKien(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            let id = document.getElementById('maSuKien').value;
            let data = {
                tieuDe: document.getElementById('tieuDe').value,
                thoiGian: document.getElementById('thoiGian').value,
                diaDiem: document.getElementById('diaDiem').value,
                moTa: document.getElementById('moTa').value
            };

            let url = '/api/them-su-kien';
            let method = 'POST';
            if(id) {
                url = '/api/sua-su-kien';
                method = 'PUT';
                data.maSuKien = parseInt(id);
            }

            let res = await fetch(url, {
                method: method,
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            let result = await res.json();
            if(result.success) {
                alert(result.message);
                dongModal();
                taiTatCaDuLieu();
            } else {
                alert('Lá»—i: ' + result.error);
            }
        }

        async function xoaSuKien(id) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao("Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c muá»‘n xÃ³a sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ y?")) return;
            let res = await fetch(`/api/xoa-su-kien/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            let result = await res.json();
            if(result.success) {
                alert(result.message);
                taiTatCaDuLieu();
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', taiTatCaDuLieu);
    </script>
</body>

        function hienThiBang(data) {

            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            data.forEach(item => {
                let badgeClass = item.TrangThai === 'Sáºµn sÃ ng' 
                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen' 
                    : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';

                tbody.innerHTML += `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/50 transition">
                        <td class="p-4 font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center space-x-3">

                        <td class="p-4"><span class="${badgeClass} text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${item.TrangThai}</span></td>
                        <td class="p-4 text-center space-x-2">
                            <button onclick="moModalSua(${item.MaMay})" class="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 p-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i></button>
                            <button onclick="xoaThueMay(${item.MaMay})" class="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 p-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });
        }

        function moModalThem() {
            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = "ThÃªm Loáº¡i MÃ¡y Má»›i";
            document.getElementById('formThueMay').reset();
            document.getElementById('maMay').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.add('flex');
        }

        function moModalSua(id) {
            let item = danhSachThueMayGoc.find(m => m.MaMay === id);
            if(!item) return;

            document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = "Cáº­p Nháº­t Thiáº¿t Bá»‹ ThuÃª";
            document.getElementById('maMay').value = item.MaMay;
            document.getElementById('tenMay').value = item.TenMay;
            document.getElementById('loaiMay').value = item.LoaiMay;
            document.getElementById('giaThue').value = item.GiaThue;
            document.getElementById('donViTinh').value = item.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng';
            document.getElementById('trangThai').value = item.TrangThai || 'Sáºµn sÃ ng';
            document.getElementById('hinhAnh').value = item.HinhAnh || '';
            document.getElementById('moTa').value = item.MoTa || '';

            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.add('flex');
        }

        function dongModal() {
            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.remove('flex');
            document.getElementById('modalThueMay').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        async function luuThueMay(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            let maMay = document.getElementById('maMay').value;
            let data = {
                tenMay: document.getElementById('tenMay').value,
                loaiMay: document.getElementById('loaiMay').value,
                giaThue: parseFloat(document.getElementById('giaThue').value),
                donViTinh: document.getElementById('donViTinh').value,
                trangThai: document.getElementById('trangThai').value,
                hinhAnh: document.getElementById('hinhAnh').value,
                moTa: document.getElementById('moTa').value
            };

            let url = '/api/them-thue-may';
            let method = 'POST';

            if(maMay) {
                url = '/api/sua-thue-may';
                method = 'PUT';
                data.maMay = parseInt(maMay);
            }

            try {
                let res = await fetch(url, {
                    method: method,
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify(data)
                });
                let result = await res.json();
                if(result.success) {
                    alert(result.message);
                    dongModal();
                    taiDanhSachThueMay();
                } else {
                    alert('Lá»—i: ' + result.error);
                }
            } catch(err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i lÆ°u dá»¯ liá»‡u:", err);
            }
        }

        async function xoaThueMay(id) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao("Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a thiáº¿t bá»‹ nÃ y khÃ´ng?")) return;
            try {
                let res = await fetch(`/api/xoa-thue-may/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                let result = await res.json();
                if(result.success) {
                    alert(result.message);
                    taiDanhSachThueMay();
                } else {
                    alert('Lá»—i: ' + result.error);
                }
            } catch(err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i xÃ³a dá»¯ liá»‡u:", err);
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', taiDanhSachThueMay);
    </script>
</body>
