            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p báº±ng tÃ i khoáº£n Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            if (!sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', rawUser);
            }

            const user = JSON.parse(rawUser);
            if (user.role !== 'admin') {
                await hienThongBao('Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n truy cáº­p trang Quáº£n trá»‹!');
                window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
                return;
            }

            fetch('/admin-header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }
                    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(link => {
                        if (window.location.pathname.includes(link.getAttribute('data-target'))) {
                            link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                            link.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        }
                    });
                });

            khoiTaoBoLocThoiGian();
            taiDuLieuThongKe();
        });

        function khoiTaoBoLocThoiGian() {
            const now = new Date();
            const namHienTai = now.getFullYear();
            const thangHienTai = now.getMonth() + 1;
            const ngayHomNay = `${namHienTai}-${String(thangHienTai).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

            document.getElementById('input-filter-ngay').value = ngayHomNay;

            const selThang = document.getElementById('select-filter-thang');
            selThang.innerHTML = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => {
                const m = i + 1;
                return `<option value="${m}" ${m === thangHienTai ? 'selected' : ''}>ThÃ¡ng ${m}</option>`;
            }).join('');

            const dsNam = [];
            for (let y = namHienTai - 3; y <= namHienTai + 1; y++) dsNam.push(y);

            const optionsNam = dsNam.map(y =>
                `<option value="${y}" ${y === namHienTai ? 'selected' : ''}>NÄƒm ${y}</option>`
            ).join('');

            document.getElementById('select-filter-nam-thang').innerHTML = optionsNam;
            document.getElementById('select-filter-nam').innerHTML = optionsNam;
        }

        function doiCheDoLoc(mode) {
            cheDoLoc = mode;

            document.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'shadow-xs');
                btn.classList.add('text-gray-600');
            });
            const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-mode-${mode}`);
            if (activeBtn) {
                activeBtn.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'shadow-xs');
                activeBtn.classList.remove('text-gray-600');
            }

            document.getElementById('wrap-filter-ngay').classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== 'ngay');
            document.getElementById('wrap-filter-thang').classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== 'thang');
            document.getElementById('wrap-filter-nam').classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== 'nam');

            // Äá»•i nhÃ£n nÃºt xuáº¥t bÃ¡o cÃ¡o tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng vá»›i cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Ä‘ang chá»n
            const txtBtn = document.getElementById('txt-btn-xuat-bao-cao');
            if (txtBtn) {
                if (mode === 'ngay') txtBtn.innerText = 'Xuáº¥t bÃ¡o cÃ¡o ngÃ y';
                else if (mode === 'thang') txtBtn.innerText = 'Xuáº¥t bÃ¡o cÃ¡o thÃ¡ng';
                else if (mode === 'nam') txtBtn.innerText = 'Xuáº¥t bÃ¡o cÃ¡o nÄƒm';
                else txtBtn.innerText = 'Xuáº¥t bÃ¡o cÃ¡o tá»•ng há»£p';
            }

            capNhatThongKe();
        }

        async function taiDuLieuThongKe() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/thong-ke');
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    rawData = result;
                    capNhatThongKe();
                } else {
                    alert('KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u thá»‘ng kÃª: ' + (result.message || ''));
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i thá»‘ng kÃª:', err);
            }
        }

        function thuocKyLoc(dateStr) {
            if (cheDoLoc === 'tat-ca') return true;
            if (!dateStr) return false;

            const d = new Date(dateStr);
            if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;

            const y = d.getFullYear();
            const m = d.getMonth() + 1;
            const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
            const monthStr = String(m).padStart(2, '0');
            const isoDay = `${y}-${monthStr}-${day}`;

            if (cheDoLoc === 'ngay') {
                return isoDay === document.getElementById('input-filter-ngay').value;
            }
            if (cheDoLoc === 'thang') {
                const targetM = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-filter-thang').value);
                const targetY = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-filter-nam-thang').value);
                return m === targetM && y === targetY;
            }
            if (cheDoLoc === 'nam') {
                const targetY = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-filter-nam').value);
                return y === targetY;
            }
            return true;
        }

        function tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp) {
            const thanhTien = Number(sp.GiaTien || 0) * Number(sp.SoLuong || 0);
            if (sp.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') return thanhTien;
            if (['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang giao hÃ ng'].includes(sp.TrangThai)) return Number(sp.TienCoc || 0);
            return 0;
        }

        function tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(m) {
            const thanhTien = Number(m.GiaThue || 0) * Number(m.SoCong || 0);
            if (m.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') return thanhTien;
            if (['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch'].includes(m.TrangThai)) return Number(m.TienCoc || 0);
            return 0;
        }

        function capNhatThongKe() {
            const moTaEl = document.getElementById('mo-ta-ky-thong-ke');
            let tieuDeBaoCao = '';
            let tenFile = '';

            if (cheDoLoc === 'ngay') {
                const val = document.getElementById('input-filter-ngay').value;
                const ngayDep = val.split('-').reverse().join('/');
                const ngayFile = val.split('-').reverse().join('-');
                moTaEl.innerText = `Sá»‘ liá»‡u thá»‘ng kÃª trong ngÃ y ${ngayDep}`;
                tieuDeBaoCao = `BÃO CÃO THá»NG KÃŠ HOáº T Äá»˜NG NGÃ€Y ${ngayDep}`;
                tenFile = `Bao_Cao_Ngay_${ngayFile}.xlsx`;
            } else if (cheDoLoc === 'thang') {
                const m = String(document.getElementById('select-filter-thang').value).padStart(2, '0');
                const y = document.getElementById('select-filter-nam-thang').value;
                moTaEl.innerText = `Sá»‘ liá»‡u thá»‘ng kÃª trong ThÃ¡ng ${parseInt(m)}/${y}`;
                tieuDeBaoCao = `BÃO CÃO THá»NG KÃŠ HOáº T Äá»˜NG THÃNG ${m}/${y}`;
                tenFile = `Bao_Cao_Thang_${m}-${y}.xlsx`;
            } else if (cheDoLoc === 'nam') {
                const y = document.getElementById('select-filter-nam').value;
                moTaEl.innerText = `Tá»•ng há»£p hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng kinh doanh cáº£ NÄƒm ${y}`;
                tieuDeBaoCao = `BÃO CÃO THá»NG KÃŠ HOáº T Äá»˜NG NÄ‚M ${y}`;
                tenFile = `Bao_Cao_Nam_${y}.xlsx`;
            } else {
                moTaEl.innerText = `Tá»•ng há»£p toÃ n bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u tá»« trÆ°á»›c Ä‘áº¿n nay trÃªn há»‡ thá»‘ng LÃºa VÃ ng`;
                tieuDeBaoCao = `BÃO CÃO THá»NG KÃŠ Tá»”NG Há»¢P TOÃ€N THá»œI GIAN`;
                tenFile = `Bao_Cao_Tong_Hop_Tat_Ca.xlsx`;
            }

            const donSPLoc = (rawData.donSanPham || []).filter(
                x => !['Trong giá»', 'ÄÃ£ há»§y'].includes(x.TrangThai) && thuocKyLoc(x.NgayThem)
            );
            const donMayLoc = (rawData.donThueMay || []).filter(
                x => !['Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n', 'ÄÃ£ há»§y'].includes(x.TrangThai) && thuocKyLoc(x.NgayTao || x.NgayThue)
            );
            const dangKySKLoc = (rawData.dangKySuKien || []).filter(
                x => thuocKyLoc(x.NgayDangKy)
            );

            let dtSP = 0, tongGiaTriSP = 0, tongSLSP = 0, spHoanTat = 0;
            donSPLoc.forEach(sp => {
                dtSP += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp);
                tongGiaTriSP += Number(sp.GiaTien || 0) * Number(sp.SoLuong || 0);
                tongSLSP += Number(sp.SoLuong || 0);
                if (sp.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') spHoanTat++;
            });

            let dtMay = 0, tongGiaTriMay = 0, tongCongMay = 0, mayDangChay = 0;
            donMayLoc.forEach(m => {
                dtMay += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(m);
                tongGiaTriMay += Number(m.GiaThue || 0) * Number(m.SoCong || 0);
                tongCongMay += Number(m.SoCong || 0);
                if (['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n'].includes(m.TrangThai)) mayDangChay++;
            });

            const tongDoanhThuNhan = dtSP + dtMay;
            const tongGiaTriPhatSinh = tongGiaTriSP + tongGiaTriMay;
            const conPhaiThu = Math.max(0, tongGiaTriPhatSinh - tongDoanhThuNhan);

            document.getElementById('val-kpi-doanh-thu').innerText = tongDoanhThuNhan.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('sub-dt-sp').innerText = dtSP.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('sub-dt-may').innerText = dtMay.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';

            document.getElementById('val-kpi-san-pham').innerText = `${donSPLoc.length} Ä‘Æ¡n`;
            document.getElementById('sub-sp-sl').innerText = tongSLSP.toLocaleString('vi-VN');
            document.getElementById('sub-sp-hoan-tat').innerText = `${spHoanTat} Ä‘Æ¡n`;

            document.getElementById('val-kpi-thue-may').innerText = `${donMayLoc.length} lá»‹ch`;
            document.getElementById('sub-may-cong').innerText = `${tongCongMay.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} cÃ´ng`;
            document.getElementById('sub-may-chay').innerText = `${mayDangChay} lá»‹ch`;

            document.getElementById('val-kpi-su-kien').innerText = `${dangKySKLoc.length} lÆ°á»£t ÄK`;
            document.getElementById('sub-sk-tong').innerText = `${(rawData.danhSachSuKien || []).length} chÆ°Æ¡ng trÃ¬nh`;
            document.getElementById('sub-nong-dan').innerText = `${rawData.tongNongDan || 0} TV`;

            document.getElementById('val-tong-gia-tri-don').innerText = tongGiaTriPhatSinh.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('box-da-thu').innerText = tongDoanhThuNhan.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('box-con-thu').innerText = conPhaiThu.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';

            const tongHopSP = renderBangChiTietSanPham(donSPLoc);
            const tongHopMay = renderBangChiTietThueMay(donMayLoc);
            const tongHopSuKien = renderBangChiTietSuKien(dangKySKLoc);

            duLieuHienTaiDeXuat = {
                tieuDeBaoCao,
                tenFile,
                donSPLoc,
                donMayLoc,
                dangKySKLoc,
                tongHopSP,
                tongHopMay,
                tongHopSuKien,
                kpi: {
                    dtSP,
                    dtMay,
                    tongDoanhThuNhan,
                    tongGiaTriPhatSinh,
                    conPhaiThu,
                    soDonSP: donSPLoc.length,
                    tongSLSP,
                    spHoanTat,
                    soLichMay: donMayLoc.length,
                    tongCongMay,
                    mayDangChay,
                    soLuotDKSuKien: dangKySKLoc.length,
                    tongSuKien: (rawData.danhSachSuKien || []).length,
                    tongNongDan: rawData.tongNongDan || 0
                }
            };

            veBieuDoDoanhThu(donSPLoc, donMayLoc);
            veBieuDoCoCau(dtSP, dtMay);
        }

        function veBieuDoDoanhThu(donSPLoc, donMayLoc) {
            let labels = [];
            let dataSP = [];
            let dataMay = [];

            if (cheDoLoc === 'ngay') {
                labels = ['00h - 06h', '06h - 12h', '12h - 18h', '18h - 24h'];
                dataSP = [0, 0, 0, 0];
                dataMay = [0, 0, 0, 0];

                donSPLoc.forEach(sp => {
                    const h = new Date(sp.NgayThem).getHours();
                    const idx = Math.min(3, Math.floor((isNaN(h) ? 8 : h) / 6));
                    dataSP[idx] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp);
                });
                donMayLoc.forEach(m => {
                    const h = new Date(m.NgayTao || m.NgayThue).getHours();
                    const idx = Math.min(3, Math.floor((isNaN(h) ? 8 : h) / 6));
                    dataMay[idx] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(m);
                });
            } else if (cheDoLoc === 'thang') {
                const m = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-filter-thang').value);
                const y = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-filter-nam-thang').value);
                const soNgayTrongThang = new Date(y, m, 0).getDate();

                labels = Array.from({ length: soNgayTrongThang }, (_, i) => `${i + 1}/${m}`);
                dataSP = new Array(soNgayTrongThang).fill(0);
                dataMay = new Array(soNgayTrongThang).fill(0);

                donSPLoc.forEach(sp => {
                    const d = new Date(sp.NgayThem).getDate();
                    if (d >= 1 && d <= soNgayTrongThang) dataSP[d - 1] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp);
                });
                donMayLoc.forEach(mItem => {
                    const d = new Date(mItem.NgayTao || mItem.NgayThue).getDate();
                    if (d >= 1 && d <= soNgayTrongThang) dataMay[d - 1] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(mItem);
                });
            } else if (cheDoLoc === 'nam') {
                labels = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `ThÃ¡ng ${i + 1}`);
                dataSP = new Array(12).fill(0);
                dataMay = new Array(12).fill(0);

                donSPLoc.forEach(sp => {
                    const mIdx = new Date(sp.NgayThem).getMonth();
                    if (mIdx >= 0 && mIdx < 12) dataSP[mIdx] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp);
                });
                donMayLoc.forEach(mItem => {
                    const mIdx = new Date(mItem.NgayTao || mItem.NgayThue).getMonth();
                    if (mIdx >= 0 && mIdx < 12) dataMay[mIdx] += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(mItem);
                });
            } else {
                const mapThang = {};
                const themVaoMap = (dateStr, loai, tien) => {
                    if (!dateStr) return;
                    const d = new Date(dateStr);
                    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return;
                    const key = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
                    if (!mapThang[key]) mapThang[key] = { sp: 0, may: 0 };
                    mapThang[key][loai] += tien;
                };

                donSPLoc.forEach(sp => themVaoMap(sp.NgayThem, 'sp', tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp)));
                donMayLoc.forEach(m => themVaoMap(m.NgayTao || m.NgayThue, 'may', tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(m)));

                const sortedKeys = Object.keys(mapThang).sort();
                if (sortedKeys.length === 0) {
                    labels = ['ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u'];
                    dataSP = [0];
                    dataMay = [0];
                } else {
                    labels = sortedKeys.map(k => {
                        const [y, m] = k.split('-');
                        return `T${parseInt(m)}/${y}`;
                    });
                    dataSP = sortedKeys.map(k => mapThang[k].sp);
                    dataMay = sortedKeys.map(k => mapThang[k].may);
                }
            }

            const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-doanh-thu').getContext('2d');
            if (chartDoanhThuInstance) chartDoanhThuInstance.destroy();

            chartDoanhThuInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels,
                    datasets: [
                        {
                            label: 'Doanh thu Sáº£n pháº©m (Ä‘)',
                            data: dataSP,
                            backgroundColor: '#064e3b',
                            borderRadius: 6
                        },
                        {
                            label: 'Doanh thu ThuÃª mÃ¡y (Ä‘)',
                            data: dataMay,
                            backgroundColor: '#d97706',
                            borderRadius: 6
                        }
                    ]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { position: 'top', labels: { font: { size: 11, weight: 'bold' } } },
                        tooltip: {
                            callbacks: {
                                label: ctx => `${ctx.dataset.label}: ${Number(ctx.raw).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘`
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    scales: {
                        y: {
                            beginAtZero: true,
                            ticks: {
                                font: { size: 10 },
                                callback: val => Number(val).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘'
                            }
                        },
                        x: { ticks: { font: { size: 10 } } }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        function veBieuDoCoCau(dtSP, dtMay) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-co-cau').getContext('2d');
            if (chartCoCauInstance) chartCoCauInstance.destroy();

            const coDuLieu = (dtSP + dtMay) > 0;
            chartCoCauInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'doughnut',
                data: {
                    labels: coDuLieu ? ['Sáº£n pháº©m váº­t tÆ°', 'Dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y'] : ['ChÆ°a cÃ³ doanh thu'],
                    datasets: [{
                        data: coDuLieu ? [dtSP, dtMay] : [1],
                        backgroundColor: coDuLieu ? ['#064e3b', '#d97706'] : ['#e5e7eb'],
                        borderWidth: 2,
                        borderColor: '#ffffff'
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    cutout: '65%',
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { font: { size: 11, weight: 'bold' } } },
                        tooltip: {
                            callbacks: {
                                label: ctx => coDuLieu
                                    ? `${ctx.label}: ${Number(ctx.raw).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘`
                                    : 'ChÆ°a phÃ¡t sinh giao dá»‹ch'
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        function renderBangChiTietSanPham(donSPLoc) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-tk-san-pham');
            if (donSPLoc.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">KhÃ´ng cÃ³ Ä‘Æ¡n sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o trong ká»³ Ä‘Ã£ chá»n.</td></tr>`;
                return [];
            }

            const gomNhom = {};
            donSPLoc.forEach(sp => {
                const key = sp.TenSanPham;
                if (!gomNhom[key]) {
                    gomNhom[key] = {
                        ten: sp.TenSanPham,
                        danhMuc: sp.DanhMuc,
                        donVi: sp.DonViTinh,
                        soDon: 0,
                        soLuong: 0,
                        doanhThu: 0
                    };
                }
                gomNhom[key].soDon += 1;
                gomNhom[key].soLuong += Number(sp.SoLuong || 0);
                gomNhom[key].doanhThu += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanSP(sp);
            });

            const danhSach = Object.values(gomNhom).sort((a, b) => b.doanhThu - a.doanhThu);
            tbody.innerHTML = danhSach.map(item => `
                <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                        <p class="font-bold text-gray-800">${item.ten}</p>
                        <span class="text-[11px] text-gray-400">${item.danhMuc}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center font-semibold">${item.soDon}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center font-bold text-luaGreen">${item.soLuong} ${item.donVi}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-black text-luaYellow">${item.doanhThu.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');

            return danhSach;
        }

        function renderBangChiTietThueMay(donMayLoc) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-tk-thue-may');
            if (donMayLoc.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">KhÃ´ng cÃ³ lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ o trong ká»³ Ä‘Ã£ chá»n.</td></tr>`;
                return [];
            }

            const gomNhom = {};
            donMayLoc.forEach(m => {
                const key = m.TenMay;
                if (!gomNhom[key]) {
                    gomNhom[key] = {
                        ten: m.TenMay,
                        loai: m.LoaiMay,
                        donVi: m.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng',
                        soLuot: 0,
                        soCong: 0,
                        doanhThu: 0
                    };
                }
                gomNhom[key].soLuot += 1;
                gomNhom[key].soCong += Number(m.SoCong || 0);
                gomNhom[key].doanhThu += tinhDoanhThuThucNhanMay(m);
            });

            const danhSach = Object.values(gomNhom).sort((a, b) => b.doanhThu - a.doanhThu);
            tbody.innerHTML = danhSach.map(item => `
                <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                        <p class="font-bold text-gray-800">${item.ten}</p>
                        <span class="text-[11px] text-amber-700">${item.loai}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center font-semibold">${item.soLuot}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center font-bold text-blue-700">${item.soCong} ${item.donVi}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-black text-luaYellow">${item.doanhThu.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');

            return danhSach;
        }

        function renderBangChiTietSuKien(dangKySKLoc) {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-tk-su-kien');
            const dsSuKien = rawData.danhSachSuKien || [];
            const tatCaDK = rawData.dangKySuKien || [];

            if (dsSuKien.length === 0 && tatCaDK.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">ChÆ°a cÃ³ há»™i tháº£o hoáº·c sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ o trong há»‡ thá»‘ng.</td></tr>`;
                return [];
            }

            const mapSK = {};
            dsSuKien.forEach(sk => {
                const tieuDe = (sk.TieuDe || '').trim();
                if (!tieuDe) return;
                mapSK[tieuDe.toLowerCase()] = {
                    tieuDe,
                    thoiGian: sk.ThoiGian || 'Theo lá»‹ch ban tá»• chá»©c',
                    diaDiem: sk.DiaDiem || 'Äang cáº­p nháº­t',
                    trongKy: 0,
                    tongTichLuy: 0
                };
            });

            tatCaDK.forEach(dk => {
                const tieuDe = (dk.TieuDeSuKien || '').trim();
                const key = tieuDe.toLowerCase();
                if (!mapSK[key]) {
                    mapSK[key] = {
                        tieuDe,
                        thoiGian: 'Theo lá»‹ch ban tá»• chá»©c',
                        diaDiem: 'Há»™i trÆ°á»ng / Äá»“ng ruá»™ng',
                        trongKy: 0,
                        tongTichLuy: 0
                    };
                }
                mapSK[key].tongTichLuy += 1;
            });

            dangKySKLoc.forEach(dk => {
                const key = (dk.TieuDeSuKien || '').trim().toLowerCase();
                if (mapSK[key]) mapSK[key].trongKy += 1;
            });

            const danhSach = Object.values(mapSK).sort((a, b) => b.trongKy - a.trongKy || b.tongTichLuy - a.tongTichLuy);
            tbody.innerHTML = danhSach.map(sk => `
                <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <td class="py-3.5 px-4 font-bold text-gray-800">${sk.tieuDe}</td>
                    <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-gray-600">${sk.thoiGian}</td>
                    <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-gray-600">${sk.diaDiem}</td>
                    <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <span class="bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 font-black px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${sk.trongKy} nÃ´ng dÃ¢n</span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center font-bold text-gray-700">${sk.tongTichLuy} nÃ´ng dÃ¢n</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');

            return danhSach;
        }

        // ================= HÃ€M XUáº¤T FILE EXCEL BÃO CÃO THEO NGÃ€Y / THÃNG / NÄ‚M =================
        function formatThoiGianVN(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return '--';
            const d = new Date(dateStr);
            if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(dateStr);
            return d.toLocaleString('vi-VN');
        }

    <script>
        let tatCaTinNhan = [];
        let sdtDangChon = null;   // SÄT cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Ä‘ang chat
        let tuKhoaTim = '';
        let dangGui = false;

        // Dáº¥u vÃ¢n tay Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‰ váº½ láº¡i khi dá»¯ liá»‡u tháº­t sá»± thay Ä‘á»•i (khÃ´ng giáº­t, khÃ´ng nháº£y cuá»™n)
        let dauVanListCu = '';
        let dauVanChatCu = '';

        // ---------- Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c" (lÆ°u trong trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t cá»§a admin) ----------
        const KHOA_DA_DOC = 'luaVangAdminDaDoc';
        let daDoc = {};              // { sdt: thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm (ms) cá»§a tin nÃ´ng dÃ¢n cuá»‘i cÃ¹ng admin Ä‘Ã£ xem }
        let daKhoiTaoDaDoc = false;

        function luuDaDoc() {
            try { localStorage.setItem(KHOA_DA_DOC, JSON.stringify(daDoc)); } catch (e) {}
        }

        function thoiDiemTinNongDanCuoi(sdt) {
            let max = 0;
            tatCaTinNhan.forEach(m => {
                if (m.SoDienThoai === sdt && m.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan') {
                    const t = new Date(m.NgayGui).getTime();
                    if (t > max) max = t;
                }
            });
            return max;
        }

        function danhDauDaDoc(sdt) {
            const t = thoiDiemTinNongDanCuoi(sdt);
            if (t > (daDoc[sdt] || 0)) {
                daDoc[sdt] = t;
                luuDaDoc();
            }
        }

        function khoiTaoDaDoc() {
            let luu = null;
            try { luu = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KHOA_DA_DOC)); } catch (e) {}

            if (luu) {
                daDoc = luu;
            } else {
                // Láº§n Ä‘áº§u dÃ¹ng: coi tin cÅ© lÃ  Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c, chá»‰ bÃ¡o cháº¥m Ä‘á» cho tin má»›i tá»« giá»
                new Set(tatCaTinNhan.map(m => m.SoDienThoai)).forEach(sdt => {
                    daDoc[sdt] = thoiDiemTinNongDanCuoi(sdt);
                });
                luuDaDoc();
            }
            daKhoiTaoDaDoc = true;
        }

        // ---------- Tiá»‡n Ã­ch ----------
        function escapeHtml(text) {
            return String(text ?? '')
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
        }

        function dinhDangNoiDung(text) {
            return escapeHtml(text).replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
        }

        function chuVietTat(hoTen) {
            return escapeHtml((hoTen || 'ND').trim().substring(0, 2).toUpperCase());
        }

        function gioPhut(ngay) {
            return new Date(ngay).toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
        }

        function laTinAI(msg) {
            return msg.NguoiGui === 'admin' && String(msg.NoiDung || '').includes('[Trá»£ lÃ½ AI');
        }

        // ---------- BÃ¡o tráº¡ng thÃ¡i admin online cho server ----------
        function guiPingAdmin() {
            fetch('/api/admin-ping', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
        }

        // ---------- Khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng trang ----------
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            document.getElementById('search-nongdan').addEventListener('input', function (e) {
                tuKhoaTim = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
                dauVanListCu = '';
                renderDanhSachNongDan();
            });

            document.getElementById('danh-sach-nong-dan').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                const item = e.target.closest('[data-sdt]');
                if (item) chonNongDan(item.dataset.sdt);
            });

            document.getElementById('btn-admin-send').addEventListener('click', guiTinNhanAdmin);
            document.getElementById('admin-chat-input').addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    guiTinNhanAdmin();
                }
            });

            taiTatCaTinNhan();
            setInterval(taiTatCaTinNhan, 3000);

            // Admin Ä‘ang má»Ÿ trang nÃ y: AI táº¡m ngá»«ng tráº£ lá»i
            guiPingAdmin();
            setInterval(guiPingAdmin, 10000);

            // Khi admin chuyá»ƒn sang trang khÃ¡c hoáº·c Ä‘Ã³ng tab thÃ¬ bÃ¡o offline ngay
            window.addEventListener('pagehide', function () {
                navigator.sendBeacon('/api/admin-offline');
            });
        });

        async function taiTatCaTinNhan() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/tin-nhan');
                const result = await res.json();
                if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error || 'KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c tin nháº¯n');
                tatCaTinNhan = result.data;

                if (!daKhoiTaoDaDoc) khoiTaoDaDoc();

                // Äang xem cuá»™c trÃ² chuyá»‡n nÃ o thÃ¬ tin má»›i cá»§a ngÆ°á»i Ä‘Ã³ coi nhÆ° Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c
                if (sdtDangChon && document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
                    danhDauDaDoc(sdtDangChon);
                }

                renderDanhSachNongDan();
                if (sdtDangChon) renderNoiDungChat(false);
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i tin nháº¯n:', err);
            }
        }

        // ---------- Danh sÃ¡ch nÃ´ng dÃ¢n ----------
        function layDanhSachCuocTroChuyen() {
            const map = {};
            tatCaTinNhan.forEach(item => {
                const sdt = item.SoDienThoai;
                if (!map[sdt]) {
                    map[sdt] = { soDienThoai: sdt, hoTen: 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n', noiDungCuoi: '', ngayCuoi: 0, chuaDoc: 0 };
                }
                const nd = map[sdt];
                const t = new Date(item.NgayGui).getTime();

                if (item.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan') {
                    // TÃªn láº¥y tá»« tin cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n
                    if (item.HoTen) nd.hoTen = item.HoTen;
                    // Tin cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n má»›i hÆ¡n láº§n admin xem cuá»‘i thÃ¬ lÃ  chÆ°a Ä‘á»c
                    if (t > (daDoc[sdt] || 0)) nd.chuaDoc++;
                }

                if (t >= nd.ngayCuoi) {
                    nd.ngayCuoi = t;
                    nd.noiDungCuoi = item.NoiDung;
                    nd.nguoiGuiCuoi = item.NguoiGui;
                }
            });

            // Chá»‰ giá»¯ nhá»¯ng ngÆ°á»i Ä‘Ã£ tá»«ng nháº¯n tin (cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 1 tin tá»« nÃ´ng dÃ¢n)
            const coTinNongDan = new Set(
                tatCaTinNhan.filter(m => m.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan').map(m => m.SoDienThoai)
            );

            return Object.values(map)
                .filter(nd => coTinNongDan.has(nd.soDienThoai))
                .sort((a, b) => b.ngayCuoi - a.ngayCuoi);
        }

        function renderDanhSachNongDan() {
            const container = document.getElementById('danh-sach-nong-dan');
            let danhSach = layDanhSachCuocTroChuyen();

            if (tuKhoaTim) {
                danhSach = danhSach.filter(nd =>
                    (nd.hoTen || '').toLowerCase().includes(tuKhoaTim) ||
                    (nd.soDienThoai || '').toLowerCase().includes(tuKhoaTim)
                );
            }

            const dauVan = JSON.stringify([sdtDangChon, danhSach.map(nd => [nd.soDienThoai, nd.hoTen, nd.noiDungCuoi, nd.ngayCuoi, nd.chuaDoc])]);
            if (dauVan === dauVanListCu) return;
            dauVanListCu = dauVan;

            if (danhSach.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-6 text-center text-gray-400 text-xs">${
                    tuKhoaTim ? 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y nÃ´ng dÃ¢n phÃ¹ há»£p.' : 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ nÃ´ng dÃ¢n nÃ o nháº¯n tin.'
                }</div>`;
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = danhSach.map(nd => {
                const dangChon = sdtDangChon === nd.soDienThoai;
                const coChuaDoc = nd.chuaDoc > 0;
                const lop = dangChon ? 'bg-emerald-50/80 border-l-4 border-luaGreen' : 'hover:bg-gray-100/50';
                const tienTo = nd.nguoiGuiCuoi === 'nong-dan' ? '' : 'Báº¡n/AI: ';
                const xemTruoc = tienTo + String(nd.noiDungCuoi || '').replace(/\n/g, ' ');
                return `
                    <div data-sdt="${escapeHtml(nd.soDienThoai)}" class="p-4 ${lop} cursor-pointer flex items-center space-x-3 transition">
                        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-emerald-200 text-luaGreen flex items-center justify-center font-bold flex-shrink-0">
                            ${chuVietTat(nd.hoTen)}
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <p class="font-bold text-sm ${coChuaDoc ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-800'} truncate">${escapeHtml(nd.hoTen)}</p>
                                <span class="flex items-center space-x-1.5 ml-2 flex-shrink-0">
                                    ${coChuaDoc ? '<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full" title="CÃ³ tin nháº¯n má»›i chÆ°a Ä‘á»c"></span>' : ''}
                                    <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">${gioPhut(nd.ngayCuoi)}</span>
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <p class="text-xs ${coChuaDoc ? 'text-gray-800 font-semibold' : 'text-gray-500'} truncate">${escapeHtml(xemTruoc)}</p>
                            <p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 truncate">SÄT: ${escapeHtml(nd.soDienThoai)}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        // ---------- Khung chat ----------
        function chonNongDan(sdt) {
            sdtDangChon = sdt;
            const nd = layDanhSachCuocTroChuyen().find(x => x.soDienThoai === sdt);
            const hoTen = nd ? nd.hoTen : 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n';

            document.getElementById('chat-name').textContent = hoTen;
            document.getElementById('chat-phone').textContent = 'SÄT: ' + sdt;
            document.getElementById('chat-avatar').textContent = (hoTen || 'ND').trim().substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
            document.getElementById('chat-status').textContent = 'Äang trÃ² chuyá»‡n';

            danhDauDaDoc(sdt);   // má»Ÿ cuá»™c trÃ² chuyá»‡n thÃ¬ táº¯t cháº¥m Ä‘á»

            dauVanListCu = '';
            dauVanChatCu = '';
            renderDanhSachNongDan();
            renderNoiDungChat(true);
            document.getElementById('admin-chat-input').focus();
        }

        function renderNoiDungChat(cuonXuongCuoi) {
            const container = document.getElementById('admin-chat-messages');
            const messages = tatCaTinNhan.filter(m => m.SoDienThoai === sdtDangChon);

            const dauVan = sdtDangChon + '|' + messages.length + '|' +
                (messages.length ? messages[messages.length - 1].NoiDung : '');
            if (dauVan === dauVanChatCu && !cuonXuongCuoi) return;

            // Náº¿u Ä‘ang Ä‘á»©ng gáº§n cuá»‘i thÃ¬ tá»± cuá»™n xuá»‘ng khi cÃ³ tin má»›i
            const ganCuoi = container.scrollHeight - container.scrollTop - container.clientHeight < 80;
            const coTinMoi = dauVan !== dauVanChatCu;
            dauVanChatCu = dauVan;

            if (messages.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-gray-400 py-20 text-xs">ChÆ°a cÃ³ tin nháº¯n nÃ o.</div>';
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = messages.map(msg => {
                const gio = gioPhut(msg.NgayGui);
                const noiDung = dinhDangNoiDung(msg.NoiDung);

                if (msg.NguoiGui === 'nong-dan') {
                    return `
                        <div class="flex items-start space-x-2">
                            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-emerald-200 text-luaGreen flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0 font-bold">
                                ${chuVietTat(msg.HoTen)}
                            </div>
                            <div class="bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 max-w-[80%] text-gray-800">
                                <p class="font-bold text-luaGreen text-xs mb-1">${escapeHtml(msg.HoTen)}</p>
                                <p class="leading-relaxed">${noiDung}</p>
                                <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1 block">${gio}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }

                if (laTinAI(msg)) {
                    return `
                        <div class="flex items-start justify-end space-x-2">
                            <div class="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm max-w-[80%] text-gray-800">
                                <p class="font-bold text-amber-700 text-xs mb-1 text-right">Trá»£ lÃ½ AI (tá»± Ä‘á»™ng)</p>
                                <p class="leading-relaxed">${noiDung}</p>
                                <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1 block text-right">${gio}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-amber-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-robot"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }

                return `
                    <div class="flex items-start justify-end space-x-2">
                        <div class="bg-luaGreen text-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm max-w-[80%]">
                            <p class="font-bold text-emerald-200 text-xs mb-1 text-right">Admin (Báº¡n)</p>
                            <p class="leading-relaxed">${noiDung}</p>
                            <span class="text-[10px] text-emerald-200 mt-1 block text-right">${gio}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-luaGreen text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0 font-bold">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-user-shield"></i>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');

            if (cuonXuongCuoi || (coTinMoi && ganCuoi)) {
                container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
            }
        }

        // ---------- Gá»­i tin nháº¯n cá»§a admin ----------
        async function guiTinNhanAdmin() {
            if (dangGui) return;

            if (!sdtDangChon) {
                alert('Vui lÃ²ng chá»n má»™t nÃ´ng dÃ¢n á»Ÿ danh sÃ¡ch bÃªn trÃ¡i Ä‘á»ƒ tráº£ lá»i!');
                return;
            }

            const input = document.getElementById('admin-chat-input');
            const nut = document.getElementById('btn-admin-send');
            const noiDung = input.value.trim();
            if (!noiDung) return;

            const nd = layDanhSachCuocTroChuyen().find(x => x.soDienThoai === sdtDangChon);
            const hoTen = nd ? nd.hoTen : 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n';

            dangGui = true;
            nut.disabled = true;
            nut.classList.add('opacity-50');

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/tin-nhan', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        nguoiGui: 'admin',
                        hoTen: hoTen,
                        soDienThoai: sdtDangChon,
                        noiDung: noiDung
                    })
                });
                const result = await res.json();

                if (!res.ok || !result.success) {
                    throw new Error(result.error || ('MÃ¡y chá»§ tráº£ vá» lá»—i ' + res.status));
                }

                input.value = '';
                await taiTatCaTinNhan();
                renderNoiDungChat(true);
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i gá»­i tin nháº¯n:', err);
                alert('KhÃ´ng gá»­i Ä‘Æ°á»£c tin nháº¯n: ' + err.message);
            } finally {
                dangGui = false;
                nut.disabled = false;
                nut.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                input.focus();
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script>
        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const loai = params.get('loai') || 'san-pham';
        const id = parseInt(params.get('id'));

        const DANH_SACH_KHUNG_GIO = [
            { value: 'SÃ¡ng sá»›m (06:00 - 09:00)', nhan: 'SÃ¡ng sá»›m', gio: '06:00 - 09:00' },
            { value: 'Buá»•i sÃ¡ng (09:00 - 12:00)', nhan: 'Buá»•i sÃ¡ng', gio: '09:00 - 12:00' },
            { value: 'Buá»•i chiá»u (13:30 - 16:30)', nhan: 'Buá»•i chiá»u', gio: '13:30 - 16:30' },
            { value: 'Cáº£ ngÃ y (06:00 - 16:30)', nhan: 'Cáº£ ngÃ y', gio: '06:00 - 16:30' }
        ];

        let itemHienTai = null;
        let donGiaHienTai = 0;
        let soLuongChon = 1;
        let khungGioChon = '';
        let lichDaDatMay = [];
        let lichTrongGioMay = [];
        let timerKiemTraQR = null;
        let maThanhToanDangCho = '';

        function layNgayHomNay() {
            const d = new Date();
            const offset = d.getTimezoneOffset();
            const local = new Date(d.getTime() - offset * 60 * 1000);
            return local.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        }

        function kiemTraTrungKhungGioClient(gioA, gioB) {
            if (!gioA || !gioB) return false;
            if (gioA === gioB) return true;
            if (gioA.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y') || gioB.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y')) return true;
            return false;
        }

        function layTaiKhoanTab() {
            const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (raw && !sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', raw);
            }
            return raw;
        }

        function lamSachMoTa(text) {
            return String(text || '')
                .replace(/\$\s*\\ge\s*([^$]+)\$/g, 'â‰¥ $1')
                .replace(/\$\s*\\le\s*([^$]+)\$/g, 'â‰¤ $1');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const urlQuayLai = loai === 'thue-may' ? '/thue-may.html' : '/san-pham.html';
            const tenMuc = loai === 'thue-may' ? 'ThuÃª mÃ¡y' : 'Sáº£n pháº©m';

            document.getElementById('btn-quay-lai').href = urlQuayLai;
            document.getElementById('text-quay-lai').innerText = `Quay láº¡i danh sÃ¡ch ${tenMuc.toLowerCase()}`;
            document.getElementById('breadcrumb-loai').href = urlQuayLai;
            document.getElementById('breadcrumb-loai').innerText = tenMuc;
            document.getElementById('link-xem-tat-ca').href = urlQuayLai;

            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }

                    const targetPage = loai === 'thue-may' ? 'thue-may.html' : 'san-pham.html';
                    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(link => {
                        if (link.getAttribute('data-target') === targetPage) {
                            link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                            link.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        } else {
                            link.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        }
                    });
                });

            taiChiTiet();
        });

        async function taiChiTiet() {
            const container = document.getElementById('khung-chi-tiet');
            if (!id) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ sáº£n pháº©m/dá»‹ch vá»¥ cáº§n xem.</div>`;
                return;
            }

            try {
                if (loai === 'thue-may') {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/thue-may');
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        const ds = result.data || [];
                        itemHienTai = ds.find(m => Number(m.MaMay) === id);
                        if (!itemHienTai) {
                            container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">Dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ y khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ xÃ³a.</div>`;
                            return;
                        }
                        renderChiTietThueMay(itemHienTai);
                        renderLienQuanThueMay(ds, itemHienTai);
                        document.getElementById('section-lich-may').classList.remove('hidden');
                        await taiLichDaDatMay();
                    }
                } else {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/san-pham');
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        const ds = result.data || [];
                        itemHienTai = ds.find(sp => Number(sp.MaSanPham) === id);
                        if (!itemHienTai) {
                            container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">Sáº£n pháº©m nÃ y khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ xÃ³a.</div>`;
                            return;
                        }
                        renderChiTietSanPham(itemHienTai);
                        renderLienQuanSanPham(ds, itemHienTai);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i chi tiáº¿t:', err);
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-12 text-center text-red-500">KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i mÃ¡y chá»§ Ä‘á»ƒ táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u.</div>`;
            }
        }

        // ================= Lá»ŠCH ÄÃƒ Äáº¶T MINH Báº CH Cá»¦A Tá»ªNG MÃY =================
        async function taiLichDaDatMay() {
            if (!itemHienTai || loai !== 'thue-may') return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/thue-may/${itemHienTai.MaMay}/lich-da-dat`);
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    lichDaDatMay = result.lichDaDat || [];
                    lichTrongGioMay = result.lichTrongGio || [];
                    capNhatLuaChonKhungGio();
                    renderBangLichCongKhai();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i lá»‹ch mÃ¡y:', err);
            }
        }

        function capNhatLuaChonKhungGio() {
            const ngayChon = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue')?.value || '';
            const gridEl = document.getElementById('grid-khung-gio');
            const modalSelect = document.getElementById('modal-khung-gio');

            if (!gridEl) return;

            const donTrongNgay = lichDaDatMay.filter(x => x.NgayThue === ngayChon);
            const gioTrongNgay = lichTrongGioMay.filter(x => x.NgayThue === ngayChon);

            if (khungGioChon && donTrongNgay.some(d => kiemTraTrungKhungGioClient(d.KhungGio, khungGioChon))) {
                khungGioChon = '';
            }

            gridEl.innerHTML = DANH_SACH_KHUNG_GIO.map(kg => {
                const daBiDat = ngayChon && donTrongNgay.some(d => kiemTraTrungKhungGioClient(d.KhungGio, kg.value));
                const dangGiuGio = !daBiDat && ngayChon && gioTrongNgay.some(g => kiemTraTrungKhungGioClient(g.KhungGio, kg.value));
                const dangChon = khungGioChon === kg.value;

                if (daBiDat) {
                    return `
                        <button type="button" disabled class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/80 text-left opacity-80 cursor-not-allowed">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 line-through">${kg.nhan}</span>
                                <span class="text-[10px] font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">ÄÃ£ kÃ­n</span>
                            </div>
                            <p class="text-[11px] text-red-500 mt-0.5">${kg.gio}</p>
                        </button>
                    `;
                }

                const borderCls = dangChon
                    ? 'border-luaGreen bg-emerald-50 ring-2 ring-luaGreen/30'
                    : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-luaGreen hover:bg-emerald-50/40';

                const badgeHtml = dangGiuGio
                    ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">Äang giá»¯ giá»</span>`
                    : `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">${dangChon ? 'Äang chá»n' : 'CÃ²n trá»‘ng'}</span>`;

                return `
                    <button type="button" onclick="chonKhungGio('${kg.value}')" class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border text-left transition ${borderCls}">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-800">${kg.nhan}</span>
                            ${badgeHtml}
                        </div>
                        <p class="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">${kg.gio}</p>
                    </button>
                `;
            }).join('');

            if (modalSelect) {
                modalSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Chá»n khung giá» --</option>` + DANH_SACH_KHUNG_GIO.map(kg => {
                    const daBiDat = ngayChon && donTrongNgay.some(d => kiemTraTrungKhungGioClient(d.KhungGio, kg.value));
                    const selected = khungGioChon === kg.value ? 'selected' : '';
                    const disabled = daBiDat ? 'disabled' : '';
                    const label = daBiDat ? `${kg.value} (ÄÃ£ cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i Ä‘áº·t)` : `${kg.value} (CÃ²n trá»‘ng)`;
                    return `<option value="${kg.value}" ${selected} ${disabled}>${label}</option>`;
                }).join('');
            }
        }

        function chonKhungGio(val) {
            khungGioChon = val;
            capNhatLuaChonKhungGio();
        }

        function thayDoiNgayThueChinh(val) {
            const modalNgay = document.getElementById('modal-ngay-thue');
            if (modalNgay) modalNgay.value = val;
            capNhatLuaChonKhungGio();
            renderBangLichCongKhai();
        }

        function thayDoiNgayTrongModal(val) {
            const mainNgay = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue');
            if (mainNgay) mainNgay.value = val;
            capNhatLuaChonKhungGio();
            renderBangLichCongKhai();
        }

        function renderBangLichCongKhai() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tbody-lich-cong-khai');
            if (!tbody) return;

            const chiXemNgayChon = document.getElementById('chk-loc-theo-ngay')?.checked;
            const ngayChon = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue')?.value || '';

            let dsHienThi = lichDaDatMay;
            if (chiXemNgayChon && ngayChon) {
                dsHienThi = lichDaDatMay.filter(x => x.NgayThue === ngayChon);
            }

            if (dsHienThi.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="6" class="py-8 text-center text-emerald-700 font-semibold bg-emerald-50/30">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1.5"></i>
                            ${chiXemNgayChon && ngayChon
                                ? `NgÃ y ${ngayChon} hiá»‡n Ä‘ang trá»‘ng toÃ n bá»™ cÃ¡c ca â€” BÃ  con cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘áº·t ngay!`
                                : 'Hiá»‡n chÆ°a cÃ³ lá»‹ch Ä‘áº·t trÆ°á»›c nÃ o cho thiáº¿t bá»‹ nÃ y â€” Táº¥t cáº£ khung giá» Ä‘á»u Ä‘ang trá»‘ng!'}
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
                return;
            }

            tbody.innerHTML = dsHienThi.map(item => {
                const badgeCls = item.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800'
                    : item.TrangThai === 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n'
                        ? 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-800'
                        : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';

                return `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50/80">
                        <td class="py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-800">${item.NgayThue || '--'}</td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4 font-bold text-luaGreen">
                            <i class="fa-regular fa-clock mr-1 text-luaYellow"></i>${item.KhungGio || 'ChÆ°a ghi ca'}
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">${item.SoCong} ${item.DonViTinh}</td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4 text-gray-600">${item.KhuVuc}</td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-gray-700">${item.NguoiThue}</td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4">
                            <span class="${badgeCls} font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">${item.TrangThai}</span>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        // ================= RENDER CHI TIáº¾T Sáº¢N PHáº¨M =================
        function renderChiTietSanPham(sp) {
            document.title = `LÃºa VÃ ng - ${sp.TenSanPham}`;

        // ================= RENDER CHI TIáº¾T THUÃŠ MÃY =================
        function renderChiTietThueMay(item) {
            document.title = `LÃºa VÃ ng - ${item.TenMay}`;
            document.getElementById('breadcrumb-ten').innerText = item.TenMay;

            let iconClass = 'fa-tractor text-amber-600';
            let bgClass = 'bg-amber-50/70';
            let badgeClass = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';
            const loaiMayText = item.LoaiMay || 'ThuÃª mÃ¡y';

            if (loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('xáº¡ phÃ¢n') || loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('giá»‘ng')) {
                iconClass = 'fa-seedling text-emerald-600';
                bgClass = 'bg-emerald-50/70';
                badgeClass = 'bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen';
            } else if (loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('phun thuá»‘c')) {
                iconClass = 'fa-helicopter text-amber-600';
                bgClass = 'bg-amber-50/70';
                badgeClass = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';
            }

            let hinhAnhHtml = `
                <div class="${bgClass} rounded-2xl h-80 md:h-96 flex items-center justify-center border border-gray-100">

            if (item.HinhAnh && item.HinhAnh.trim() !== '' && item.HinhAnh !== 'default.jpg') {
                hinhAnhHtml = `
                    <div class="rounded-2xl h-80 md:h-96 overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <img src="/hinh-anh/${item.HinhAnh}" alt="${item.TenMay}" class="w-full h-full object-cover"
                             onerror="this.parentElement.innerHTML='<div class=\\'${bgClass} w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center\\'><i class=\\'fa-solid ${iconClass}\\' style=\\'font-size: 5rem;\\'></i></div>'">
                    </div>
                `;
            }

            donGiaHienTai = Number(item.GiaThue);
            const donVi = item.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng';

        // ================= Äá»’NG Bá»˜ Sá» LÆ¯á»¢NG & TÃNH TIá»€N =================
        function capNhatHienThiGia() {
            const tong = soLuongChon * donGiaHienTai;
            const coc = Math.round(tong * 0.3);
            const elMainQty = document.getElementById('input-so-luong');
            const elMainTotal = document.getElementById('tam-tinh');
            const elModalQty = document.getElementById('modal-input-qty');
            const elModalTotal = document.getElementById('modal-tong-tien');
            const elModalCoc = document.getElementById('modal-tien-coc');

            if (elMainQty) elMainQty.value = soLuongChon;
            if (elMainTotal) elMainTotal.innerText = tong.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            if (elModalQty) elModalQty.value = soLuongChon;
            if (elModalTotal) elModalTotal.innerText = tong.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            if (elModalCoc) elModalCoc.innerText = coc.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
        }

        function thayDoiSoLuong(delta) {
            soLuongChon = Math.max(1, soLuongChon + delta);
            capNhatHienThiGia();
        }

        function nhapSoLuongTrucTiep(val) {
            soLuongChon = Math.max(1, parseInt(val) || 1);
            capNhatHienThiGia();
        }

        function dongBoThueMayTuModal() {
            const mNgay = document.getElementById('modal-ngay-thue')?.value || '';
            const mGhiChu = document.getElementById('modal-ghi-chu-thue')?.value || '';
            const mainNgay = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue');
            const mainGhiChu = document.getElementById('input-ghi-chu-thue');
            if (mainNgay) mainNgay.value = mNgay;
            if (mainGhiChu) mainGhiChu.value = mGhiChu;
        }

        // ================= NÃšT 1: THÃŠM VÃ€O GIá»Ž =================
        async function themVaoGioChiTiet() {
            const userData = layTaiKhoanTab();
            if (!userData) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi thÃªm vÃ o giá»!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);

            try {
                if (loai === 'thue-may') {
                    const ngayThue = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue')?.value || '';
                    const ghiChu = document.getElementById('input-ghi-chu-thue')?.value || '';

                    const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            soDienThoai: user.phone,
                            maMay: itemHienTai.MaMay,
                            soCong: soLuongChon,
                            ngayThue,
                            khungGio: khungGioChon,
                            ghiChu
                        })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                        await taiLichDaDatMay();
                        alert(`âœ… ÄÃ£ thÃªm "${itemHienTai.TenMay}" (${soLuongChon} ${itemHienTai.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng'}) vÃ o giá» hÃ ng cá»§a ${user.name}!`);
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    }
                } else {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/san-pham', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            soDienThoai: user.phone,
                            maSanPham: itemHienTai.MaSanPham,
                            soLuong: soLuongChon
                        })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                        alert(`âœ… ÄÃ£ thÃªm ${soLuongChon} ${itemHienTai.DonViTinh} "${itemHienTai.TenSanPham}" vÃ o giá» hÃ ng cá»§a ${user.name}!`);
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n server!');
            }
        }

        // ================= NÃšT 2: Má»ž MODAL Äáº¶T MUA NGAY / Äáº¶T Lá»ŠCH THUÃŠ NGAY =================
        async function moModalDatNgay() {
            const userData = layTaiKhoanTab();
            if (!userData) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘áº·t Ä‘Æ¡n!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);

            document.getElementById('modal-user-info').innerText = `${user.name} (${user.phone})`;
            capNhatHienThiGia();

            if (loai === 'thue-may') {
                document.getElementById('modal-icon').className = 'fa-solid fa-tractor text-amber-300 text-lg';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y';
                document.getElementById('modal-payment-badge').innerText = 'Thanh toÃ¡n sau khi hoÃ n thÃ nh';
                document.getElementById('modal-item-badge').innerText = itemHienTai.LoaiMay || 'ThuÃª mÃ¡y';
                document.getElementById('modal-item-name').innerText = itemHienTai.TenMay;
                document.getElementById('modal-item-price').innerText = `ÄÆ¡n giÃ¡: ${donGiaHienTai.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ / ${(itemHienTai.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng').toLowerCase()}`;
                document.getElementById('modal-qty-label').innerText = `Sá»‘ ${(itemHienTai.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng').toLowerCase()} cáº§n thuÃª:`;
                document.getElementById('text-btn-chot-don').innerText = 'XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch thuÃª';
                document.getElementById('modal-coc-row').classList.add('hidden');

                document.getElementById('modal-thue-may-fields').classList.remove('hidden');
                const modalNgay = document.getElementById('modal-ngay-thue');
                modalNgay.min = layNgayHomNay();
                modalNgay.value = document.getElementById('input-ngay-thue')?.value || layNgayHomNay();
                document.getElementById('modal-ghi-chu-thue').value = document.getElementById('input-ghi-chu-thue')?.value || '';
                capNhatLuaChonKhungGio();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('modal-icon').className = 'fa-solid fa-bag-shopping text-amber-300 text-lg';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Äáº·t mua & Cá»c 30% qua VietQR';
                document.getElementById('modal-payment-badge').innerText = 'Cá»c 30% qua SePay â€¢ 70% COD';
                document.getElementById('modal-item-badge').innerText = itemHienTai.DanhMuc || 'Sáº£n pháº©m';
                document.getElementById('modal-item-name').innerText = itemHienTai.TenSanPham;
                document.getElementById('modal-item-price').innerText = `ÄÆ¡n giÃ¡: ${donGiaHienTai.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ / ${(itemHienTai.DonViTinh || 'Ä‘Æ¡n vá»‹').toLowerCase()}`;
                document.getElementById('modal-qty-label').innerText = `Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng (${(itemHienTai.DonViTinh || 'Ä‘Æ¡n vá»‹').toLowerCase()}):`;
                document.getElementById('text-btn-chot-don').innerText = 'Táº¡o mÃ£ QR Ä‘áº·t cá»c 30%';
                document.getElementById('modal-coc-row').classList.remove('hidden');

                document.getElementById('modal-thue-may-fields').classList.add('hidden');
            }

            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-dat-ngay');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
        }

        function dongModalDatNgay() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-dat-ngay');
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
        }

        async function xacNhanChotDonNgay() {
            const userData = layTaiKhoanTab();
            if (!userData) return;
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);

            try {
                if (loai === 'thue-may') {
                    const ngayThue = document.getElementById('modal-ngay-thue').value.trim();
                    const khungGio = document.getElementById('modal-khung-gio').value.trim();
                    const ghiChu = document.getElementById('modal-ghi-chu-thue').value.trim();

                    if (!ngayThue) {
                        alert('Vui lÃ²ng chá»n ngÃ y dá»± kiáº¿n thuÃª mÃ¡y!');
                        document.getElementById('modal-ngay-thue').focus();
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!khungGio) {
                        alert('Vui lÃ²ng chá»n khung giá» (ca thuÃª) cÃ²n trá»‘ng!');
                        document.getElementById('modal-khung-gio').focus();
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!ghiChu) {
                        alert('Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ Ä‘á»“ng ruá»™ng hoáº·c ghi chÃº Ä‘á»ƒ ká»¹ thuáº­t viÃªn Ä‘iá»u mÃ¡y!');
                        document.getElementById('modal-ghi-chu-thue').focus();
                        return;
                    }

                    const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            soDienThoai: user.phone,
                            maMay: itemHienTai.MaMay,
                            soCong: soLuongChon,
                            ngayThue,
                            khungGio,
                            ghiChu,
                            trangThai: 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch'
                        })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        await hienThongBao('âœ… ' + result.message);
                        window.location.href = '/gio-hang.html?tab=thue-may';
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                        await taiLichDaDatMay();
                    }
                } else {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/san-pham', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            soDienThoai: user.phone,
                            maSanPham: itemHienTai.MaSanPham,
                            soLuong: soLuongChon,
                            trangThai: 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n'
                        })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        dongModalDatNgay();
                        moModalQRSePay(result);
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n server!');
            }
        }

        // ================= POPUP QR SEPAY & Tá»° Äá»˜NG KIá»‚M TRA TIá»€N VÃ€O =================
        function moModalQRSePay(data) {
            maThanhToanDangCho = data.maThanhToan;
            document.getElementById('qr-img-sepay').src = data.qrUrl;
            document.getElementById('qr-so-tien-coc').innerText = Number(data.tienCoc).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('qr-noi-dung').innerText = data.maThanhToan;

            const banner = document.getElementById('qr-status-banner');
            banner.className = 'bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2';
            banner.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-luaYellow"></i><span>Äang chá» chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n... Há»‡ thá»‘ng tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xÃ¡c nháº­n ngay khi tiá»n vÃ o!</span>`;

            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-qr-sepay');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');

            if (timerKiemTraQR) clearInterval(timerKiemTraQR);
            timerKiemTraQR = setInterval(kiemTraTrangThaiThanhToan, 3000);
        }

        function dongModalQR() {
            if (timerKiemTraQR) clearInterval(timerKiemTraQR);
            window.location.href = '/gio-hang.html?tab=san-pham';
        }

        function copyTextQR(text) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
            alert('ÄÃ£ sao chÃ©p: ' + text);
        }

        async function kiemTraThanhToanThuCong() {
            const daXong = await kiemTraTrangThaiThanhToan();
            if (!daXong) {
                alert('Há»‡ thá»‘ng chÆ°a nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c biáº¿n Ä‘á»™ng sá»‘ dÆ° cho mÃ£ ' + maThanhToanDangCho + '. Náº¿u báº¡n vá»«a chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n, vui lÃ²ng chá» 5-10 giÃ¢y rá»“i báº¥m láº¡i!');
            }
        }

        async function kiemTraTrangThaiThanhToan() {
            if (!maThanhToanDangCho) return false;
            const userData = layTaiKhoanTab();
            if (!userData) return false;
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(user.phone)}`);
                const result = await res.json();
                if (!result.success) return false;

                const donDaCoc = (result.sanPham || []).find(
                    sp => sp.MaThanhToan === maThanhToanDangCho && sp.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)'
                );

                if (donDaCoc) {
                    if (timerKiemTraQR) clearInterval(timerKiemTraQR);
                    const banner = document.getElementById('qr-status-banner');
                    banner.className = 'bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2';
                    banner.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-600 text-base"></i><span>ÄÃ£ nháº­n tiá»n cá»c 30% thÃ nh cÃ´ng! Äang chuyá»ƒn sang giá» hÃ ng...</span>`;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = '/gio-hang.html?tab=san-pham';
                    }, 1800);
                    return true;
                }
            } catch (e) {}
            return false;
        }

        // ================= RENDER Gá»¢I Ã CÃ™NG LOáº I =================
        function renderLienQuanSanPham(tatCa, hienTai) {
            const cungLoai = tatCa.filter(x => x.DanhMuc === hienTai.DanhMuc && Number(x.MaSanPham) !== Number(hienTai.MaSanPham)).slice(0, 3);
            if (cungLoai.length === 0) return;

            document.getElementById('section-lien-quan').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('tieu-de-lien-quan').innerText = `Sáº£n pháº©m cÃ¹ng nhÃ³m (${hienTai.DanhMuc})`;

            document.getElementById('danh-sach-lien-quan').innerHTML = cungLoai.map(sp => {
                const gia = Number(sp.GiaTien).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
                return `
                    <a href="/chi-tiet.html?loai=san-pham&id=${sp.MaSanPham}" class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between group">
                        <div>
                            <span class="inline-block bg-emerald-50 text-luaGreen text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md mb-2">${sp.DanhMuc}</span>
                            <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800 group-hover:text-luaGreen transition mb-1">${sp.TenSanPham}</h3>
                            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 line-clamp-2 mb-3">${lamSachMoTa(sp.MoTa)}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-50">
                            <span class="text-luaYellow font-black">${gia} / ${sp.DonViTinh.toLowerCase()}</span>
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-luaGreen">Xem chi tiáº¿t â†’</span>
                        </div>
                    </a>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function renderLienQuanThueMay(tatCa, hienTai) {
            const cungLoai = tatCa.filter(x => x.LoaiMay === hienTai.LoaiMay && Number(x.MaMay) !== Number(hienTai.MaMay)).slice(0, 3);

    <!-- SCRIPT LOGIC VÃ€ CAPTCHA -->
    <script>
        let currentCaptcha = '';

        // HÃ m táº¡o mÃ£ captcha ngáº«u nhiÃªn 6 kÃ½ tá»±
        function generateCaptcha() {
            const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789';
            currentCaptcha = '';
            for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                currentCaptcha += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
            }
            document.getElementById('captchaBox').innerText = currentCaptcha;
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o mÃ£ captcha khi táº£i trang
            generateCaptcha();

            // Load header
            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(response => response.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                });

            // Xá»­ lÃ½ gá»­i form Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ xuá»‘ng Backend káº¿t ná»‘i SQL Server
            document.getElementById('register-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                
                const userInput = document.getElementById('captchaInput').value.trim();
                
                // Kiá»ƒm tra mÃ£ captcha khá»›p hay khÃ´ng
                if (userInput !== currentCaptcha) {
                    alert('âŒ MÃ£ xÃ¡c nháº­n (Captcha) chÆ°a chÃ­nh xÃ¡c. Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p láº¡i!');
                    generateCaptcha();
                    document.getElementById('captchaInput').value = '';
                    document.getElementById('captchaInput').focus();
                    return;
                }

                const fullname = document.getElementById('fullname').value.trim();
                const phone = document.getElementById('phone').value.trim();
                const address = document.getElementById('address').value.trim();
                const password = document.getElementById('reg-password').value;

                try {
                    // Gá»­i request POST xuá»‘ng API backend Node.js
                    const response = await fetch('/api/dang-ky', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ fullname, phone, address, password })
                    });

                    const result = await response.json();

                    if (result.success) {
                        await hienThongBao('âœ… ÄÄƒng kÃ½ thÃ nh cÃ´ng! Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u vÃ o CSDL LuaVangDB.');
                        
                        // LÆ°u thÃ´ng tin vÃ o localStorage Ä‘á»ƒ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ lÃªn header
                        localStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', JSON.stringify({
                            name: fullname,
                            phone: phone,
                            role: 'nong-dan'
                        }));

                        window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                        generateCaptcha();
                        document.getElementById('captchaInput').value = '';
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i:', err);
                    alert('âŒ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i tá»›i server!');
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {

                .then(response => response.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;

            document.getElementById('login-form').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
