                const username = document.getElementById('username').value.trim();
                const password = document.getElementById('password').value;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch('/api/dang-nhap', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password })
                    });

                    const result = await response.json();

                    if (result.success) {
                        await hienThongBao('âœ… ' + result.message);
                        
                        const userInfo = JSON.stringify({
                            name: result.user.name,
                            phone: result.user.phone,
                            role: result.user.role
                        });

                        // LÆ°u riÃªng cho tab hiá»‡n táº¡i vÃ o sessionStorage (khÃ´ng ghi Ä‘Ã¨ phiÃªn cá»§a tab khÃ¡c)
                        sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', userInfo);
                        localStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', userInfo);

                        // Chuyá»ƒn hÆ°á»›ng vá» trang chá»§
                        window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
                    } else {
                        alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i:', err);
                    alert('âŒ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i tá»›i server!');

            });

        function formatPhutGiay(tongGiay) {
            const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tongGiay) || 0));
            const mm = String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
            const ss = String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0');
            return `${mm}:${ss}`;
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function () {
            const rawUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Ä‘á»ƒ xem giá» hÃ ng cá»§a báº¡n!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            if (!sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', rawUser);
            }

            currentUser = JSON.parse(rawUser);
            document.getElementById('owner-name').innerText = currentUser.name || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n';
            document.getElementById('owner-phone').innerText = currentUser.phone || '';

            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }
                });

            taiDuLieuGioHang();

            // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng lÃ m má»›i má»—i 15 giÃ¢y náº¿u cÃ³ Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘ang chá» thanh toÃ¡n 10 phÃºt
            setInterval(() => {
                const coDonCho = [...(duLieuGioHang.sanPham || []), ...(duLieuGioHang.thueMay || [])]
                    .some(x => x.TrangThai === 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n');
                if (coDonCho && !maTTDangMoGH) {
                    taiDuLieuGioHang();
                }
            }, 15000);

            const tabMacDinh = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('tab');
            if (tabMacDinh && ['san-pham', 'thue-may', 'su-kien'].includes(tabMacDinh)) {
                chuyenTab(tabMacDinh);

        function chuyenTab(tenTab) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.add('hidden'));
            document.getElementById(`panel-${tenTab}`).classList.remove('hidden');

            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
                btn.classList.add('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
                const badge = btn.querySelector('span:last-child');
                if (badge) {
                    badge.classList.remove('bg-white/20', 'text-white');
                    badge.classList.add('bg-gray-100', 'text-gray-700');
                }
            });

            const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${tenTab}`);
            activeBtn.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white');
            activeBtn.classList.remove('bg-white', 'text-gray-600', 'border', 'border-gray-100');
            const activeBadge = activeBtn.querySelector('span:last-child');
            if (activeBadge) {
                activeBadge.classList.add('bg-white/20', 'text-white');
                activeBadge.classList.remove('bg-gray-100', 'text-gray-700');
            }
        }

        async function taiDuLieuGioHang() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(currentUser.phone)}`);
                const result = await res.json();
                if (!result.success) return;

                duLieuGioHang = result;
                document.getElementById('count-san-pham').innerText = result.sanPham.length;
                document.getElementById('count-thue-may').innerText = result.thueMay.length;
                document.getElementById('count-su-kien').innerText = result.suKien.length;

                renderSanPham(result.sanPham);
                renderThueMay(result.thueMay);
                renderSuKien(result.suKien);

                if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') {
                    window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                }

                if (maTTDangMoGH) {
                    const btnCheck = document.getElementById('btn-xac-nhan-ck-gh');
                    const danhSachCheck = loaiDoiTuongQRGH === 'MAY' ? (result.thueMay || []) : (result.sanPham || []);

                            st.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-600 text-base"></i><span>ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n Ä‘á»§ 70% cÃ²n láº¡i! ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng Ä‘Ã£ hoÃ n táº¥t 100%.</span>`;
                            if (btnCheck) {
                                btnCheck.innerText = 'HoÃ n táº¥t & ÄÃ³ng';
                                btnCheck.onclick = dongModalQRGioHang;
                            }
                            maTTDangMoGH = '';
                        }
                    } else {
                        const daCoc = danhSachCheck.find(
                            x => x.MaThanhToan === maTTDangMoGH && ['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang giao hÃ ng', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n', 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh'].includes(x.TrangThai)
                        );
                        if (daCoc) {
                            dungCacBoDemQR();
                            const st = document.getElementById('gh-qr-status');
                            st.className = 'bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2';
                            st.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-600 text-base"></i><span>ÄÃ£ nháº­n tiá»n cá»c 30% thÃ nh cÃ´ng! ÄÆ¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»‘t.</span>`;
                            if (btnCheck) {
                                btnCheck.innerText = 'HoÃ n táº¥t & ÄÃ³ng';
                                btnCheck.onclick = dongModalQRGioHang;
                            }
                            maTTDangMoGH = '';
                        } else {
                            // Náº¿u Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Ã£ quÃ¡ 10 phÃºt vÃ  tá»± tráº£ vá» giá» hÃ ng thÃ¬ tá»± Ä‘Ã³ng QR vÃ  bÃ¡o khÃ¡ch

            } catch (err) {

        function dungCacBoDemQR() {
            if (timerQRGioHang) clearInterval(timerQRGioHang);
            if (timerCountdownGH) clearInterval(timerCountdownGH);
            timerQRGioHang = null;
            timerCountdownGH = null;
        }

        function taoBadgeTrangThai(trangThai, giayConLai = 0) {
            if (trangThai === 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n') {
                const textThoiGian = giayConLai > 0 ? ` â€¢ CÃ²n ${formatPhutGiay(giayConLai)}` : ' â€¢ Äang hoÃ n giá»...';
                return `<span class="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock mr-1"></i>Chá» cá»c 30%${textThoiGian}</span>`;
            }
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)') {
                return `<span class="bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-shield-check mr-1"></i>ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)</span>`;
            }
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh') {
                return `<span class="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1"></i>${trangThai}</span>`;
            }
            if (trangThai === 'Äang giao hÃ ng' || trangThai === 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n') {
                return `<span class="bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-truck-fast mr-1"></i>${trangThai}</span>`;
            }
            if (trangThai === 'ÄÃ£ há»§y') {
                return `<span class="bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-xmark mr-1"></i>${trangThai}</span>`;
            }
            return `<span class="bg-emerald-50 text-luaGreen border border-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full"><i class="fa-solid fa-check mr-1"></i>${trangThai}</span>`;
        }

        // ---------- 1. RENDER Sáº¢N PHáº¨M ----------
        function renderSanPham(ds) {
            const emptyBox = document.getElementById('empty-san-pham');
            const wrapGio = document.getElementById('wrap-san-pham-gio');
            const listGio = document.getElementById('list-san-pham-gio');
            const footer = document.getElementById('footer-san-pham');
            const wrapDaDat = document.getElementById('wrap-san-pham-da-dat');
            const listDaDat = document.getElementById('list-san-pham-da-dat');

            if (ds.length === 0) {
                wrapGio.classList.add('hidden');
                footer.classList.add('hidden');
                wrapDaDat.classList.add('hidden');
                emptyBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                emptyBox.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-12 text-center border border-gray-100">
                        <p class="text-gray-400 text-sm mb-3">Báº¡n chÆ°a thÃªm sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o vÃ o giá» hÃ ng.</p>
                        <a href="/san-pham.html" class="inline-block bg-luaGreen text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl">Äi chá»n giá»‘ng lÃºa, phÃ¢n, thuá»‘c</a>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            emptyBox.classList.add('hidden');

            const trongGio = ds.filter(sp => sp.TrangThai === 'Trong giá»');
            const daLenDon = ds.filter(sp => sp.TrangThai !== 'Trong giá»');

            document.getElementById('count-sp-da-dat').innerText = daLenDon.length;

            wrapGio.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (trongGio.length > 0) {
                let tongTienTrongGio = 0;
                listGio.innerHTML = trongGio.map(sp => {
                    const thanhTien = Number(sp.GiaTien) * Number(sp.SoLuong);
                    tongTienTrongGio += thanhTien;

                    return `

                const tienCocGio = Math.round(tongTienTrongGio * 0.3);
                footer.classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('tong-tien-san-pham').innerHTML = `${tongTienTrongGio.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ <span class="block text-xs font-bold text-luaGreen mt-0.5">Cá»c trÆ°á»›c 30% qua QR: ${tienCocGio.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘</span>`;
            } else {
                footer.classList.add('hidden');
                listGio.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="bg-white/70 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center border border-dashed border-gray-200 text-xs text-gray-400">
                        Hiá»‡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o Ä‘ang chá» Ä‘áº·t mua trong giá».

            }

            if (daLenDon.length > 0) {
                wrapDaDat.classList.remove('hidden');
                listDaDat.innerHTML = daLenDon.map(sp => {
                    const thanhTien = Number(sp.GiaTien) * Number(sp.SoLuong);
                    const tienCocItem = Number(sp.TienCoc || 0);
                    const conLai = Math.max(0, thanhTien - tienCocItem);
                    const soTienQRConLai = Number(sp.TongConLaiDon || conLai);
                    const badgeTrangThai = taoBadgeTrangThai(sp.TrangThai, sp.GiayConLai);

                    if (sp.TrangThai === 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n') {

                    } else if (sp.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng' || sp.TrangThai === 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)') {

                        hanhDongHtml = `

        function moModalQRGioHang(maTT, soTien, qrUrl, loaiQR = 'COC', noiDungHienThi = '', loaiDoiTuong = 'SP', giayConLai = 600) {
            maTTDangMoGH = maTT;
            loaiQRDangMoGH = loaiQR;
            loaiDoiTuongQRGH = loaiDoiTuong;

            const isCOD = loaiQR === 'COD';
            const tenDoiTuong = loaiDoiTuong === 'MAY' ? 'ThuÃª MÃ¡y ' : '';
            document.getElementById('gh-qr-title').innerText = isCOD
                ? `QuÃ©t QR Tráº£ Ná»‘t 70% ${tenDoiTuong}(SePay)`
                : `QuÃ©t QR Äáº·t Cá»c 30% ${tenDoiTuong}(SePay)`;
            document.getElementById('gh-qr-amount-label').innerText = isCOD
                ? 'Sá»‘ tiá»n cÃ²n láº¡i (70%):'
                : 'Sá»‘ tiá»n Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%):';

            document.getElementById('gh-qr-img').src = qrUrl;
            document.getElementById('gh-qr-tien-coc').innerText = Number(soTien).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('gh-qr-noi-dung').innerText = noiDungHienThi || maTT;

            const giayKhoiTao = Math.max(1, Number(giayConLai) || 600);
            hanKetThucQRMs = Date.now() + giayKhoiTao * 1000;

            const st = document.getElementById('gh-qr-status');
            st.className = 'bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-semibold flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1';
            st.innerHTML = `
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-luaYellow"></i>
                    <span>Äang chá» chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n... Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng cáº­p nháº­t ngay khi SePay bÃ¡o tiá»n vÃ o!</span>
                </div>
                ${!isCOD ? `<div class="text-[11px] text-rose-700 font-bold">Thá»i gian giá»¯ Ä‘Æ¡n / giá»¯ lá»‹ch cÃ²n láº¡i: <span id="gh-qr-countdown" class="font-mono text-sm bg-rose-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${formatPhutGiay(giayKhoiTao)}</span></div>` : ''}
            `;

            const btnCheck = document.getElementById('btn-xac-nhan-ck-gh');
            if (btnCheck) {
                btnCheck.innerText = 'TÃ´i Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n';
                btnCheck.onclick = kiemTraThuCongGH;
            }

            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-qr-gio-hang');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');

            dungCacBoDemQR();
            timerQRGioHang = setInterval(taiDuLieuGioHang, 3000);

            if (!isCOD) {
                timerCountdownGH = setInterval(() => {
                    const conLai = Math.ceil((hanKetThucQRMs - Date.now()) / 1000);
                    const cdEl = document.getElementById('gh-qr-countdown');
                    if (cdEl) cdEl.innerText = formatPhutGiay(conLai);

                    if (conLai <= 0) {
                        dongModalQRGioHang();
                        alert('â±ï¸ ÄÃ£ háº¿t 10 phÃºt thanh toÃ¡n! ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng / lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y Ä‘Ã£ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tráº£ vá» láº¡i giá» hÃ ng vÃ  má»Ÿ láº¡i ca trá»‘ng.');
                    }
                }, 1000);
            }
        }

        function moLaiQRDonHang(maTT, loaiQR = 'COC', loaiDoiTuong = 'SP') {
            const danhSach = loaiDoiTuong === 'MAY' ? (duLieuGioHang.thueMay || []) : (duLieuGioHang.sanPham || []);
            const item = danhSach.find(x => x.MaThanhToan === maTT);
            if (!item) {
                alert('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thÃ´ng tin thanh toÃ¡n cho má»¥c nÃ y!');
                return;
            }

            if (loaiQR === 'COD') {
                if (!item.QrUrlCOD) {
                    alert('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ QR thanh toÃ¡n pháº§n cÃ²n láº¡i!');
                    return;
                }
                const donGia = loaiDoiTuong === 'MAY' ? Number(item.GiaThue) : Number(item.GiaTien);
                const soLuong = loaiDoiTuong === 'MAY' ? Number(item.SoCong) : Number(item.SoLuong);
                const soTienConLai = item.TongConLaiDon || Math.max(0, donGia * soLuong - Number(item.TienCoc || 0));
                moModalQRGioHang(maTT, soTienConLai, item.QrUrlCOD, 'COD', item.MaThanhToanCOD || `${maTT} COD`, loaiDoiTuong, 600);
            } else {
                if (!item.QrUrl) {
                    alert('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ QR Ä‘áº·t cá»c cho má»¥c nÃ y!');
                    return;
                }
                if (Number(item.GiayConLai || 0) <= 0) {
                    alert('â±ï¸ ÄÆ¡n nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n 10 phÃºt giá»¯ Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c tráº£ vá» giá» hÃ ng!');
                    taiDuLieuGioHang();
                    return;
                }
                moModalQRGioHang(maTT, item.TongCocDon || item.TienCoc, item.QrUrl, 'COC', maTT, loaiDoiTuong, item.GiayConLai);
            }
        }

        function dongModalQRGioHang() {
            dungCacBoDemQR();
            maTTDangMoGH = '';
            loaiQRDangMoGH = 'COC';
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-qr-gio-hang');
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
            taiDuLieuGioHang();
        }

        function copyTextGH(text) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
            alert('ÄÃ£ sao chÃ©p: ' + text);
        }

        async function kiemTraThuCongGH() {
            if (!maTTDangMoGH) {
                dongModalQRGioHang();
                return;
            }
            const maDangCheck = maTTDangMoGH;
            const noiDungCheck = document.getElementById('gh-qr-noi-dung').innerText;
            await taiDuLieuGioHang();
            if (maDangCheck && maTTDangMoGH === maDangCheck) {
                alert('ChÆ°a ghi nháº­n giao dá»‹ch má»›i cho mÃ£ ' + noiDungCheck + '. Náº¿u báº¡n vá»«a chuyá»ƒn tiá»n, vui lÃ²ng Ä‘á»£i thÃªm vÃ i giÃ¢y rá»“i thá»­ láº¡i!');
            }
        }

        async function huyDonSanPham(maGH) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n nÃ y vÃ  Ä‘Æ°a sáº£n pháº©m trá»Ÿ láº¡i giá» hÃ ng khÃ´ng?')) return;

            try {
                const checkRes = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(currentUser.phone)}`);
                const checkData = await checkRes.json();
                if (checkData.success) {
                    const donHienTai = (checkData.sanPham || []).find(item => Number(item.MaGH) === Number(maGH));
                    const choPhepHuy = ['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)'];
                    if (!donHienTai || !choPhepHuy.includes(donHienTai.TrangThai)) {
                        alert(`âš ï¸ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y! ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng hiá»‡n Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn sang tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "${donHienTai ? donHienTai.TrangThai : 'má»›i'}".`);
                        taiDuLieuGioHang();
                        return;
                    }
                }

                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/don-hang/san-pham', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ maGH, trangThai: 'Trong giá»' })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert('âœ… ÄÃ£ há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  Ä‘Æ°a sáº£n pháº©m trá»Ÿ láº¡i giá» hÃ ng!');
                    taiDuLieuGioHang();
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng'));
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§!');
            }
        }

        async function doiSoLuongSP(maGH, soLuongMoi) {
            await fetch('/api/gio-hang/san-pham', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ maGH, soLuong: soLuongMoi })
            });
            taiDuLieuGioHang();
        }

        async function xoaSP(maGH) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('XÃ³a sáº£n pháº©m nÃ y khá»i giá» hÃ ng?')) return;
            await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/san-pham/${maGH}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            taiDuLieuGioHang();
        }

        async function xacNhanDatMua() {
            const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/san-pham/dat-mua', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ soDienThoai: currentUser.phone })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                await taiDuLieuGioHang();
                moModalQRGioHang(result.maThanhToan, result.tienCoc, result.qrUrl, 'COC', result.maThanhToan, 'SP', result.giayConLai || 600);
            } else {
                alert('âš ï¸ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘áº·t cá»c!'));
            }
        }

        // ---------- 2. RENDER THUÃŠ MÃY ----------
        function renderThueMay(ds) {
            const emptyBox = document.getElementById('empty-thue-may');
            const wrapGio = document.getElementById('wrap-thue-may-gio');
            const listGio = document.getElementById('list-thue-may-gio');
            const footer = document.getElementById('footer-thue-may');
            const wrapDaDat = document.getElementById('wrap-thue-may-da-dat');
            const listDaDat = document.getElementById('list-thue-may-da-dat');

            if (ds.length === 0) {
                wrapGio.classList.add('hidden');
                footer.classList.add('hidden');
                wrapDaDat.classList.add('hidden');
                emptyBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                emptyBox.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-12 text-center border border-gray-100">
                        <p class="text-gray-400 text-sm mb-3">TÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n hiá»‡n chÆ°a Ä‘áº·t thuÃª thiáº¿t bá»‹ mÃ¡y mÃ³c nÃ o.</p>
                        <a href="/thue-may.html" class="inline-block bg-luaGreen text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl">Xem danh sÃ¡ch mÃ¡y cho thuÃª</a>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            emptyBox.classList.add('hidden');

            const trongGioMay = ds.filter(m => m.TrangThai === 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n');
            const daLenDonMay = ds.filter(m => m.TrangThai !== 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n');

            document.getElementById('count-may-gio').innerText = trongGioMay.length;
            document.getElementById('count-may-da-dat').innerText = daLenDonMay.length;

            wrapGio.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (trongGioMay.length > 0) {
                let tongThue = 0;

        async function huyDonThueMay(maDatMay) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n há»§y lá»‹ch thuÃª nÃ y vÃ  Ä‘Æ°a dá»‹ch vá»¥ trá»Ÿ láº¡i giá» hÃ ng khÃ´ng?')) return;

            try {
                const checkRes = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(currentUser.phone)}`);
                const checkData = await checkRes.json();
                if (checkData.success) {
                    const phieuHienTai = (checkData.thueMay || []).find(item => Number(item.MaDatMay) === Number(maDatMay));
                    const choPhepHuyMay = ['Chá» thanh toÃ¡n', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thuÃª / ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t lá»‹ch'];
                    if (!phieuHienTai || !choPhepHuyMay.includes(phieuHienTai.TrangThai)) {
                        alert(`âš ï¸ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y! Lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y hiá»‡n Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn sang tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "${phieuHienTai ? phieuHienTai.TrangThai : 'má»›i'}".`);
                        taiDuLieuGioHang();
                        return;
                    }
                }

                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/don-hang/thue-may', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ maDatMay, trangThai: 'Chá» xÃ¡c nháº­n' })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert('âœ… ÄÃ£ há»§y lá»‹ch vÃ  Ä‘Æ°a dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y trá»Ÿ láº¡i giá» hÃ ng!');
                    taiDuLieuGioHang();
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y'));
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§!');
            }
        }

        async function capNhatMay(maDatMay, thayDoi) {
            const hienTai = (duLieuGioHang.thueMay || []).find(x => Number(x.MaDatMay) === Number(maDatMay));
            if (!hienTai) return;

            const payload = {
                maDatMay,
                soCong: thayDoi.soCong !== undefined ? thayDoi.soCong : hienTai.SoCong,
                ngayThue: thayDoi.ngayThue !== undefined ? thayDoi.ngayThue : (hienTai.NgayThue || ''),
                khungGio: thayDoi.khungGio !== undefined ? thayDoi.khungGio : (hienTai.KhungGio || ''),
                ghiChu: thayDoi.ghiChu !== undefined ? thayDoi.ghiChu : (hienTai.GhiChu || '')
            };

            await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(payload)
            });
            taiDuLieuGioHang();
        }

        async function xoaMay(maDatMay) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('Báº¡n muá»‘n xÃ³a dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ y khá»i giá»?')) return;
            await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/thue-may/${maDatMay}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            taiDuLieuGioHang();
        }

        async function xacNhanLichThue() {
            const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may/xac-nhan', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ soDienThoai: currentUser.phone })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                await taiDuLieuGioHang();
                moModalQRGioHang(result.maThanhToan, result.tienCoc, result.qrUrl, 'COC', result.maThanhToan, 'MAY', result.giayConLai || 600);
            } else {
                alert('âš ï¸ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº¡o phiáº¿u Ä‘áº·t cá»c thuÃª mÃ¡y!'));
            }
        }

        // ---------- 3. RENDER Sá»° KIá»†N ÄÃƒ ÄÄ‚NG KÃ ----------
        function renderSuKien(ds) {
            const container = document.getElementById('list-su-kien');

            if (ds.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="col-span-2 bg-white rounded-2xl p-12 text-center border border-gray-100">
                        <p class="text-gray-400 text-sm mb-3">Báº¡n chÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tham gia sá»± kiá»‡n / há»™i tháº£o nÃ o.</p>
                        <a href="/su-kien.html" class="inline-block bg-luaGreen text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl">Xem lá»‹ch sá»± kiá»‡n sáº¯p diá»…n ra</a>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = ds.map(sk => {
                const ngayDK = sk.NgayDangKy ? new Date(sk.NgayDangKy).toLocaleString('vi-VN') : 'ÄÃ£ ghi nháº­n';
                return `
                    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4">
                        <div>
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                                <span class="bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1"></i> ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tham gia
                                </span>
                                <button onclick="huySuKien(${sk.MaDK})" class="text-xs text-red-500 hover:underline font-semibold">
                                    Há»§y tham gia
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-base leading-snug mb-3">${sk.TieuDeSuKien}</h3>
                            <div class="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-gray-600">
                                <p><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-days text-luaGreen w-4"></i> Thá»i gian tá»• chá»©c: <strong>${sk.ThoiGian || 'Theo lá»‹ch ban tá»• chá»©c'}</strong></p>
                                <p><i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot text-amber-600 w-4"></i> Äá»‹a Ä‘iá»ƒm: <strong>${sk.DiaDiem || 'Äang cáº­p nháº­t'}</strong></p>
                                <p><i class="fa-solid fa-clock text-gray-400 w-4"></i> Thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½: ${ngayDK}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        async function huySuKien(maDK) {
            if (!await xacNhanThongBao('Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n há»§y Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tham gia sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ y?')) return;
            const res = await fetch(`/api/dang-ky-su-kien/${maDK}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                alert('ÄÃ£ há»§y Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ sá»± kiá»‡n!');
                taiDuLieuGioHang();
            }
        }

<script>
    window.layTaiKhoanHeader = function() {
        const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
        if (raw && !sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
            sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', raw);
        }
        return raw;
    };

    window.dangXuatHeader = function() {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('luaVangUser');
        localStorage.removeItem('luaVangUser');
        window.location.href = '/trang-chu.html';
    };

    window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang = async function() {
        const badge = document.getElementById('cart-badge');
        const loggedUser = window.layTaiKhoanHeader();
        if (!badge) return;

        if (!loggedUser) {
            badge.innerText = '0';
            return;
        }

        try {
            const user = JSON.parse(loggedUser);
            if (!user.phone || user.role === 'admin') {
                badge.innerText = '0';
                return;
            }
            const res = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(user.phone)}`);
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                const tongMuc = (data.sanPham?.length || 0) + (data.thueMay?.length || 0) + (data.suKien?.length || 0);
                badge.innerText = tongMuc;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Lá»—i cáº­p nháº­t badge giá» hÃ ng:', e);
        }
    };

    window.toXanhMenuHienTai = function() {
        const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
        document.querySelectorAll('#nav-menu .nav-link').forEach(link => {
            const target = link.getAttribute('data-target') || '';
            const slug = target.replace('.html', '');
            const isHome = (currentPath === '/' || currentPath === '') && target === 'trang-chu.html';
            const isActive = isHome || (slug && currentPath.includes(slug));

            if (isActive) {
                link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                link.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
            } else {
                link.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                link.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
            }
        });
    };

    window.capNhatHeader = function() {
        const loggedUser = window.layTaiKhoanHeader();
        const authGuest = document.getElementById('auth-guest');
        const authUser = document.getElementById('auth-user');
        const userNameDisplay = document.getElementById('user-name-display');
        const navMenu = document.getElementById('nav-menu');
        const cartLink = document.getElementById('header-cart-link');
        const profileLink = document.getElementById('header-profile-link');

        if (!authGuest || !authUser) return;

        if (loggedUser) {
            try {
                const user = JSON.parse(loggedUser);
                authGuest.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
                authUser.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
                if (userNameDisplay) userNameDisplay.innerText = user.name;

                if (user.role === 'admin' && navMenu) {
                    if (cartLink) cartLink.style.display = 'none';
                    if (profileLink) profileLink.href = '/admin-trang-chu.html';
                    const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
                    const adminLinks = [
                        { href: '/admin-trang-chu.html', text: 'Trang chá»§ Admin' },
                        { href: '/admin-san-pham.html', text: 'QL Sáº£n pháº©m' },
                        { href: '/admin-thue-may.html', text: 'QL ThuÃª mÃ¡y' },
                        { href: '/admin-don-hang.html', text: 'QL ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng' },
                        { href: '/admin-nong-dan.html', text: 'QL NÃ´ng dÃ¢n' },
                        { href: '/admin-su-kien.html', text: 'QL Sá»± kiá»‡n' },
                        { href: '/admin-tu-van.html', text: 'QL TÆ° váº¥n' }
                    ];

                    navMenu.innerHTML = adminLinks.map(item => {
                        const isActive = currentPath.includes(item.href);
                        const cls = isActive
                            ? 'bg-luaGreen text-white font-bold shadow'
                            : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-luaGreen';
                        return `<a href="${item.href}" class="nav-link px-3 py-2 rounded-xl transition ${cls}">${item.text}</a>`;
                    }).join('');
                } else {
                    window.toXanhMenuHienTai();
                    if (window.location.pathname.includes('ho-so.html') && profileLink) {
                        profileLink.classList.remove('bg-emerald-50', 'border-emerald-200');
                        profileLink.classList.add('bg-amber-100', 'border-amber-300', 'ring-2', 'ring-amber-200');
                    }
                    window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
            }
        } else {
            authGuest.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
            authUser.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
            window.toXanhMenuHienTai();
            window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
        }
    };

    window.capNhatHeader();
</script>

    <script>
        let currentUser = null;

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function () {
            const rawUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n Ä‘á»ƒ xem há»“ sÆ¡!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            if (!sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', rawUser);
            }

            currentUser = JSON.parse(rawUser);

            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(res => res.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }
                });

            taiHoSoCaNhan();
        });

        function toggleMatKhau(inputId, btn) {
            const inp = document.getElementById(inputId);
            const isPass = inp.type === 'password';
            inp.type = isPass ? 'text' : 'password';
            btn.innerHTML = isPass ? '<i class="fa-regular fa-eye-slash"></i>' : '<i class="fa-regular fa-eye"></i>';
        }

        async function taiHoSoCaNhan() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/nong-dan/ho-so/${encodeURIComponent(currentUser.phone)}`);
                const result = await res.json();
                if (!result.success) return;

                const d = result.data;
                const tk = result.thongKe || {};

                document.getElementById('profile-title-name').innerText = d.HoTen || currentUser.name;
                document.getElementById('profile-title-phone').innerText = d.SoDienThoai;
                document.getElementById('input-hs-sdt').value = d.SoDienThoai;
                document.getElementById('input-hs-ten').value = d.HoTen || '';
                document.getElementById('input-hs-diachi').value = d.DiaChi || '';

                document.getElementById('stat-don-sp').innerText = tk.soDonSP || 0;
                document.getElementById('stat-lich-may').innerText = tk.soLichMay || 0;
                document.getElementById('stat-su-kien').innerText = tk.soSuKien || 0;
                document.getElementById('stat-chi-tieu').innerText = Number(tk.tongChiTieu || 0).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';

                currentUser.name = d.HoTen;
                currentUser.diaChi = d.DiaChi || '';
                const serialized = JSON.stringify(currentUser);
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', serialized);
                if (localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                    localStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', serialized);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i há»“ sÆ¡:', err);
            }
        }

        async function luuHoSoCaNhan(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const hoTen = document.getElementById('input-hs-ten').value.trim();
            const diaChi = document.getElementById('input-hs-diachi').value.trim();

            const res = await fetch('/api/nong-dan/ho-so', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    soDienThoai: currentUser.phone,
                    hoTen,
                    diaChi
                })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                alert('âœ… ' + result.message);
                const headerName = document.getElementById('user-name-display');
                if (headerName) headerName.innerText = hoTen;
                taiHoSoCaNhan();
            } else {
                alert('âŒ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t há»“ sÆ¡'));
            }
        }

        async function doiMatKhauCaNhan(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const matKhauCu = document.getElementById('input-mk-cu').value;
            const matKhauMoi = document.getElementById('input-mk-moi').value;
            const xacNhan = document.getElementById('input-mk-xac-nhan').value;

            if (matKhauMoi !== xacNhan) {
                alert('âš ï¸ Máº­t kháº©u má»›i vÃ  xÃ¡c nháº­n máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng khá»›p!');
                return;
            }

            const res = await fetch('/api/nong-dan/doi-mat-khau', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    soDienThoai: currentUser.phone,
                    matKhauCu,
                    matKhauMoi
                })
            });
            const result = await res.json();
            if (result.success) {
                alert('âœ… ' + result.message);
                document.getElementById('input-mk-cu').value = '';
                document.getElementById('input-mk-moi').value = '';
                document.getElementById('input-mk-xac-nhan').value = '';
            } else {
                alert('âŒ ' + (result.message || 'Äá»•i máº­t kháº©u tháº¥t báº¡i'));

        let tatCaSanPham = [];

        function lamSachMoTa(text) {
            return String(text || '')
                .replace(/\$\s*\\ge\s*([^$]+)\$/g, 'â‰¥ $1')
                .replace(/\$\s*\\le\s*([^$]+)\$/g, 'â‰¤ $1');
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            // Fetch header chung
            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(response => response.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;

                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }

                    const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(link => {
                        if (currentPath.includes(link.getAttribute('data-target'))) {
                            link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                            link.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        } else {
                            link.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        }
                    });
                });

            // Táº£i danh sÃ¡ch sáº£n pháº©m tháº­t tá»« CSDL
            taiDanhSachSanPham();
        });

        async function taiDanhSachSanPham() {
            try {
                let response = await fetch('/api/san-pham');
                let result = await response.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    tatCaSanPham = result.data;
                    hienThiSanPham(tatCaSanPham);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i khi táº£i sáº£n pháº©m:', err);
            }
        }

        function xemChiTietSanPham(maSanPham) {
            window.location.href = `/chi-tiet.html?loai=san-pham&id=${maSanPham}`;
        }

        function hienThiSanPham(danhSach) {
            const container = document.getElementById('danh-sach-san-pham');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            if (danhSach.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-3 text-center py-16 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">ChÆ°a cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u.</div>`;
                return;

                let bgClass = 'bg-emerald-50/60';

                    iconClass = 'fa-sack-dollar text-amber-600';
                    bgClass = 'bg-amber-50/60';
                    badgeClass = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';
                    tagText = 'PhÃ¢n bÃ³n';

                    iconClass = 'fa-seedling text-emerald-600';
                    bgClass = 'bg-emerald-50/60';
                    badgeClass = 'bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen';
                    tagText = 'Giá»‘ng lÃºa';

                let hinhAnhHtml = `
                    <div class="${bgClass} p-8 flex items-center justify-center h-48">
                        <i class="fa-solid ${iconClass.split(' ')[0]} text-6xl ${iconClass.split(' ')[1]}"></i>

        // Lá»c sáº£n pháº©m theo danh má»¥c bÃªn trÃ¡i
        function locSanPham(danhMuc, element) {
            const sidebarItems = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item');
            sidebarItems.forEach(i => {
                i.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow-md');
                i.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:bg-emerald-50', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
            });
            element.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow-md');
            element.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:bg-emerald-50', 'hover:text-luaGreen');

            if (danhMuc === 'Táº¥t cáº£') {
                hienThiSanPham(tatCaSanPham);
            } else {
                let filtered = tatCaSanPham.filter(sp => sp.DanhMuc === danhMuc);
                hienThiSanPham(filtered);

    const queue = [];
    let active = false;

    function classify(message) {
        const normalized = message.toLocaleLowerCase('vi');
        if (['lá»—i', 'tháº¥t báº¡i', 'sai', 'khÃ´ng thá»ƒ', 'âŒ'].some(term => normalized.includes(term))) {
            return {
                title: 'CÃ³ lá»—i xáº£y ra',
                iconColor: '#dc2626',
                iconBackground: '#fef2f2',
                icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 8v5m0 3h.01M10.3 3.9 1.8 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h17a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.9a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>'
            };
        }
        if (['thÃ nh cÃ´ng', 'hoÃ n táº¥t', 'âœ…'].some(term => normalized.includes(term))) {
            return {
                title: 'ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
                iconColor: '#064e3b',
                iconBackground: '#ecfdf5',
                icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m5 12 4 4L19 6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>'
            };
        }
        return {
            title: 'LÃºa VÃ ng thÃ´ng bÃ¡o',
            iconColor: '#d97706',
            iconBackground: '#fffbeb',
            icon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M12 11v5m0-8h.01" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>'
        };
    }

    function cleanMessage(message) {
        return message.replace(/^\s*(?:(?:âœ…|âŒ|âš ï¸|â±ï¸)\s*)+/u, '').trim();
    }

    function enqueue(message, isConfirmation) {
        return new Promise(resolve => {
            queue.push({
                message: message == null ? '' : String(message),
                isConfirmation,
                resolve
            });
            renderNext();
        });
    }

    function renderNext() {
        if (active || queue.length === 0) return;
        active = true;

        const item = queue.shift();
        const meta = classify(item.message);
        const destructive = /xÃ³a|há»§y|huy|delete|cancel/i.test(item.message);
        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs lv-notice-backdrop';
        overlay.setAttribute('role', 'presentation');

        const dialog = document.createElement('section');
        dialog.className = 'rounded-3xl max-w-md w-[92%] p-6 shadow-2xl border border-[#064e3b]/15 lv-notice-card';
        dialog.setAttribute('role', 'alertdialog');
        dialog.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
        dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'lv-notice-title');
        dialog.setAttribute('aria-describedby', 'lv-notice-message');

        const icon = document.createElement('div');
        icon.className = 'flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full';
        icon.style.color = meta.iconColor;
        icon.style.backgroundColor = meta.iconBackground;
        icon.innerHTML = meta.icon;
        icon.firstElementChild.setAttribute('width', '28');
        icon.firstElementChild.setAttribute('height', '28');

        const title = document.createElement('h2');
        title.id = 'lv-notice-title';
        title.className = 'mt-4 text-xl font-extrabold text-[#064e3b]';
        title.textContent = item.isConfirmation && destructive ? 'XÃ¡c nháº­n thao tÃ¡c' : meta.title;

        const body = document.createElement('p');
        body.id = 'lv-notice-message';
        body.className = 'lv-notice-message mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-gray-700';
        body.textContent = cleanMessage(item.message);

        const actions = document.createElement('div');
        actions.className = 'lv-notice-actions';

        const cancel = item.isConfirmation ? document.createElement('button') : null;
        if (cancel) {
            cancel.type = 'button';
            cancel.className = 'lv-notice-button lv-notice-cancel';
            cancel.textContent = 'Há»§y bá»';
        }

        const primary = document.createElement('button');
        primary.type = 'button';
        primary.className = `lv-notice-button ${item.isConfirmation && destructive ? 'lv-notice-danger' : 'lv-notice-primary'}`;
        primary.textContent = item.isConfirmation ? 'XÃ¡c nháº­n' : 'ÄÃ³ng';

        let closed = false;
        function close(result) {
            if (closed) return;
            closed = true;
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
            overlay.remove();
            active = false;
            item.resolve(result);
            renderNext();
        }

        function onKeyDown(event) {
            if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                event.preventDefault();
                close(item.isConfirmation ? false : undefined);
            } else if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                event.preventDefault();
                close(item.isConfirmation ? true : undefined);
            }
        }

        if (cancel) {
            cancel.addEventListener('click', () => close(false));
            actions.appendChild(cancel);
        }
        primary.addEventListener('click', () => close(item.isConfirmation ? true : undefined));
        actions.appendChild(primary);

        dialog.append(icon, title, body, actions);
        overlay.appendChild(dialog);
        (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(overlay);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
        (cancel || primary).focus();
    }

    window.hienThongBao = function (noiDung) {
        return enqueue(noiDung, false);
    };

    window.xacNhanThongBao = function (noiDung) {
        return enqueue(noiDung, true);
    };

    window.alert = function (msg) {
        return window.hienThongBao(msg);
    };
})();

    <!-- SCRIPT FETCH HEADER & Dá»® LIá»†U Tá»ª CSDL -->
    <script>
        const DANH_SACH_KHUNG_GIO = [
            'SÃ¡ng sá»›m (06:00 - 09:00)',
            'Buá»•i sÃ¡ng (09:00 - 12:00)',
            'Buá»•i chiá»u (13:30 - 16:30)',
            'Cáº£ ngÃ y (06:00 - 16:30)'
        ];

        let tatCaThueMay = [];
        let mayDangChon = null;
        let lichDaDatCuaMay = [];
        let timerQRThueMay = null;
        let timerCountdownTM = null;
        let hanKetThucTM = 0;
        let maTTDangMoTM = '';
        let sdtDangMoTM = '';

        function formatPhutGiayTM(tongGiay) {
            const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tongGiay) || 0));
            const mm = String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
            const ss = String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0');
            return `${mm}:${ss}`;
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(response => response.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;

                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) {
                        eval(script.innerText);
                    }

                    const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(link => {
                        if (currentPath.includes(link.getAttribute('data-target'))) {
                            link.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow');
                            link.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        } else {
                            link.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
                        }
                    });
                });

            taiDanhSachThueMay();
        });

        async function taiDanhSachThueMay() {
            try {
                let response = await fetch('/api/thue-may');
                let result = await response.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    tatCaThueMay = result.data;
                    hienThiThueMay(tatCaThueMay);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i khi táº£i danh sÃ¡ch thuÃª mÃ¡y:', err);
            }
        }

        function xemChiTietThueMay(maMay) {
            window.location.href = `/chi-tiet.html?loai=thue-may&id=${maMay}`;
        }

        function hienThiThueMay(danhSach) {
            const container = document.getElementById('danh-sach-thue-may');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            if (danhSach.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-3 text-center py-16 text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y nÃ o trong cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u.</div>`;
                return;
            }

            danhSach.forEach(item => {
                let iconClass = 'fa-tractor text-amber-600';
                let bgClass = 'bg-amber-50/60';
                let badgeClass = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';

                let loaiMayText = item.LoaiMay || 'ThuÃª mÃ¡y';
                if (loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('xáº¡ phÃ¢n') || loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('giá»‘ng')) {
                    iconClass = 'fa-seedling text-emerald-600';
                    bgClass = 'bg-emerald-50/60';
                    badgeClass = 'bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen';
                } else if (loaiMayText.toLowerCase().includes('phun thuá»‘c')) {
                    iconClass = 'fa-helicopter text-amber-600';
                    bgClass = 'bg-amber-50/60';
                    badgeClass = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';
                }

                let hinhAnhHtml = `
                    <div class="${bgClass} p-8 flex items-center justify-center h-48">
                        <i class="fa-solid ${iconClass.split(' ')[0]} text-6xl ${iconClass.split(' ')[1]}"></i>
                    </div>
                `;

                if (item.HinhAnh && item.HinhAnh.trim() !== '' && item.HinhAnh !== 'default.jpg') {
                    hinhAnhHtml = `
                        <div class="h-48 overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center">
                            <img src="/hinh-anh/${item.HinhAnh}" alt="${item.TenMay}" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-300" onerror="this.parentElement.innerHTML='<div class=\\'${bgClass} p-8 flex items-center justify-center h-full w-full\\'><i class=\\'fa-solid ${iconClass}\\' style=\\'font-size: 3.5rem;\\'></i></div>'">
                        </div>
                    `;
                }

                let giaFormatted = Number(item.GiaThue).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
                let donVi = item.DonViTinh ? ` / ${item.DonViTinh.toLowerCase()}` : ' / cÃ´ng';
                let tenAnToan = String(item.TenMay || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");

                let card = `
                    <div onclick="xemChiTietThueMay(${item.MaMay})" class="product-card cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between group">
                        ${hinhAnhHtml}
                        <div class="p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
                            <div>
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                                    <span class="inline-block ${badgeClass} text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md">${loaiMayText}</span>
                                    <span class="text-xs text-luaGreen font-semibold opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition">Chi tiáº¿t â†’</span>
                                </div>
                                <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800 group-hover:text-luaGreen transition text-base mb-1">${item.TenMay}</h3>
                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-3 line-clamp-2">${item.MoTa || 'Dá»‹ch vá»¥ mÃ¡y mÃ³c nÃ´ng nghiá»‡p hiá»‡n Ä‘áº¡i, phá»¥c vá»¥ nhanh chÃ³ng.'}</p>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <p class="text-luaYellow font-black text-lg mb-3">${giaFormatted}${donVi}</p>
                                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                                    <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); thueNgay(${item.MaMay}, '${tenAnToan}')" class="bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen font-bold py-2.5 px-2 rounded-xl transition text-xs">
                                        + ThÃªm giá»
                                    </button>
                                    <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); moModalDatThue(${item.MaMay})" class="bg-luaGreen hover:bg-emerald-900 text-white font-bold py-2.5 px-2 rounded-xl transition shadow text-xs">
                                        Äáº·t thuÃª ngay
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                container.innerHTML += card;
            });
        }

        function locThueMay(loaiMay, element) {
            const sidebarItems = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item');
            sidebarItems.forEach(i => {
                i.classList.remove('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow-md');
                i.classList.add('text-gray-600', 'hover:bg-emerald-50', 'hover:text-luaGreen');
            });
            element.classList.add('bg-luaGreen', 'text-white', 'font-bold', 'shadow-md');
            element.classList.remove('text-gray-600', 'hover:bg-emerald-50', 'hover:text-luaGreen');

            if (loaiMay === 'Táº¥t cáº£') {
                hienThiThueMay(tatCaThueMay);
            } else {
                let filtered = tatCaThueMay.filter(item => item.LoaiMay && item.LoaiMay.toLowerCase().includes(loaiMay.toLowerCase()));
                hienThiThueMay(filtered);
            }
        }

        async function thueNgay(maMay, tenMay) {
            const userData = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!userData) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘áº·t thuÃª mÃ¡y!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }

            const user = JSON.parse(userData);
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        soDienThoai: user.phone,
                        maMay: maMay,
                        soCong: 1
                    })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert(`âœ… ÄÃ£ thÃªm dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª "${tenMay}" vÃ o giá» cá»§a ${user.name}!`);
                    if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') {
                        window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                    }
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + result.message);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n server!');
            }
        }

        // ================= Äáº¶T THUÃŠ NHANH & Cá»ŒC 30% SEPAY =================
        async function moModalDatThue(maMay) {
            const userData = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!userData) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘áº·t thuÃª mÃ¡y!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }

            mayDangChon = tatCaThueMay.find(x => Number(x.MaMay) === Number(maMay));
            if (!mayDangChon) return;

            document.getElementById('dt-ten-may').innerText = mayDangChon.TenMay;
            document.getElementById('dt-gia-may').innerText = `${Number(mayDangChon.GiaThue).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} Ä‘ / ${mayDangChon.DonViTinh || 'cÃ´ng'}`;
            document.getElementById('dt-so-cong').value = 1;

            // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng Ä‘iá»n Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ ruá»™ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cá»§a nÃ´ng dÃ¢n
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);
            const inputGhiChu = document.getElementById('dt-ghi-chu');
            inputGhiChu.value = user.diaChi || '';

            if (!inputGhiChu.value && user.phone) {
                fetch(`/api/nong-dan/ho-so/${encodeURIComponent(user.phone)}`)
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(hs => {
                        if (hs.success && hs.data && hs.data.DiaChi) {
                            inputGhiChu.value = hs.data.DiaChi;
                            user.diaChi = hs.data.DiaChi;
                            sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', JSON.stringify(user));
                            if (localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                                localStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', JSON.stringify(user));
                            }
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(() => {});
            }

            const now = new Date();
            const homNay = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
            const inputNgay = document.getElementById('dt-ngay-thue');
            inputNgay.min = homNay;
            inputNgay.value = homNay;

            tinhTienDatThue();

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/thue-may/${maMay}/lich-da-dat`);
                const data = await res.json();
                lichDaDatCuaMay = data.success ? (data.lichDaDat || []) : [];
            } catch (e) {
                lichDaDatCuaMay = [];
            }

            capNhatKhungGioTrong();

            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-dat-thue-nhanh');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
        }

        function dongModalDatThue() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-dat-thue-nhanh');
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
        }

        function tinhTienDatThue() {
            if (!mayDangChon) return;
            const cong = Math.max(1, parseInt(document.getElementById('dt-so-cong').value) || 1);
            const tong = Number(mayDangChon.GiaThue || 0) * cong;
            const coc = Math.round(tong * 0.3);
            document.getElementById('dt-tong-tien').innerText = tong.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('dt-tien-coc').innerText = coc.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
        }

        function kiemTraTrungGio(gioA, gioB) {
            if (!gioA || !gioB) return false;
            if (gioA === gioB) return true;
            if (gioA.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y') || gioB.startsWith('Cáº£ ngÃ y')) return true;
            return false;
        }

        function capNhatKhungGioTrong() {
            const ngayChon = document.getElementById('dt-ngay-thue').value;
            const selectGio = document.getElementById('dt-khung-gio');
            const goiY = document.getElementById('dt-goi-y-lich');

            const daDatTrongNgay = lichDaDatCuaMay.filter(x => x.NgayThue === ngayChon);
            let soCaBan = 0;

            selectGio.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Chá»n khung giá» --</option>` + DANH_SACH_KHUNG_GIO.map(kg => {
                const biTrung = daDatTrongNgay.some(d => kiemTraTrungGio(d.KhungGio, kg));
                if (biTrung) {
                    soCaBan++;
                    return `<option value="${kg}" disabled>${kg} (ÄÃ£ cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i Ä‘áº·t)</option>`;
                }
                return `<option value="${kg}">${kg}</option>`;
            }).join('');

            if (soCaBan > 0) {
                goiY.innerHTML = `<span class="text-amber-700 font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info mr-1"></i>NgÃ y ${ngayChon} cÃ³ ${soCaBan} khung giá» Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘áº·t trÆ°á»›c.</span>`;
            } else {
                goiY.innerHTML = `<span class="text-emerald-700"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1"></i>NgÃ y ${ngayChon} hiá»‡n Ä‘ang trá»‘ng toÃ n bá»™ khung giá».</span>`;
            }
        }

        async function guiDatThueNgay() {
            const userData = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!userData || !mayDangChon) return;
            const user = JSON.parse(userData);

            const ngayThue = document.getElementById('dt-ngay-thue').value;
            const khungGio = document.getElementById('dt-khung-gio').value;
            const soCong = Math.max(1, parseInt(document.getElementById('dt-so-cong').value) || 1);
            const ghiChu = document.getElementById('dt-ghi-chu').value.trim();

            if (!ngayThue || !khungGio) {
                alert('Vui lÃ²ng chá»n Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ NgÃ y thuÃª vÃ  Khung giá»!');
                return;
            }

            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/thue-may', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        soDienThoai: user.phone,
                        maMay: mayDangChon.MaMay,
                        soCong,
                        ngayThue,
                        khungGio,
                        ghiChu,
                        trangThai: 'Chá» thanh toÃ¡n'
                    })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    dongModalDatThue();
                    if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') {
                        window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                    }
                    moModalQRThueMay(user.phone, result.maThanhToan, result.tienCoc, result.qrUrl, result.giayConLai || 600);
                } else {
                    alert('âš ï¸ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ giá»¯ lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y!'));
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§!');
            }
        }

        function dungCacBoDemTM() {
            if (timerQRThueMay) clearInterval(timerQRThueMay);
            if (timerCountdownTM) clearInterval(timerCountdownTM);
            timerQRThueMay = null;
            timerCountdownTM = null;
        }

        function moModalQRThueMay(sdt, maTT, tienCoc, qrUrl, giayConLai = 600) {
            sdtDangMoTM = sdt;
            maTTDangMoTM = maTT;

            const giayKhoiTao = Math.max(1, Number(giayConLai) || 600);
            hanKetThucTM = Date.now() + giayKhoiTao * 1000;

            document.getElementById('tm-qr-img').src = qrUrl;
            document.getElementById('tm-qr-tien-coc').innerText = Number(tienCoc).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
            document.getElementById('tm-qr-noi-dung').innerText = maTT;

            const st = document.getElementById('tm-qr-status');
            st.className = 'bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-semibold flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1';
            st.innerHTML = `
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-luaYellow"></i>
                    <span>Äang chá» chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n... Khung giá» Ä‘Æ°á»£c giá»¯ trong 10 phÃºt!</span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-[11px] text-rose-700 font-bold">
                    Thá»i gian giá»¯ lá»‹ch cÃ²n láº¡i: <span id="tm-qr-countdown" class="font-mono text-sm bg-rose-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${formatPhutGiayTM(giayKhoiTao)}</span>
                </div>
            `;

            const btnCheck = document.getElementById('btn-xac-nhan-ck-tm');
            if (btnCheck) {
                btnCheck.innerText = 'TÃ´i Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn khoáº£n';
                btnCheck.onclick = kiemTraThuCongTM;
            }

            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-qr-thue-may');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');

            dungCacBoDemTM();
            timerQRThueMay = setInterval(kiemTraTrangThaiCocTM, 3000);
            timerCountdownTM = setInterval(() => {
                const conLai = Math.ceil((hanKetThucTM - Date.now()) / 1000);
                const cdEl = document.getElementById('tm-qr-countdown');
                if (cdEl) cdEl.innerText = formatPhutGiayTM(conLai);

                if (conLai <= 0) {
                    alert('â±ï¸ ÄÃ£ háº¿t 10 phÃºt giá»¯ lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y! Phiáº¿u thuÃª Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c tráº£ vá» giá» hÃ ng vÃ  má»Ÿ láº¡i khung giá» trá»‘ng.');
                    dongModalQRThueMay();
                }
            }, 1000);
        }

        async function kiemTraTrangThaiCocTM() {
            if (!maTTDangMoTM || !sdtDangMoTM) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/gio-hang/${encodeURIComponent(sdtDangMoTM)}`);
                const result = await res.json();
                if (!result.success) return;

                const daCoc = (result.thueMay || []).find(
                    x => x.MaThanhToan === maTTDangMoTM && ['ÄÃ£ Ä‘áº·t cá»c (30%)', 'Äang thá»±c hiá»‡n', 'ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n & HoÃ n thÃ nh'].includes(x.TrangThai)
                );
                if (daCoc) {
                    dungCacBoDemTM();
                    const st = document.getElementById('tm-qr-status');
                    st.className = 'bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2';
                    st.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check text-emerald-600 text-base"></i><span>ÄÃ£ nháº­n tiá»n cá»c 30% thÃ nh cÃ´ng! Lá»‹ch thuÃª mÃ¡y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»‘t.</span>`;

                    const btnCheck = document.getElementById('btn-xac-nhan-ck-tm');
                    if (btnCheck) {
                        btnCheck.innerText = 'HoÃ n táº¥t & Xem lá»‹ch';
                        btnCheck.onclick = dongModalQRThueMay;
                    }
                    maTTDangMoTM = '';
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        async function kiemTraThuCongTM() {
            if (!maTTDangMoTM) {
                dongModalQRThueMay();
                return;
            }
            const maCheck = maTTDangMoTM;
            await kiemTraTrangThaiCocTM();
            if (maCheck && maTTDangMoTM === maCheck) {
                alert('ChÆ°a ghi nháº­n giao dá»‹ch má»›i cho mÃ£ ' + maCheck + '. Náº¿u báº¡n vá»«a chuyá»ƒn tiá»n, vui lÃ²ng Ä‘á»£i thÃªm vÃ i giÃ¢y rá»“i thá»­ láº¡i!');
            }
        }

        function dongModalQRThueMay() {
            dungCacBoDemTM();
            maTTDangMoTM = '';
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-qr-thue-may');
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
            window.location.href = '/gio-hang.html?tab=thue-may';
        }

        function copyTextTM(text) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
            alert('ÄÃ£ sao chÃ©p: ' + text);
        }
    </script>

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            // Náº¡p Header dÃ¹ng chung
            fetch('/header.html')

        async function taiDuLieuTrangChu() {
            try {
                const [resSP, resMay, resSK] = await Promise.all([
                    fetch('/api/san-pham').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({ success: false, data: [] })),
                    fetch('/api/thue-may').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({ success: false, data: [] })),
                    fetch('/api/su-kien').then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({ success: false, data: [] }))
                ]);

                const dsSanPham = resSP.success ? (resSP.data || []) : [];
                const dsThueMay = resMay.success ? (resMay.data || []) : [];
                const dsSuKien = resSK.success ? (resSK.data || []) : [];

                // Cáº­p nháº­t sá»‘ liá»‡u trÃªn Hero Banner
                document.getElementById('hero-stat-sp').innerText = `${dsSanPham.length}+`;
                document.getElementById('hero-stat-may').innerText = `${dsThueMay.length} mÃ¡y`;
                document.getElementById('hero-stat-sk').innerText = `${dsSuKien.length} lá»‹ch`;

                renderSanPhamNoiBat(dsSanPham.slice(0, 4));
                renderThueMayNoiBat(dsThueMay.slice(0, 3));
                renderSuKienNoiBat(dsSuKien.slice(0, 3));
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('Lá»—i táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u trang chá»§:', err);
            }
        }

        function renderSanPhamNoiBat(ds) {
            const container = document.getElementById('home-san-pham-list');
            if (!ds || ds.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-4 text-center py-10 text-xs text-gray-400 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">ChÆ°a cÃ³ sáº£n pháº©m nÃ o trong kho.</div>`;
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = ds.map(sp => {
                const dm = sp.TenDanhMucChuan || sp.DanhMuc || 'Váº­t tÆ°';
                const gia = Number(sp.GiaTien || 0).toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' Ä‘';
                const dvt = sp.DonViTinh ? ` / ${sp.DonViTinh}` : '';
                const tenSafe = String(sp.TenSanPham || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");

                const imgHtml = (sp.HinhAnh && sp.HinhAnh.trim() !== '' && sp.HinhAnh !== 'default.jpg')
                    ? `<div class="h-44 overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center">
                           <img src="/hinh-anh/${sp.HinhAnh}" alt="${sp.TenSanPham}" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-300" onerror="this.parentElement.innerHTML='<div class=\\'bg-emerald-50/70 h-full w-full flex items-center justify-center\\'><i class=\\'fa-solid fa-seedling text-5xl text-emerald-600\\'></i></div>'">
                       </div>`
                    : `<div class="h-44 bg-emerald-50/70 flex items-center justify-center">
                           <i class="fa-solid fa-seedling text-5xl text-emerald-600"></i>
                       </div>`;

                return `
                    <div onclick="window.location.href='/chi-tiet.html?loai=san-pham&id=${sp.MaSanPham}'" class="cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between group">

                                    <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); themNhanhVaoGio(${sp.MaSanPham}, '${tenSafe}')" class="bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-luaGreen font-bold py-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition">
                                        + ThÃªm giá»
                                    </button>
                                    <a href="/chi-tiet.html?loai=san-pham&id=${sp.MaSanPham}" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="bg-luaGreen hover:bg-emerald-900 text-white font-bold py-2 rounded-xl text-xs text-center transition">

        async function themNhanhVaoGio(maSanPham, tenSanPham) {
            const rawUser = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (!rawUser) {
                await hienThongBao('Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ´ng dÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c khi thÃªm sáº£n pháº©m vÃ o giá»!');
                window.location.href = '/dang-nhap.html';
                return;
            }
            const user = JSON.parse(rawUser);
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/gio-hang/san-pham', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        soDienThoai: user.phone,
                        maSanPham,
                        soLuong: 1
                    })
                });
                const result = await res.json();
                if (result.success) {
                    alert(`âœ… ÄÃ£ thÃªm "${tenSanPham}" vÃ o giá» hÃ ng!`);
                    if (typeof window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang === 'function') {
                        window.capNhatSoLuongGioHang();
                    }
                } else {
                    alert('âŒ ' + (result.message || 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ thÃªm vÃ o giá» hÃ ng'));
                }
            } catch (err) {
                alert('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§!');
            }
        }

                    <input type="text" id="chat-input" placeholder="Nháº­p cÃ¢u há»i vá» ká»¹ thuáº­t trá»“ng lÃºa, sÃ¢u bá»‡nh hoáº·c dá»‹ch vá»¥ thuÃª mÃ¡y..." class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-luaGreen text-sm" onkeypress="if(event.key === 'Enter') guiTinNhanNongDan()">
