    <script>
        let dangChoAI = false;

        function layTaiKhoanTab() {
            const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser') || localStorage.getItem('luaVangUser');
            if (raw && !sessionStorage.getItem('luaVangUser')) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('luaVangUser', raw);
            }
            return JSON.parse(raw || '{}');
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            fetch('/header.html')
                .then(response => response.text())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('site-header').innerHTML = data;
                    const scripts = document.getElementById('site-header').getElementsByTagName('script');
                    for (let script of scripts) { eval(script.innerText); }
                });

            taiLichSuChat();
            setInterval(taiLichSuChat, 3000);
        });

        function guiCauHoiGoiY(cauHoi) {
            if (dangChoAI) return;
            document.getElementById('chat-input').value = cauHoi;
            guiTinNhanNongDan();
        }

        async function taiLichSuChat() {
            try {
                let res = await fetch('/api/tin-nhan');
                let result = await res.json();
                if(result.success) {
                    const container = document.getElementById('chat-messages-container');
                    const loggedUser = layTaiKhoanTab();
                    const myPhone = loggedUser.phone || 'khach';

                    const messages = result.data.filter(m => m.SoDienThoai === myPhone);
                    
                    let html = '';
                    if(messages.length === 0) {
                        html = `
                            <div class="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-luaGreen text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-robot"></i>
                                </div>
                                <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 max-w-[75%] text-gray-700">
                                    <p class="font-bold text-luaGreen text-xs mb-1">Trá»£ LÃ½ AI LÃºa VÃ ng</p>
                                    <p class="text-sm">ChÃ o bÃ  con! Em lÃ  Trá»£ lÃ½ AI cá»§a LÃºa VÃ ng. BÃ  con cÃ³ thá»ƒ báº¥m vÃ o cÃ¡c <b>cÃ¢u há»i gá»£i Ã½ phÃ­a dÆ°á»›i</b> hoáº·c nháº¯n trá»±c tiáº¿p tÃ¬nh tráº¡ng cÃ¢y lÃºa Ä‘á»ƒ em tÆ° váº¥n ngay nhÃ©!</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        `;
                    } else {
                        messages.forEach(msg => {
                            let thoiGian = new Date(msg.NgayGui).toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
                            let noiDungFormatted = msg.NoiDung.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');

                            if(msg.NguoiGui === 'admin') {
                                let isAI = msg.NoiDung.includes('[Trá»£ lÃ½ AI') || msg.NoiDung.includes('[Há»‡ thá»‘ng]');
                                html += `
                                    <div class="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                        <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full ${isAI ? 'bg-amber-600' : 'bg-luaGreen'} text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm">
                                            <i class="fa-solid ${isAI ? 'fa-robot' : 'fa-user-shield'}"></i>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 max-w-[75%] text-gray-700">
                                            <p class="font-bold ${isAI ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-luaGreen'} text-xs mb-1">
                                                ${isAI ? 'Trá»£ LÃ½ AI LÃºa VÃ ng' : 'Admin LÃºa VÃ ng'}
                                            </p>
                                            <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed">${noiDungFormatted}</p>
                                            <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1.5 block">${thoiGian}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                            } else {
                                html += `
                                    <div class="flex items-start justify-end space-x-3">
                                        <div class="bg-emerald-100 p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm max-w-[75%] text-gray-800">
                                            <p class="font-bold text-luaGreen text-xs mb-1 text-right">Báº¡n (NÃ´ng dÃ¢n)</p>
                                            <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed">${noiDungFormatted}</p>
                                            <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-1.5 block text-right">${thoiGian}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-emerald-200 text-luaGreen flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0 font-bold">
                                            ${(msg.HoTen || 'ND').substring(0,2).toUpperCase()}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    if (dangChoAI) {
                        html += `
                            <div class="flex items-start space-x-3 animate-pulse">
                                <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-amber-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs flex-shrink-0">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-robot"></i>
                                </div>
                                <div class="bg-white px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 text-gray-500 text-xs italic">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin mr-1 text-amber-600"></i> Trá»£ lÃ½ AI Ä‘ang phÃ¢n tÃ­ch cÃ¢u há»i cá»§a bÃ  con...
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        `;
                    }

                    container.innerHTML = html;
                    container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
                }
            } catch(err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i táº£i chat:", err);
            }
        }

        async function guiTinNhanNongDan() {
            if (dangChoAI) return;
            const input = document.getElementById('chat-input');
            const btnSend = document.getElementById('btn-send-chat');
            const noiDung = input.value.trim();
            if(!noiDung) return;

            const loggedUser = layTaiKhoanTab();
            const hoTen = loggedUser.name || 'NÃ´ng dÃ¢n khÃ¡ch';
            const soDienThoai = loggedUser.phone || 'khach';

            dangChoAI = true;
            input.value = '';
            input.disabled = true;
            btnSend.disabled = true;
            btnSend.classList.add('opacity-50');
            taiLichSuChat();

            try {
                let res = await fetch('/api/tin-nhan', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ nguoiGui: 'nong-dan', hoTen, soDienThoai, noiDung })
                });
                let result = await res.json();
                if(result.success) {
                    dangChoAI = false;
                    taiLichSuChat();
                }
            } catch(err) {
                console.error("Lá»—i gá»­i tin nháº¯n:", err);
                dangChoAI = false;
            } finally {
                input.disabled = false;
                btnSend.disabled = false;
                btnSend.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                input.focus();
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

async function startServer() {
    try {
        await ketNoiDb();
        console.log('âœ… Káº¿t ná»‘i SQL Server (LuaVangDB) thÃ nh cÃ´ng!');

        await donDepDonQuaHan10Phut();
        setInterval(() => {
            donDepDonQuaHan10Phut();
        }, 15000);
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('âŒ Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i SQL Server:', err.message);
    }

    app.listen(PORT, () => {
        console.log(`ðŸš€ LÃºa VÃ ng API Ä‘ang cháº¡y táº¡i http://localhost:${PORT}`);
        console.log(`ðŸ”— Webhook SePay sáºµn sÃ ng táº¡i: ${sepayConfig.WEBHOOK_URL}`);
    });
}

startServer();
